III. Ikibonezamvugo

		
1. Erekana uturemajambo twungirije inshinga igira? Ese inshinga itondaguye
igira uturemajambo tungahe? Andika amazina yatwo.
		
2. Hari ubwoko bungahe bw’ingereka mu nshinga itondaguye?
		3. Ingirwanshinga ni iki? Garagaza uturango twayo?
		4. Ukoresheje imbonerahamwe, sesengura inshinga itondandaguye
n’ingirwanshinga ugaragaza amazina y’uturemajambo n’amategeko
y’igenamajwi yakoreshejwe.
a) Kamanzi ati: “Mu bucuruzi bwange, nkoresha abantu benshi”.
b) Ese Petero arakishonjesha iyo bamubwiye kujya mu mirimo isaba
ingufu?
c) Za ngabo zaracumbukuye, ubu zigeze hakurya ya Nyabarongo.
d) Kera Abanyarwanda bamesheshaga imigwegwe. Ese ubu baracyakora
batyo?

IV. Ihangamwandiko

Hanga umwandiko ntekerezo kuri iyi nsanganyamatsiko:
Kugira ngo u Rwanda rutere imbere rukwiye guteza imbere ubwikorezi ariko
rutirengagije ibidukikije.

148

8

Ubukoroni

Umwandiko: Rugigana n’ingoma ye

Nyuma y’igihe kirekire avuye mu buroko Ruhakana yakundaga kuganiriza abaturanyi
ibijyanye n’igihe cya gikoroni akabumvisha akababaro batewe n’abakoroni. Igihe
kimwe araterura maze atangira kubaganiriza ababwita ati: “Bavandimwe namwe
nshuti, mwese uko muteraniye hano kwa Ruhakana Gamariyeri, nagira ngo
dusubire mu y’inzuki wenda ubuki ntibuzaribwe. Bene Kazungu mujya mwumva,
baradukoronije, baratuyobya, baduca ku muco wacu turabayoboka da! Ngo nta
mutware kabeba. Umukoroni yaragufataga ati: “Ryama uzirikwe bagukubite
ibiboko umunani”. Waba umwana, waba umuntu mukuru, uruhinja rw’umukoroni
rwashoboraga kugukoresha icyo rushatse kandi ururusha amaboko. Mwibuka uyu
mukecuru Nyirashiku washikuranye n’imishike ku bwa ba gashakabuhake akaba

149

agejeje aya magingo? Rubanda bakomeza umutsi! Abagabo, abagore, abasore
barakubiswe barongera barakubitwa bazira ubusa, uretse agasuzuguro Kazungu
yatugaragarizaga nta kindi. Kazungu yazanye ihohotera rikoresha ikiboko ariko ubu
ryarandukanye n’imizi yaryo mu Rwanda.

Turikubwigenge wowe uvutse ejo, twe muge muduhumuriza mudusabire
twarababaye bihagije. Rumashana uyu mureba wuzuye iminkanyari mu gahanga,
umusatsi wose ukaba ari uruyenzi yarwanye intambara y’Abamayimayi muri
Tanzaniya n’iya Abamawumawu muri Kenya aharanira ko abakoroni badakomeza
kudutsikamira no kudupyinagaza. Muri Tanzaniya na Kenya iyi mitwe yari ikaze.
Abakoroni bakijijwe n’Iyakare bari bahazahariye pe! Abantu bapfuye ari benshi
cyane. Rumasha, si uko byagenze? Mwarasahuraga, mukibasira abakozi b’abakoroni,
ikindi kandi, ahari ngo mwumvaga mutateshuka ku muco wacu gakondo mwamagana
idini rya gikirisitu! Nge narumiwe ariko ndanabishyigikiye pe! Ese Rashe, harya
sogokuru wawe ntiyaguye mu ntambara yari yashojwe na Runyota Kanyarufunzo
wo mu Burundi wari wivumbagatanyije ku Babirigi?

Bamara akanya bacecetse, umwe mu bari aho aratangara ati: “Ego mwana wa
Musinga, ibyo umbaza ubizi neza wowe wahakanwe na we kwa Yuhi V Musinga.
Amateka atubwira ko Musinga atigeze yumva abakoroni b’Ababirigi, yarabasuzuguye
arabarwanya, ngo nyuma baje kumunyaga ubutegetsi n’abatware bamucira i
Kamembe. Abo Banyaburayi babonye Abanyarwanda bagikunze umwami wabo,
bamucira ishyanga ahitwa Moba muri Katanga ho muri Repubulika Iharanira
Demukarasi ya Kongo ari na ho yatangiye. Mwibuke neza, muri icyo gihe ni bwo
Rukara rwa Bishingwe abazungu bamunyongeye mu Gahunga k’Abarashi.”

Ruhakana aramwunganira ati: “Abakoroni badukozeho, bari bafite ubutegetsi
bukandamiza. Abanyarwanda kimwe n’abandi Banyafurika babyumva neza bakanga
gutanga imisoro, gukora uburetwa, kwikorera imizigo, gutanga amakoro, gukubitwa,
gutukwa n’ibindi, byatumaga abantu banga bidasubirwaho abo ba Rugigana. Nge
narabizize murabizi imyaka nakoze uburoko. Naho uwo Kanyarufunzo na we ngo
iby’abazungu ntiyabikozwaga, yatangaga amategeko yo kwigomeka kuri Kazungu
yibereye mu rufunzo. Ba Mwarimu Nyerere muri Tanzaniya, ba Jomo Kenyata muri
Kenya, ugira ngo hari uko batari bagize? Abongereza byari byabagoye kwinjira
Afurika y’uburasirazuba, Abanyakenya n’Abanyatanzaniya bari bashaririye. Iyo
umusaza Kirongo asobanura iby’intambara barwanye kandi bakazitsinda ntiwabifata
nk’ikinamico. Abaturage bafataga umugambi wo kunyaga Abongereza ubutaka bwiza
bigaruriye bugacya byashyizwe mu bikorwa kubera gushyira hamwe kw’abenegihugu.
Yewe, burya ntawugera ku ntego yiyemeje atiyushye akuya! Mu Rwanda na ho
ntibyaboroheye umwami ntiyigeze abareba neza na busa.”
Turikubwigenge Natanayeri we, yavutse ibibazo byarakemutse, amatara yaka. Iyo
abavandimwe be basubira mu bya ba sogokuru, ukuntu bangaga umuco wazanye
n’ubukoroni, usanga yumiwe afitiye impuhwe abasaza barwanyije abazungu ndetse

150

bamwe bagahara amagara yabo ku nyungu zacu abakiriho. Iyo hinikijwe ikiganiro
gikomoza ku bakoroni, abakiri bato bakeka ko tubatobeye. Muge mwihangana
tuge tuganira n’iby’abakuru. Umurage tugomba gusigira mwe mudukomokaho
ni ukubumvisha ukuntu ubwigenge bwaharaniwe bukagerwaho benshi bamaze
gutakaza ubuzima bwabo; imbuto z’amahoro ubu turimo gusoroma ni iz’abadutanze
kubona izuba.”

Turikubwigenge, umusore wari aho yumva ibyo bavuga maze na we ababaza ikibazo
agira ati: “Nge njya numva muvuga amagi y’umuzungu, inka z’umugogoro, inka ya
Rugaravu, amata ya reteri,… ibyo byo ni ibiki, ko twe mu mabyiruka yacu tubyumva
nk’amateka? Ababayeho igihe k’ingoma ya gikoroni muri abo kubabarirwa, ariko
na none muge mudusangiza ayo mateka. Mu nama yabereye i Berilini mu Budage
ku wa 15 Ugushyingo 1884 ikarangira ku wa 26 Gashyantare 1885, ngo uwitwa Oto
Voni Bisimariki (Otto von Bismarck) wari uyoboye iyo nama, hamwe na bagenzi be,
bigabanyije Afurika bayigira agatanyu. Badukoreyeho ubufindo muri Gashyantare
1910 ku itariki ya 8, aho Ababirigi, Abadage n’Abongereza bahuriye i Buruseri mu
Buburigi ngo bagamije kwiga ibyerekeye inkiko z’imigabane yabo muri aka karere ka
Afurika. Badutandukanya n’u Burundi, badutandukanya na Kongo Mbirigi, dusigara
dutyo mu majune. Bageze ku Rwanda, umwami Musinga ati: ‘Ntimurenge biriya bisozi
byo mu majyaruguru, mu burengerazuba ntimwirushye murenga kariya gasozi kicaye
muri ariya mazi magari, mu magepfo ntimurenge Akanyaru, nimugera ku Rusumo
ntimwambuke Akagera’. Si uko ko se u Rwanda rwasigaye ari agashumi! Imipaka
yarahindaguwe ibintu birayoba.”
Umwe mu bagabo b’ibikwerere wari aho aramusubiza ati: “Rumashana se ko nshimye
ko twavutse agahato gashize, umbajije iby’amata, inka za Rugaravu n’ibindi waba
unduhije cyane, gusa abakoroni baratuzengereje bitavugwa, ariko se Turikubwige,
abo bakoroni bumvaga amaherezo bizagenda bite, bibwiraga ko tutari abantu ko
tutazigera tubigaranzura?”

Ruhakana arakomeza ati: “Mukure ariko mube abana. Kazungu ntiyagombaga
kutwumva. Kazungu akiva mu nda ya nyina yari afite ingengabitekerezo
y’ivanguraruhu; yumvaga tudatekereza, ko nta muco twigirira na mba, ko nta dini,
ko Abanyafurika batagira icyo bemera, ko batagira ibitekerezo by’indengakamere,
mbese ko Abanyafurika batazi amavu n’amajyo y’ubuzima bwabo. None se si
yo mpamvu baharaniraga kuza muri Afurika ngo badutoze umuco mvaburaya,
bamamaze ukwemera kwabo, abe ari ko kugenga imico n’imyifatire y’Abanyarwanda
n’Abanyafurika muri rusange. Ngewe Ruhakana, ngisimbuka akatsi sinigeze numva
impamvu abazungu baduca ku madini gakondo, imyenda twari tuzi kwihangira, mu
bucuzi twari tugeze kure, uruzi urushingo n’urushingati ku Munyarwanda usheshe
akajumba ashaka kukiyokereza! Ese iyo abo bene Kazungu batadukoma mu nkokora
tuba tutarivumburiye byinshi? Ngo aha batuzaniye ingirwamabiriti y’imyambi! Yego
byari gutinda, ariko kera kabaye tukagira ibyo dushyira ahagaragara bivuye mu
bwenge bwacu. Ingoma ya mpatsibihugu yaratudindije bikabije.

151

Rugigana n’ingoma ye! Ababonye bakira iyi ngoma bakize inkiko, bagomba kubyinira
ku rukoma bakabyirata, ariko bakibuka ko ingoma y’Abakoroni yadusigiye ibikomere.
Abantu bagiye bacuruzwa bucakara mu masoko, ababyeyi bamwe na bamwe
bakagurisha abana babo, abafatwaga mu minyago, abana b’abakobwa, abacaracaraga
ijoro riguye kimwe n’abatagira kivurira, abo bose baracuruzwaga. Ahantu hazwi
hagurishirizwaga abantu hari ku Kivumu cya Mpushi, Rukira mu Burasirazuba, mu
Rwanza rwa Save muri Huye na Mubuga ho mu Bukonya mu cyahoze ari Ruhengeri.
Abasore b’ingufu, abagabo b’ibikwerere n’abagore b’inkumi n’amajigija batwarwaga
n’Abakoroni, bamwe bagashorwa mu ntambara nk’iya mbere n’iya kabiri y’isi yose.
Ubu se imiryango y’Abanyafurika yajyanwe bunyago ku mugabane wa Amerika cyane
cyane, tuzongera guhurira hehe? Ubukoroni bwaduteye icyuho cy’abantu ku buryo
budasubirwaho.”
Rumashana na we rimwanga mu nda ati: “Ngewe Rumashana narwanye iy’Abongereza
nubwo bari bafite ishyaka ryo kurwanya abacuruzaga abantu. Twarwanye inkundura
karahava! Hari inyandiko z’abahanga zari zitangiye kuboneka zamagana ubwo
bucuruzi bw’abantu. Nko muri Amerika ntibari bagikeneye amaboko y’abahinga
ibisheke kimwe n’abacukura amabuye y’agaciro. Ibi ni byo koko kuko mu Burayi
ho bari bamaze kuvumbura imashini zishobora guhinga ahantu hanini kandi mu
gihe gito. Icuruzwa ry’abirabura ryagize inkurikizi harimo igabanuka ry’abaturage.
Afurika yose yatakaje abantu iryaguye kandi abenshi bari mu kigero k’imyaka
icumi na mirongo itatu, ni ukuvuga abana, abasore, inkumi, abagabo n’abagore
bose bari bafite imbaraga. Murumva ingufu batunyaze? Abakiri bato kugeza kuri
iyi saha nge ndabasabira. Twe tumaze gushira amenyo mu kanwa, tumaze kubona
igufwa ry’umuyaga, ibyo bigarutse ntibatureba! Ese ugira ngo ubwo bucuruzi
ntibwahungabanyije umutekano? Icyo tuzi ni uko itunda ry’abirabura ryakuruye
iterabwoba kenshi kandi henshi; hakoreshejwe imbunda kugira ngo bashobore
gufata abirabura benshi. Uzi ko iri curuza ryatumye abariho kiriya gihe basubiranamo
ubwabo. Ibihugu n’imiryango myinshi yari ifite ubutegetsi bukomeye yagiye isenyuka
abantu baratatana. Abacakara bajya kwikiriza Abanyamerika mu buhinzi no mu
nganda. Abirabura bakoreraga ubusa ba shebuja bakironkera inyungu nyinshi.
Ndabona Turikubwigenge we wagira ngo ikiganiro ntagikurikira, kandi uyu
mwuzukuru wa Rugango ari we uherutse mu ishuri. Se sha, muri ayo mashuri nta
cyo mwatoyemo, cyangwa wajyagayo ari ukurangiza umuhango?”

Turikubwigenge aramusubiza ati: “Wagira ngo muri Abanyamateka da! Muravuga
ibintu mukabiva imuzingo rwose! Cyakora ngo buriya ubukoronize bwagize nabi
bugira n’ibyo bwungura Abanyafurika. Abanyaburayi batuzaniye ibigori, inanasi,
imyumbati, ubunyobwa bivuye muri Amerika. Insina n’imyembe yo ngo yaba yaravuye
muri Aziya. Abitwa Abarabu batwigishije uburyo bwo guhinga umuceri no kubaka
amazu afite imfuruka za kizungu.”

152

Ruhakana arakomeza. “Abazungu bakoronije Afurika ku mpamvu zo kwisahurira
amabuye y’agaciro no kugurisha imyenda yakorwaga n’inganda zabo. Ibikoresho
fatizo bari kubikura hehe, hatari muri Afurika? Ikindi, ibihugu by’ i Burayi
byahataniraga gutegeka isi. Iyi mpamvu ya mpatsibihugu yaheshaga ishema
Abanyaburayi ndetse bakigwizaho amaboko. Ubutegetsi bwa kiboko ntawuzamura
akarimi, bwapfunyengeje, bupyinagaza abirabura benshi. Ntawagiraga isambu
yigengaho, abategetsi bakuru bari ba Rutuku. Reka twemere ko ingabo z’Abakoroni
zahoshaga intambara z’urudaca zahoraga muri Afurika dore ko bari bafite imitwe
y’ingabo hirya no hino; mu irwana ryabo ariko bagakoresha imbunda!”
Undi musaza wari aho yunga mu ryo avuze ati: “Gamariyeri nkubwire, Abakoroni
baduhenze ubwenge baraturwanya turabayoboka ariko hari agakeregeshwa
k’amajyambere n’iterambere bapfuye kutugezaho. Iyo urebye ukuntu batwigishije
guhinga ibireti mu Rwanda, ipamba mu Misiri, kakawo (cacao) muri Kotedivuwari
(Côte d’ivoire) n’imikindo y’amamesa muri Kongo Mbirigi, usanga ari agashya
twavangaga n’amarira. Abazungu, nk’uko abahanga mu by’amateka babitubwira,
abazungu barwanyije inzara, barwanya indwara zatwibasiraga harimo marariya,
ubushita, indwara y’ibitotsi, ibibembe n’izindi. Ahaaa! Bubatse n’amashuri ajijura
Abanyafurika, ikibazo ni uko wasangaga ayo mashuri yaratozaga Abanyafurika imico
y’i Burayi, agatesha agaciro imico karande yo muri Afurika. Ururimi rwacu gakondo
ntirwitabweho, mu mashuri bakigisha cyanecyane mu ndimi z’i Burayi ku buryo ubu
ngubu usanga ari zo zihuza Abanyafurika.

Mwene Rumashana aratangara ati: “Ego Rumashana wampaye inyana y’igaju! Njya
mbwirwa ko ngo hari n’imigi yashinzwe n’Abakoroni, izo za Kongo Burazavile (Congo
Brazaville) zashinzwe na Savorunya do Buraza (Savorgnan De Brazza), Kigali yo mu
Rwanda ishingwa na Rishari Kanti (Richard Kandt) Abanyarwanda bitaga Kanayoge.
Abanyarwanda bamenye no kubaka ku buryo bwa kijyambere babikesha abakoroni
da! Ugeze Dodoma, i Mwanza muri Tanzaniya, ukagera iyo za Kinshasa, za Bujumbura,
za Kampala, wahasanga ibirango by’abakoroni harimo inyubako zitava ku gihe. Si
aho gusa uzagere n’ahandi Abakoroni banyuze wihere ijisho! Ngo ubusa buruta
buriburi mwana wa mama!

Ruhakana asaba abari aho gutuza maze asa n’utanga umwanzuro agira ati: “Mureke
ducutse amateka dufata akaruhuko, ahasigaye tumenye ko ubukoroni bwagize
ingaruka zitari nke ku Banyafurika ndetse n’u Rwanda muri rusange. Umuntu
akubwiye ati: ‘Wapfa umukoroni agukanga, wahita wibuka ikiboko, shiku, kuba umuja
ukorera ubusa, ndetse waba ufite umubyeyi wabigiriyemo amakuba ukababara,
ushoboye guhunga akiruka kibuno mpamaguru! Imiryango yatorongereye ishyanga,
iyo za Burayi twazayitegereza na ryari, bagiye mpiru na nyoni, kabindi na buki!’
Uzumve na bamwe bagiye muri Amerika ku bw’abacakara ko bazigera bifuza kuba
muri Afurika kandi inkomoko yabo ari muri Afurika. Gusa ariko nubwo twe nta
matunda y’ubukoroni tubona neza, abana n’abuzukuru bacu, ubuvivi n’ubuvivure
baziga bamenye imibare, ubugenge n’ubutabire, bivuze mu mavuriro akomeye
kandi agwiriyemo abahanga; kudidibuza Icyongereza, Igifaransa, Igiswayiri n’izindi

153

batuvuganira babibemo intyoza, niducyura ibyo, Imana y’i Rwanda izakomeze
itwihere impagarike n’ubugingo. Ibwotamasimbi ntibazongere kutwibazaho cyane.
Iki gitekerezo nizere ko tugihuriyeho turi benshi. Abakoroni bakoze nabi, twe
ntitwabona uko twiyishyurira kandi ikituraje ishinga si icyo, ahubwo dushikame
dukore tuzibe icyuho twatejwe n’Abakoroni. Murakoze kandi murakarama!”

Nyuma yo gucutsa ikiganiro, Ruhakana yibuka uko umuhungu we wari warayobotse
idini ry’abera yamubwiye ubwo yamubazaga impamvu ahora ku misiyoni ati
“Ntuzongere kwitwa data”. Nubwo ngo amarira y’umugabo atemba ajya mu nda
Ruhakana ntibyamukundiye amarira atemba ku matama yombi kubera ubwo buryo
abera babatandukanije n’abo bibyariye. Agenda atekereza uburyo azongera kwiyunga
n’umuhungu we kugira ngo arebe ko yaba yarahindutse. […]
Bifatiye ku nkuru Le Fils de Mikeno ya A. Ruti yerekana amabi ubukoroni bwagiriye
Abanyarwanda.

I. Inyunguramagambo

A. Sobanura amagambo akurikira:
1. Gukomeza umutsi
2. Guhara amagara
3. Amagambo azaba ashize ivuga
4. Amagi y’umuzungu
5. Inka z’umugogoro
6. Inka ya Rugaravu
7. Amajune
8. Ngisimbuka akatsi
9. Urushingo
		

10. Urushingati
11. Batadukoma mu nkokora
12. Kubyinira ku rukoma
13. Mukabiva imuzingo
14. Ubutegetsi bwa kiboko
15. Kugenda mpiru na nyoni
16. Kugenda nka kabindi na buki
17. Ubuvivi n’ubuvivure

B. Kora iyi myitozo ku nyunguramagambo:
Huza neza amagambo ari mu ruhushya A n’ibisobanuro byayo byatanzwe mu
ruhushya B.
Uruhushya A

Uruhushya B

1. Shiku

a) Amata yagemurwaga ku ngufu ngo atunge
abakozi b’abakoroni babaga mu migi.

154

2. Ikiboko
3. Amata ya Leteri

4. Ubutegetsi bwa mpatsibihugu
5. Abamawumawu

6. Ubutegetsi bwa kiboko

7. Abamayimayi

b) Inkoni ikozwe mu ruhu rw’imvubu akaba
ariyo abakoroni bakoreshaga bakubita
Abanyarwanda.
c) Ubutegetsi bwigarurira ibindi bihugu,
bukanyunyuza kandi bugakandamiza
abaturage bafashwe nk’ingaruzwamuheto.
d) Abaturage bo mu magepfo ya Tanzaniya
bivumbagatanyaga ku Budage, bagasahura
amazu y’ubutegetsi, bakica abakozi
b’Abadage n’abanyamadini. Bibwiraga ko
amazi biteraga yatuma amasasu nta cyo
yabatwara.

e) Ubuyobozi bukoresha igitugu, bupyinagaza
abene gihugu.
f) Abaturage bo muri Kenya bo mu bwoko
bw’Abakikuyu bivumbagatanyaga
barwanya abazungu babatwarariraga
ubutaka. Barwanyaga kandi ubukirisitu,
bakomeye ku mihango gakondo no ku
muco wabo.

g) Imirimo yo guhingira hamwe ibyate
by’imyumbati n’ibijumba buri wese
afite icyate ke atakirangiza agakubitwa
ibiboko umunani.

Simbuza ijambo ryanditse ritsindagiye irindi bivuga kimwe usanga mu
mwandiko.
1. Rutuku yaratuzengereje duta umutwe kugeza ku bwigenge, ubu turahumeka
umwuka w’abazima.
2. Jya wiga ushishikaye, ntukamere nka za nkorabusa zijya ku ishuri zigiye guta
igihe.
3. Ku mugabane w’i Burayi haturutseyo abakoroni b’ingeri zose.
4. Bantu mukinisha imbunda z’amasasu muge mwitonda mutazatumaraho
urubyaro.
5. Abantu b’inararibonye, bazi byinshi ku mateka y’abakoroni bigabagabanyije
Afurika.
6. Kugera ku bwigenge byaraharaniwe ku buryo budasubirwaho.
7. Ikinyarwanda ntikigeze kitabwaho mu mashuri, ku ngoma ya gashakabuhake
w’umukoroni.

155

II. Ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko

Subiza ibi bibazo byo kumva umwandiko:
1. Abakinankuru bavugwa mu mwandiko ni bande?
2. Uruhinja rw’umukoroni rwari rukomeye. Tanga interuro iri mu mwandiko
ibihamya.
3. Mu mwandiko baratubwiramo umusaza ukuze cyane. Uwo ni nde, umubwirwa
n’iki?
4. Abakoroni bafite ibintu bibiri by’ingenzi bitwaje bakoroniza Afurika. Ibyo bintu
ni ibihe?
5. Ingaruka mbi kurusha izindi za gikoronize yashegeshe igihugu cyacu cy’u Rwanda
ni iyihe?
6. Vuga imitwe ibiri y’abarwanyi yari yarazengereje abakoroni mu bihugu
by’iburasirazuba bw’u Rwanda.
7. Inama yo kwigabanya Afurika yateranye kandi irangira ryari? Hehe? Yari iyobowe
na nde?
8. Ubwo u Rwanda rwatandukanywaga n’u Burundi na Kongo rwatakaje ahantu
hanini. Havuge.
9. Tanga amazina y’imigi yashinzwe n’abakoroni n’abo yitiriwe.

III. Gusesengura umwandiko

Tekereza ku buryo bwimbitse maze usubize ibibazo bikurikira:
1. Ni iyihe nsanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu nkuru?
2. Tanga ingingo z’ingenzi zibanzweho mu nkuru wasomye.
3. Abakoroni badusigiye ibikomere n’agashashi katumurikira kujya mbere.
Sobanura byimbitse.
4. Ingamba ababyiruka bagomba gufata kugira ngo hakomeze gusigasira
ibyagezweho nyuma yo kwiyuha akuya ni izihe?

IV. Kungurana ibitekerezo

Ungurana ibitekerezo na bagenzi bawe kuri iki kibazo:
Nimugereranye ibivugwa mu nkuru n’ubuzima murimo muri iki gihe.

156

Inkuru ndende

Soma imwe muri izi nkuru zikurikira maze ukore ubushakashatsi utahure
ubwoko bwayo, uturango twayo unakore ubundi bushakashatsi bwimbitse
kuri iyo ngeri y’ubuvanganzo, unayisesengure ukurikije ibyavuye mu
bushakashatsi wakoze.

- Byuma, F., Mureranyana, 1981
- Niyonteze, P., Imari ya shuni, 1981-1982
- Uwamungu, J., Nyirabayazana, 1981
- Nkurikiyumukiza, F., Yatashye atagomba, 1987
- Niyitegeka, M.Y., Giramata, 1988
- ............................, Urwibutso, 1988
- ............................, Wishavura, 1989
- ............................, Iyo mbimenya, 1991
- Rugema, A., Rwemerikije, 1988
- Furere, R.M., Mariya Kantarama, 1998
- Karenzi, F., Ishavu ry’abato, 2000
- …
a) Inshoza y’inkuru ndende
Inkuru ndende nk’uko iryo zina ribivuga ni inkuru iba ari ndende, ibarwa
n’umubarankuru avuga uko yagenze. Bamwe mu basesenguzi b’inkuru ndende
bayivuga berekana ko igomba kuvuga ibyabayeho ndetse umwanditsi akavuga
ubuzima bwe; ibyamubayeho. Abandi bati: “Igomba kuba ari inkuru y’impimbano
n’ubwo ibyo ivuga byashobora kubaho.” Igihurirwaho na benshi ni uko inkuru
ndende igomba kuba ifite inkuru ibara, uruhererekane rw’ibikorwa, ikaba
yanditse mu nyandiko isanzwe; atari mu mikarago nk’ibisigo kandi yifitemo
ubwiza n’ubuhanga bw’imikoreshereze y’ururimi. Ibi, babishimangira bagira
bati: “Inkuru ndende ni uruhererekane rw’ibikorwa mpimbano bishobora
kubaho cyangwa byabayeho, ikaba ifite imisusire ya gihanga kandi nyabugeni
igaragaza ko umwanditsi ari intyoza mu kubara inkuru, mu kuyiha imiterere
myiza y’ibikorwa no kubikurikiranya.”
b) Uturango tw’inkuru ndende
Inkuru ndende irangwa n’imiterere yayo; ibarankuru, abakinankuru (abantu
cyangwa inyamaswa), kuba hari ikivugwa, kuba hari ahantu n’igihe kibera, kuba

157

ari ndende (akenshi hagati y’impapuro 100 na 250) kuba hari uburyo ibikorwa
bikurikirana kandi bigenda bitera amatsiko usoma, ku buryo atarambirwa
gusoma inkuru ibarwa.

c) Amateka y’inkuru ndende mu Rwanda.
Hari abavuga ko inkuru ndende mu Rwanda yaba yaratangiye mu mwaka wa
1938 kuko ari bwo Musenyeri Alexis Kagame yatangiye gusohora inyandiko ze
«Inkuru ya Matabaro» mu kanyamateka k’abana Hobe. Abashakashatsi bemeza
ariko ko inkuru ndende nyayo yasohotse mu wa 1950 yanditse mu rurimi
rw’Igifaransa na Saveriyo Nayigiziki yise «Escapade rwandaise» ifite ibice bibiri
byaje guhurizwa mu nkuru imwe ikitwa «Mes trances à trente ans». Hashize
imyaka ibiri ni bwo inkuru ndende yanditse mu Kinyarwanda yasohotse yitwa
«Ntabajyana» ya Simoni Munyakazi. Uyu mugabo yahawe igihembo cya kabiri
mu irushannwa ryiswe «Amitiés Belgo-Rwandaise». Kuva ubwo haciye imyaka
igera kuri 20 kugira ngo haboneke izindi nkuru ndende mu irushanwa ryabaye
mu wa 1971.

d) Amoko y’inkuru ndende nyarwanda
Iyo bagena ingeri z’inkuru ndende bazishyira mu matsinda, hari uburyo bwinshi
bukurikizwa. Ibihurirwaho na benshi bagena amoko y’inkuru ndende ni ibi
bikurikira : aho yandikiwe, igihe yandikiwe cyangwa se ikivugwamo, ibarankuru
ryayo (imiterere y’ibikorwa, abakinnyi) n’ibindi. Izi zikurikira ni zimwe mu ngeri
z’inkuru ndende nk’uko Nkejabahizi Jean Chrisostome azigena mu gitabo ke
“Ubuvanganzo nyarwanda. Inkuru ndende n’Ikinamico, Butare UNR, 2005”

1. Inkuru ndende z’inkundo
Zikunda kuvuga cyane ku nkundo hagati y’abasore n’inkumi, ingorane zishobora
kubatandukanya cyangwa se kubabuza kubana, kwitsinda no kudahemukirana
mu bigeragezo, amayeri yose akoreshwa kugira ngo bahure cyangwa basubirane
mu gihe ababyeyi babo batabishaka, amaherezo bakazagera ku ntego yabo yo
kubana. Mu Kinyarwanda, aha hakunze gutangwa ingero za Ntabajyana ya Simoni
Munyakazi na Giramata ya Niyitegeka Mukarugira Yuliyana.
2. Inkuru ndende nsuhuzamutima
Bene izi nkuru zamamaye cyane mu mpera z’ikinyejana cya cumi na gatandatu no
mu ntangiriro z’icya cumi na karindwi. Inkuru iba ishingiye ahanini ku bwihare ;
bahera ku gashashi cyangwa akanyotwe k’urukundo gasanzwe, kagashyigikirwa
n’ibikorwa bibiri by’ingenzi: ingendo n’imirwano. Urukundo ruvugwamo
ruriyubashye, ni isugi kandi ntiruhemuka. Umukundwa cyangwa uwihebewe
usanga ari agakumi. Ibizazane bahura na byo usanga ari ibishobora kubaho mu
buzima cyanecyane nk’igihe k’imidugararo. Kudahuza idini bituma ababyeyi
bivayo n’imizi n’imiganda ngo babuze umusore n’inkumi kubana. Ikemezo cya se
w’umukobwa cyo kumushyingira uwo ashatse kubera inyungu ze bwite, ubukire,
gutandukanywa n’intambara cyangwa se gufungwa. Mu Kinyarwanda urugero

158

twatanga hano ni Ntabajyana ya Simoni Munyakazi kubera ko ababyeyi ba
Karasankima batifuzaga ko arongora Zaninka, ahubwo bafite undi bamuhitiyemo
wo mu rwego rwabo, ariko umusore Karasankima akababera ibamba.

3. Inkuru ndende z’imyifatire
Izi ni inkuru zishingiye ku busongarere n’ubugome mu maraso. Imibereho ya
buri munsi n’imiterere y’isi muri icyo gihe, ntibishyigikirwa n’umuco muzima wo
kwiyubaha n’ubuhanga byaranze abanditsi bakomeye. Mu nkuru ndende nk’izi
usanga higanjemo gushimisha irari ry’umubiri, kuba ikigenge(ubwomanzi),
gutinyuka gukabije, ibiterasoni; mbese usanga isi yaracuramye ku buryo ikibi
kiganje, akaba ari cyo kiyobora isi. Muri ubu bwoko bw’inkuru, usanga imico
myiza n’ubupfura byitwa ubugwari, ubucucu n’amakosa. Muri izo nkuru
usangamo amagambo atameshe, umwanditsi akihatira gushushanya mu mvugo
ibifitanye isano n’ibitsina, aho bibera n’uburyo atanga ingingo ze byerekana ko ari
indwara imaze igihe. Mu Kinyarwanda nta rugero rwa bene izi nkuru ruraboneka
kugeza ubu.
4. Inkuru ndende za sarigoma
Inkuru nk’izi zifite amavuko yazo mu gihugu cya Espagne (Esipanye) mu kinyejana
cya cumi na karindwi. Uruhare runini ruhabwa imibereho mu by’ubukungu:
kugira icumbi, kubona ifunguro, umwambaro ni byo bihora bihangayikishije
abanyarubuga muri ubu bwoko bw’inkuru. Iyo nkuru bayitiriye uburyo ibazemo,
kuko ari inkuru y’umuntu uvuga ubuzima bwe. Byabaho bitabaho, umubarankuru
mukuru avuga ubuzima n’imibereho ye.
Imyandikire y’iyi nkuru bayihuza n’imibereho y’umwana ubaho ari
mbonabucya, nta cyo yimirije imbere, nta gashinga k’ubuzima afite. Mu buzima
bw’umubarankuru nyuma y’igihe runaka k’imibabaro n’ibibazo, hakurikiraho
igihe cyo kwiyuburura no gutwarwa agakundwa, ubukire yifuzaga bukaza mu
bitekerezo ariko atabwizera, ugasanga aritiranya inzozi n’ukuri. Inkuru ndende
nyarwanda ifite aho ihuriye na bene ubu bwoko ni iya Saveriyo Nayigiziki yitwa
«Mes trances à trente ans»

5. Inkuru ndende y’ubuzima busanzwe
Bene iyi nkuru ntiba igamije gukosora ngo wenda ibintu byarushaho kuba byiza,
abantu bagire imyifatire iboneye, babane neza, mbese ngo ubone ko umwanditsi
afite inzozi z’umunezero n’ubuhehemure. Iyi nkuru irangwa n’urusobe cyangwa
uruvangitirane rw’imyifatire, kuticara hamwe, gusetsa, gusesereza, uburara
n’ubwomanzi no kwifatira abantu. Iritegereza, igakabya mu gusetsa, igakabya
inkuru n’ubucakura, hagaragaramo ibitangaza no kwimaringa. Uzasanga iyi
nkuru ivuga ukuntu runaka yakoze kugira ngo yirwaneho, kugira ngo akomere
abone amaboko n’amafaranga ; uko yagiriye nabi abanzi be, uko yatsinzwe
cyangwa se yatsinze mu nkiko, uko yaje guteseka agashakisha uburyo yagana
imigi itandukanye; uko yaje guhinduka umugiranabi agakora n’andi makosa

159

bigatuma bamufunga; ibikorwa bibi yagizemo uruhare, kwiyoberanya, ingendo,
abo bahura n’ibindi. Inkuru y’Ikinyarwanda y’ubu bwoko ni iya FranÇois Byuma
muri Mureranyana.

6. Inkuru ndende za mutemberezi/ naragenze ndabona
Bakunze kuvugamo ubwiza bw’abantu, ibidukikije. Babivuga ari nk’umutako
w’aho abakinankuru baba bari ku buryo bishushanya uko bamerewe imbere
muri bo. Ari abantu, ari inyamaswa, usanga ari mahwi, biberanye n’aho biri bisa
n’aho ari ho byaremewe na ho hakabiremerwa. Ahantu ni ho hatuma abakinnyi
batekereza gutya na gutya, ni ho hababeshejeho ku buryo ubuzima bwabo
bwajyanaga n’uko isi ibakikije iteye. Mu Kinyarwanda urugero dutanga ni Imali
ya SHUNI ya Pascal Niyonteze.

7. Inkuru ndende barwa
Ubwoko bw’iyi nkuru bwaje kwemerwa mu kinyejana cya cumi n’umunani.
Havugwamo agahinda no kubona ko ibyo wibeshyaga mu nzozi, wibwira ko
ari ko bizamera nyamara ko bitagishobotse. Umuntu uvugwamo arashaka
ariko ntashobore. Ubushobozi iyo bumubanye buke bituma abeshya cyangwa
se akiyerekana uko bitari ubundi yiyerekanaga nk’umunyakuri, maze uwari
yararahiye kudasa na rubanda rusanzwe akagenda akajya inyuma y’abandi
mu ngeso mbi z’urukozasoni. Mu Kinyarwanda urugero ni “Iyo mbimenya” ya
Niyitegeka Mukarugira Yuliyana.
8. Inkuru za nanzubukoroni
Iyi nkuru igaragaza umujinya no kuzinukwa kw’abantu bahinduwe abacakara,
bakoronijwe n’abazungu, maze kwivumbura kwabo kukagira ingaruka kuri
bose ndetse n’umuntu ku giti ke. Izi nkuru zakunze kugaragara mu myaka ya za
mirongo itandatu, ubwo ibihugu bya Afurika byahagurukiraga rimwe bigamije
kwipakurura ubutegetsi bwa gikoroni. Izi nkuru zigaragaza akarengane,
gusuzugurwa no guteshwa agaciro Abanyafurika bagirirwaga. Urugero twatanga
mu Kinyarwanda ni Mureranyana ya FranÇois Byuma n’ubwo yo itavuga gusa
ikibazo cy’ubukoroni na “Matabaro” ya Alegisi Kagame.

9. Inkuru ndende ya subiza amerwe mu isaho
Abanyafurika bamaze kwigobotora ingoma ya gikoronize bari bazi ko bagiye
kwigenga no kumererwa neza, ibyo bari barabuze bakabibona ntibongere
gusuzugurwa no gufatwa nabi, kwicishwa imirimo n’ibindi. Bagize batya babona
ntacyahindutse. Abashyizwe ku ngoma y’ubutegetsi nta ho bigeze batandukanira
n’abakoroni, ndetse bamwe mu bategetsi b’Abanyafurika baruhije ubugome
abitwaga abakoroni. Abaturage barumiwe amerwe bayasubiza mu isaho.
Abategetsi banyunyuje abaturage, barabakandagira karahava: akarengane,
gufungirwa ubusa, kwigira mu migi rubanda igasigara iririra mu myotsi y’icyaro,
ahatagira amazi n’umuriro, nta terefoni, inzara n’ubukene binuma. Abategetsi

160

baradamarara, rubanda bicuza icyo barwaniye imyaka n’imyaka ngo barashaka
kwigenga, ku buryo hari n’abasigara bifuza ko bwa butegetsi bw’abakoroni
barwanyaga bwagaruka. Urugero rwo mu Kinyarwanda usanga rujya kwegera
ubu bwoko ni usanga ruberanye n’ubu bwoko bw’inkuru ni Mureranyana ya
FranÇois Byuma.

10. Inkuru ndende z’amateka
Ubwoko bw’izi nkuru bushingira ku bantu bagize uruhare mu mateka y’aha
n’aha, umuryango, igihugu; bakavuga uko babayeho, ibyo bakoze n’ibindi. Muri
Afurika twavuga nka Caka (Chaka), Sunjata (Soundjata), n’abandi. Bene izi
nkuru zitandukanye n’inkuru ndende nyirizina. Mu nkuru z’amateka usangamo
ibitekerezo bivanze n’amakabyankuru. Inkuru y’Ikinyarwanda ivuga ku mateka
ni Mureranyana ya FranÇois Byuma.

11. Inkuru ndende z’intimba
Zitekereza ku buzima n’imibereho, zitwereka ko kubaho ari ugushinyiriza, ko
ubuzima atari paradizo. Kubaho ni ukubabara. Muri izi nkuru usanga umuntu
akunda ntakundwe, yahinga akarumbya, yakira agahangayika. Iyo agerageje
kwipfira nabi cyangwa kwishabikira uko abishoboye bitewe n’uko isi yameze
amenyo, arinda apfa agikururana n’umuruho. Igihe umuntu akiriho yumva ko
ikiruta ari ukwipfira akava ku isi kuko n’ubundi asanga ari yo maherezo. Muri
izi nkuru usanga umuntu abura aho apfunda imitwe, aho agannye hose asanga
amaherere yamutanze imbere akifuza ko nyamunsi yaza ikamwanzuranya akigira
kwa nyamutezi atagumye kugaragurika mu ruzurungutane rw’ibibazo bidashira.
Inkuru nyarwanda yenda kwegera ubu bwoko bw’inkuru ni Yatashye atagomba
ya Phocas Nkurikiyumukiza.
12. Inkuru ndende z’uburere
Kubura icyo ufata n’icyo ureka byaranze Abanyafurika b’igisekuru cyose cya
makumyabiri, barerewe mu mico n’imigenzereze y’i Burayi kubera amashuri,
bagahuza ubwenge n’amaso n’imigi minini nka Parisi (Paris), Londoni (Londres),
bakabangamirwa no kwibaza ukuntu bazatahuka iwabo bagasubira mu mwijima,
mu bukene, mu bujiji… Ngibyo ibyo ahenshi inkuru z’uburere zuririraho, aho
usanga umukinankuru yabuze icyo afata n’icyo areka hagati y’imico mishyashya
yasanze aho yagiye n’ibya gakondo yakuriyemo, maze kubivanga bikamuviringa
ubwenge, abazi kuvuga neza bati « naka yasarishijwe n’ubwenge ». Iyo usesenguye
neza, usanga mu Rwanda nta nkuru ndende dusanga iri neza muri uyu murongo,
ariko hari izigaragaza iryo sizana ry’ibya kera n’iby’ubu. Urugero rutangwa ni
nka Mwanankundi ya Karege Fidèle aho avugamo umunyarubuga Mwanankundi
wagiye kwiga i Burayi ibyerekeranye n’imibereho myiza n’imibanire y’abantu.
Aho Mwanankundi atahukiye yashatse kuvuganira abagore no kubarengera
imbere y’umuco wabakandamizaga, wa kera ushaje, abatsimbaraye ku bya kera
baramwivuganye ngo arabatobera.

161

13. Inkuru ndende nshyashya
Izi nkuru zadutse nyuma y’intambara ya kabiri y’isi yose. Mbere yaho, inkuru
ndende yasaga n’iyubakiye ku muntu wari warahawe agaciro gakomeye, yenda
tutavuze kuva akiremwa, ariko byibuze mu rwego rw’ubuvanganzo, mu mpumeko
y’ibyo bise ivukabushya « renaissance » mu kinyejana cya cumi na gatanu, bamaze
kuvumbura ibyo gucapisha imashini, umuco n’ubugeni bigahabwa agaciro na
muntu agasa n’uhinduka impagata y’ibyaremwe byose.
Aho intambara zombi z’isi zibereye bakabona ukuntu umuntu ateshwa agaciro,
agasigara arutwa n’ibintu, bamwe batangiye kwibaza niba mu buvanganzo ho
bitahinduka, muntu ntiyongere kuba ari we uhabwa umwanya w’ibanze aho
byose usanga ari we bigushaho : abakinnyi, kuranga, ishushanyamvugo n’ibindi.
Inkuru ndende nshyashya nta kindi bisobanura uretse guhuza abanditsi bose
bashakashaka imiterere mishya y’inkuru ndende zashobora kuvuga cyangwa se
kurema amasano mashya hagati y’umuntu n’isi, ni ukuvuga abiyemeje guhanga
inkuru ndende ari byo kurema. Muri iki gihe usanga inkuru nshyashya isa nk’aho
itigeze ishinga imizi. Iyi nkuru ntishingira ku mukinankuru w’imena nk’uko
byari bimenyerewe. Umwanditsi aha ijambo abantu benshi kandi muri ngenga
ya mbere. Ntumenya uvuga uwo ari we, umubazi w’inkuru arazimira.
Mu nkuru nshyashya, nta nsanganyamatsiko, nta kureshya umusomyi baca
igikuba. Abari bamenyereye ibya kera barayirwanyije karahava, bakavuga ko
atari ukuri ndetse ko ari igisa n’ubuvanganzo kubera ko yanze kuba basabose.
Mu nkuru nshyashya ubona gusa uruhererekane rw’amagambo anyuranye ndetse
yivuguruza, ibitekerezo bituzuye, mbese ibintu by’ikivangavange nk’uko isi yari
imeze ikiremwa. Kugeza ubu mu Kinyarwanda nta rugero rw’inkuru nshyashya
ruraboneka.

14. Inkuru ndende porisi
Inkuru ndende porisi yubakiye ku kuvumbura ubuhanga kandi buhorobuhoro
wifashishije uburyo busanzwe, uko ibintu byagenze ku kintu kidasanzwe cyabaye.
Abashakashatsi n’abanditsi benshi bahuriza hamwe ko inkuru ndende porisi
ari iperereza rikorwa ku buryo busanzwe cyangwa se bwa gihanga. Iri perereza
riba rigamije kuvumbura, guhishura ikintu cyayoberanye, kitumvikana, cyabaye
imenamutwe. Mu magambo avunaguye inkuru ndende porisi ni inkuru y’umuhigo
wa muntu, bakoresheje gutekereza ku tuntu wakwita amafuti bakatubyaza
igisubizo.
Uko inkuru porisi ikura :
– Habanza insanganyamatsiko igizwe n’ibintu bidashobora gusobanura
urebye ikosa ryakozwe.
– Ibimenyetso bidafashije byerekana ukekwa cyangwa abakekwa ; umusomyi
n’ingenza baba bafite amahirwe angana yo gusubiza ikibazo.
– Akenshi muri izi nkuru ukekwa arafatwa ariko icyaha yafungiwe kikongera
kikaba, bigahita bigaragara ko atari we bigasa n’ibisubiye irudubi.

162

–
–
–
–

Kwitegereza neza ibintu no kubyibazaho.
Igisubizo kijyanye n’ibyabaye gikomeza kuba urujijo.
Kwigizayo ibidashoboka byose mu gushakisha igisubizo. Umunyacyaha
avumburwa uhereye ku byo wagiye ubona, ntibapfa kumugwaho
by’agatunguro cyangwa ngo yivemo.
Igisigara nubwo cyaba kitakekwaga ni cyo kiba ari igisubizo nyacyo.

Umwitozo

Bitewe n’ishami wiga, tanga ingero zinyuranye z’inkuru ndende (mu Gifaransa,
mu Cyongereza cyangwa mu Giswayiri) kuri buri bwoko unasobanure impamvu
uyishyize muri ubwo bwoko.

e) Uko basesengura inkuru ndende
Iyo basesengura inkuru ndende bibanda kuri ibi bikurikira: ikivugwa mu nkuru,
abanyarubuga, ibarankuru, akabugankuru, ibikorwa, umugendo w’inkuru,
uburebure n’ahantu. Ibi bigakorwa hakurikijwe amahange n’amahame y’ingenzi
akoreshwa mu gusesengura umwandiko w’ubuvanganzo muri rusange.
Ikivugwa mu nkuru ndende
Ingingo abanditsi b’inkuru ndende bavugaho ni nyinshi kandi ziratandukanye
kimwe n’izo dusanga mu zindi ngeri z’ubuvanganzo. Mu nkuru ndende dusangamo
urukundo rudasibangana, urukundo rwa bugigi, uburere n’umuco wa kera bitajya
imbizi n’uburere n’umuco by’ubu, poritiki n’ubutegetsi, ubwenge bw’indushyi,
uburaya n’ubwomanzi, iyimukacyaro, ubuhemu, ubugome, ishyari, inzangano, amoko,
urupfu rutunamura icumu, ubusabane mu bantu, ubukene n’ubujiji, inkuru ndende
zivuga ku bukoroni…

Abakinankuru (abanyarubuga)
Mu nkuru ndende haba umukinankuru mukuru ushobora kuba umwe cyangwa
babiri. Umukinankuru mukuru ni we uba ari ipfundo ry’inkuru. Ni we ikigamijwe
cyangwa intego y’inkuru iba ishingiyeho. Uyu ni we insanganyamatsiko rusange
ivugwa mu nkuru iba ishingiyeho.
Hari kandi n’abakinankuru bungirije. Aba ni bo usanga mu nkuru bamufasha
kugera ku kigamijwe cyangwa bakamubera imbogamizi. Aba bakinankuru kandi ni
na bo usanga insanganyamatsiko nto cyangwa zungirije zishingiyeho.

Mu nkuru ndende kandi dusangamo cyangwa dushobora gusangamo abakinankuru
ntagombwa, aba bakinankuru iyo urebye usanga kuba mu nkuru kwabo cyangwa
kutagaragaramo nta cyo byahindura ku kivugwa mu nkuru. Nta nsanganyamatsiko
iba ibashingiyeho. Mu yandi magambo twabita indorerezi.

163

Ibarankuru
Mu nkuru ndende, ibarankuru ni kimwe mu biranga inkuru ndende. Hanagaragazwa
ko ibarankuru rishobora gukorwa ku buryo bune:
–
–
–
–

Umubarankuru ashobora kubara inkuru na we ubwe akinamo. Ni muri urwo
rwego usanga akoresha ngenga ya mbere.
Umubarankuru ashobora kubara inkuru ari hanze yayo. Aha usanga akoresha
ngenga ya gatatu asa n’uvuga ibintu yareberaga iruhande mu gihe byabaga.
Umubarankuru ashobora kubara inkuru ye ubwe. Ni muri urwo rwego usanga
akoresha ngenga ya mbere kuko ibyo avuga aba abivuga kuri we.
Umubarankuru ashobora kandi kubara inkuru yiha gutekerereza umukinankuru.
Mu kubara inkuru kwe usanga yiha kubara ibyo umukinankuru yatekerezaga
igihe amubaraho inkuru.

Ikitonderwa:
Umubarankuru atandukanye n’umwanditsi w’inkuru. Umwanditsi w’inkuru ni
umuhanzi wanditse inkuru ibarwa mu gitabo ke. Muri uko kwandika inkuru ye agena
uburyo ibarwa. Muri ubwo buryo ibarwamo haba hari umuntu ugenda uyibara, uwo
akaba ari we mubarankuru. Cyakora hari igihe umwanditsi ashobora kuba ari na we
mubarankuru igihe abara inkuru y’ubuzima bwe.

Akabugankuru (Ahantu)
Inkuru ndende kandi irangwa no kuba ifite aho ibarirwa; ni ukuvuga akabugankuru.
Mu nkuru ndende akabugankuru gashobora kuba kazwi cyangwa ari agahimbano.
Iyo uwandika inkuru avuga ibyabaye ashobora no kuvuga mu by’ukuri aho byabereye
hazwi. Iyo abara inkuru y’ibitarabayeho, cyakora bishobora kubaho mu buzima
rusange, ashobora gukoresha akabugankuru mpimbano; akavuga ibintu byabereye
ahantu runaka ariko hatazwi ku ikarita y’isi.
Ibikorwa
Ibikorwa mu nkuru ndende bishingira ku bakinankuru cyane cyane ku mukinankuru
mukuru. We n’abakinankuru bungirije bashinzwe kuyobora imigendekere y’ibikorwa
byo mu nkuru kugeza ku ndunduro y’inkuru. Bamwe barema imbaraga zimufasha
kugera ku ntego umwanditsi w’inkuru aba yamuhaye. Nk’uko abasesenguzi b’inkuru
ndende babyemeza, inkuru ndende iyo ari yo yose irangwa no kuba ishobora
gukorerwa ishushanyabikorwa.

Ishushanyabikorwa mu nkuru ndende
Abakinnyi b’imena bafatanyije n’abungirije bayobora imigendekere y’ibikorwa mu
nkuru ndende kugeza ku ndunduro y’inkuru. Bityo mu nkuru ndende umusesenguzi
ashobora gushushanya ibikorwa yifashishije igishushanyo giteye gitya:

164

Ugenera/
Nyakugaba

Ikigamijwe/
Intego

Ugenerwa/nyiri
inyungu

Abafasha/
Inyunganizi

Nyiri ubwite/
Ruhamwa/
Icyatwa

Imbogamizi.

Nyiri ubwite: uyu ni we mukinankuru mukuru inkuru iba ishingiyeho, ni we uba
ufite intego agamije kugeraho muri iyo nkuru. Aba ashobora kuyigeraho cyangwa
ntayigereho.

Ikigamijwe: ni icyo umukinankuru mukuru aba agamije kugeraho mu nkuru. Ni
intego aba yahawe n’umwanditsi w’inkuru.

Ugenera: ni igituma umukinankuru mukuru agira intego afite muri iyo nkuru.
Ashobora kuba undi mukinankuru cyangwa ikindi kintu gishobora gutuma agira
intego runaka.
Ugenerwa: mu yandi magambo ni nyiri inyungu; ni uwo ari we wese mu nkuru wagira
icyo yunguka mu gihe umukinankuru mukuru ageze ku cyo yari agamije mu nkuru.

Abafasha: ni abakinankuru cyangwa ikindi kintu gishobora, mu nkuru, gutuma
umukinankuru mukuru agera ku cyo yari agamije, cyangwa ikigerageza
kumushyigikira mu rugendo rwe rwose kimufasha, kabone n’iyo atakigeraho mu
irangira ry’inkuru.
Imbogamizi: ni abakinankuru cyangwa ikindi kintu gishobora, mu nkuru,
gutuma umukinankuru mukuru atagera ku cyo yari agamije, cyangwa ikigerageza
kumubangamira mu rugendo rwe rwose kimubuza amahirwe kabone n’iyo yagera
ku cyo yari agamije mu irangira ry’inkuru, ariko kikaba cyamubangamiraga.

Umugendo w’inkuru
Mu nkuru, ibikorwa bishobora gukurikirana uko byagiye biba mu mateka yabyo.
Cyakora abanditsi b’inkuru ndende b’abahanga, usanga bakora ibyo twakwita
gukwikira inkuru, bakagenda babara inkuru nyinshi icyarimwe kandi zidakurikiranye
uko zabaye mu mateka. Umwe mu basesenguzi b’inkuru ndende T. Todorov mu gitabo
ke “Théorie de la littérature, 2001” asobanura ko hari uburyo butatu bwo gukwikira
inkuru ari bwo kuyikurikiranya mu mateka yayo (a, b, c, d...), kuyikiranya (a, b, a, b,
c, d, c, d...) no kuyivangavanga (z, a, c, e...).

165

Uburebure
Inkuru ndende nk’uko izina ryayo ribigaragaza, irangwa no kuba ari ndende koko.
Kuba hari uburyo ibikorwa bikurikirana kandi bigenda bitera amatsiko usoma
ku buryo atarambirwa n’uburebure bwayo. Uburebure bw’inkuru ndende kandi
bugaragarira mu inyuranamo ry’inkuru nyinshi zitadukanye kandi ritarambirana.
Inyigisho irimo n’indangagaciro
Buri nkuru cyangwa buri gihangano cy’ubuvanganzo kiba gikubiyemo inyigisho
n’indangagaciro runaka, ni ngombwa ko usesengura inkuru abigaragaza.
Inshamake
Usesengura inkuru kandi aba agomba gukora inshamake yayo, igaragaramo
iby’ingenzi bivugwamo.
Umwanditsi w’inkuru
Ni ngombwa ko usesengura agaragaza ubuzima bw’umwanditsi w’inkuru ndetse
n’ibindi bihangano bye. Ibi bishobora kumufasha guhuza ibivugwa mu nkuru
n’ubuzima busanzwe.
f) Itandukaniro ry’inkuru ndende n’inkuru ngufi by’umwihariko
Inkuru ndende

Inkuru ngufi

- Inkuru ndende igira abakinankuru
benshi n’ibikorwa byinshi.
- Inkuru ndende isobekeranya inkuru
nyinshi nubwo insanganyamatsiko aba
ari imwe.
- Umugendo w’inkuru ndende usanga
ugenda ucurikiranya ibikorwa.
- Usanga utubugankuru cyangwa se
ahakinirwa ari henshi.
- Inkuru ndende yo ishobora no kuvuga
ibya kera.

ibikorwa bike.
- Inkuru ngufi iba yubakiye ku nkuru

- Aho inkuru ibera ntihagomba kuba
henshi.
- Inkuru ngufi iba ivuga ibintu bishya,
byabaye bidasanzwe.

Umukoro wo guhanga inkuru ndende wandika
Ugendeye ku mabwiriza agenga imyandikire n’imiterere y’inkuru ndende, tangira
uhimbe wandika agace k’inkuru ndende ku nsanganyamatsiko wihitiyemo
n’ubwoko bw’inkuru wihitiyemo ku buryo uzayisomera bagenzi bawe mu ruhame.
(Ntimurenze impapuro 20 kandi ntimuge hasi y’impapuro 10.)

166

Ururimi n’ubwumvane

Ongera usome inkuru ya Rugigana n’ingoma ye maze ugaragaze
uvuga, ubwirwa n’ikivugwa. Ukore ubushakashatsi mu nzu y’isomero
ugaragaze inshoza y’ubwumvane, amafatizo y’ubwumvane, indanguruzi
y’ubwumvane n’imimaro y’ubwumvane.
Inshoza y’ubwumvane
Ururimi urwo ari rwo rwose, rutuma abaruvuga iyo bava bakagera bumvana, umwe
yavuga, undi agasobanukirwa n’ibyo avuze. Ibyo bituma umuntu abasha kugeza
ku bandi ibyo atekereza, ibyo yaba azi bo batazi cyangwa se akabashushanyiriza
amagambo, ibyo yabonye ariko bo batabona n’amaso yabo muri ako kanya bavugana.
Ibi byerekana ko ururimi ari igikoresho cy’ubwumvane. Kubera iyo mpamvu, ururimi
ni inzira y’ingenzi n’ikigega k’ibitekerezo n’imico y’abo ruhuza. Ururimi n’ubwumvane
bishobora kwigwa mu mpushya enye z’ingenzi : amafatizo y’ubwumvane, indanguruzi
y’ubwumvane, imimaro y’ururimi n’amategeko ngombwa agenga ubwumvane.
Amafatizo y’ubwumvane.

1. Uvuga: ni we utangiza ikiganiro, agakomoza ku kivugwaho. Aba afite uwo abwira
agakenera kubona igisubizo mu magambo cyangwa se mu bikorwa ku byo yavuze.
2. Ubwirwa: Yakira ibyo agejejweho n’uvuga. Ubwirwa agomba kumva no
gusobanukirwa neza n’ibyo yabwiwe mbere y’uko atanga igisubizo.
3. Ingambo: ni ubutumwa nyiri izina uvuga ageza ku ubwirwa.
4. Inzira: Duhereye ku buryo ibivugwa bigenda bikagera ku ubwirwa, habaho
inzira mbona nkubone cyangwa inzira mbonera iyo uvuga avugana n’uwo abwira
amaso ku yandi. Iyo uvuga yumvikanye n’uwo abwira haciyemo akanya, inzira
ikivugwa cyaciyemo iba ibaye inzira iziguye. Habaho n’inzira itaziguye nk’iyo
hakoreshejwe terefone cyangwa ibindi bikoresho by’itumanaho bikora nka yo.
5. Inkurikizo cyangwa interamvugo: ururimi cyangwa imvugo ya nyakuvuga
byumvikanisha mu buryo bufatika inshoza y’ahantu cyangwa y’igihe
y’ikivugwaho. Interamvugo rero cyangwa inkurikizo ni ururimi, ibimenyetso
cyangwa amarenga. Uvuga ashobora gukoresha imvugo cyangwa inyandiko. Iyi
mvugo cyangwa inyandiko byumvwa cyangwa bigasomwa nyakuvuga aba ashaka
kugeza ku ubwirwa ubutumwa. Ibi twabigereranya n’ibyapa byo ku muhanda
biyobora gusa ababiziranyeho. Ni bo baba basobanukiwe neza n’ubutumwa
bitanga cyangwa n’ikivugwa na byo.
6. Ikivugwaho: ni icyo abavugana baba bavuganaho. Abavugana bagomba kuba
bagihuriyeho, bakiziranyeho.

167

Indanguruzi y’ubwumvane
Dore uko ubwumvane bugenda bukurikije indanguruzi yabwo.
Ikivugwaho

Uvuga

Ubutumwa/
Ingambo
Inzira/
umuyoboro

Ubwirwa

Inkurikizo.

Imimaro y’ubwumvane
Ururimi ni igikoresho k’ingenzi mu buzima bw’umuntu. Abantu bose iyo bava
bakagera iyo bashyikirana mu biganiro, mu mbwirwaruhame, mu ikinamico, mu
misango y’ubukwe... bakoresha ururimi. Uwagize ikibazo akagira ubumuga bwo
kutavuga, bwo kudasohora ijwi, agira uburyo yihariye acishamo ubutumwa bwe
bukagera ku bo ashaka ko bamwumva nta shiti.
Abahanga mu iyigandimi, basanze ururimi rugira imimaro myinshi, ariko iy’ingenzi
ni umumaro nyakuvuga, umumaro nyakubwirwa, umumaro nkurikizo, umumaro
nyanzira, umumaro nyangambo.

1) Umumaro nyakuvuga
Uvuga yifashisha ururimi cyangwa imvugo kugira ngo avuge, yumvikanishe mu
buryo butaziguye akamuri ku mutima. Bamwe bawita kandi umumaro nsesekaza
cyangwa nsesekazamutima.
Ingero:
1. Umugeni ageze mu mpinga ati: ”Ahwi! Nari mfuye Mana y’i Rwanda!
2. Mbega we! Ntuzi ibyabaye!
3. Icyampa nkazatabaruka nirereye abana.

Ntitwabura kuvuga ko hari n’abanditsi bakoresha uyu mumaro bashyira ikivugwa
ku musomyi kandi mu by’ukuri atari umusomyi kivuga.

168

2) Umumaro nyakubwirwa
Izingiro ry’uyu mumaro ni nyakubwirwa. Wumvisha ubwirwa ko ari we shingiro,
ko ari we urebwa n’ubwumvane. Hakoreshwa ngenga ya kabiri hagamijwe:
– Gusobanura ubwirwa mu bandi.
– Kumukesha umutima ngo yakire ibyo amubwiye.
– Kumuhwitura…
Uyu mumaro ugaragarira cyanecyane mu nteko no mu mvugo yumvikanisha
guhamagara. Soma ingero zikurikira zibyerekana.
Ingero:
Igihe utanga ikiganiro kuri SIDA, ugasobanura uko yamenyekanye, uko yandura, uko
ivurwa, ubushakashatsi ku miti n’inkingo… Noneho ukarangiza wereka abo ubwira
ko ruriye abandi rutabibagiwe ko na bo ishobora kubageraho baramutse batirinze.
3) Umumaro nkurikizo
Ni ibimenyetso byose byerekana icyo ubutumwa bwerekejeho. Amagambo
akoreshwa aha uyabwirwa n’ishusho y’ahantu cyangwa y’ibihe ibivugwa
bihererejweho. Bamwe bawita kandi umumaro ndengarurimi cyangwa
nsobanuzi.
Urugero:
«Nsanze ari ijuru ry’umwezi
Nange mpimbiraho umwato
Ni ko kumwita umwanga kurutwa
Nsubiye mwita Marebe yera
Kandi atembaho amaribori
Ni umutako w’urutanisha
Ni ubutijima bw’urukundo
Ni urukenyerero rw’inkindi
Nkunda inkesha ze z’umukwira. »
(Rugamba C., Amibukiro, pp.34).

4) Umumaro nyabusizi (nyaburanga/nyangambo)
Mu bwumvane, inzira yose igira uburyo bwayo. Nyakuvuga yifashisha ururimi,
bityo agakoresha imvugo cyangwa inyandiko. Imiryohere y’inganzo yose
uzayisanga muri uyu mumaro. Injyana, isubirajwi, isubirajambo, amoko
y’imizimizo cyangwa inyandiko inogeye amaso ndetse n’amagambo y’ikivugwa
uko yakabaye hatitaweho ibisobanuro byayo, ibyo byose bigaragaza umumaro
nyangambo w’ururimi bita kandi nyabusizi.
Ingero:
1. Udutoki dutatu dutonze udutemeri.
2. Isha y’umushishe y’ishashi ishoka icitse ijosi.
3. Ugiye bagushishira uzagaruka bagushishimuye.

169

5) Umumaro nyanzira
Uyu mumaro tuwusanga mu magambo ya nyakuvuga mu gihe atangiye, akomeje
cyangwa se asubitse ubwumvane cyangwa se agenzura ko umuyoboro (inzira)
ari ntamakemwa. Iyi nzira y’ubwumvane ishobora kuba umurongo wa terefoni,
ishusho, igishushanyo cyangwa se n’ibindi bikoresho by’itumanaho byakora nka
terefoni. Aha rero ururimi rugira umumaro wo gutangira, gukomeza, gusigasira,
gusubika, gusubukura cyangwa se guhagarika ubwumvane.
Ingero:
Allo!
Birumvikana?
Si byo se?
Urebye rero…
Nyine…
Nawe urabyumva…
Nuko…
Mbese mwaramutse bantu bakuru?

6) Umumaro nyakivugwa
Iyo umuntu avuga hari ubutumwa cyangwa amakuru aba ashaka gutanga, akaba
ari amakuru afitiye gihamya.
Dore uko imimaro y’ubwumvane igaragara ku ndanguruzi y’ubwumvane:
Umumaro
nyakivugwa

Umumaro
nyakuvuga/
nsesekaza

Umumaro
nyabusizi/
nyangambo

Umumaro
nyanzira

Umumaro
ndengarurimi/
nsobanuzi/
nyankurikizo

Umumaro
nyakubwirwa

Ikitonderwa:
Nta nyandiko cyangwa umwandiko wiharirwa n’umumaro w’ururimi umwe gusa.
Imimaro myinshi ishobora kugaragarira icya rimwe mu mwandiko cyangwa
mu kivugwa kimwe mu buryo no mu bwiganze butandukanye. Cyakora kugira

170

ngo itumanaho ribe ryuzuye imimaro yose igomba kuba irimo akaba ari na byo
bitandukanya imvugo y’umuntu n’iy’inyamaswa.

Amategeko ngombwa agenga ubwumvane
Kugira ngo ubwumvane bushoboke, hari amategeko ngombwa agomba kubahirizwa.
Uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba:
– Guhuza inkurikizo: uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba kuba bahuje ururimi cyangwa
ibimenyetso bakoresha.
– Guhurira ku kivugwa: uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba kuba bafite ikivugwa
baziranyeho. Iyo bataziranye ku kivugwa ntibashobora kumvikana.
– Guhuza inzira: uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba guhuza uburyo bakoresha kugira ngo
bumvikane. Niba ari terefone bombi bagomba kuba bayifite, yaba ari ibaruwa,
bombi bakaba bazi gusoma.
Umwitozo

1. Mu Rwanda iyo Abanyarwanda bashyikirana bakoresha inzira zitandukanye.
Andika inzira zose ubona zishoboka zaba zikoreshwa n’uvuga kugira ngo
ikivugwa kigere ku ubwirwa.
2. Wifashishije indanguruzi y’ubwumvane, garagaza amasano agenga ubwumvane
mu misango y’ubukwe.
3. Tegura umwandiko mugufi (ipaji imwe), ugaragaramo imimaro yose
y’ubwumvane. Ushobora no kuba ikiganiro hagati y’abantu babiri. Erekana
igice umumaro runaka ugaragaramo kandi uwusobanure.

Inshamake y’ibyizwe mu mutwe wa munani
–

–

–

Inkuru ndende ari ingeri ubuvanganzo nyandiko irangwa no kuba ari ndende,
kugira abakinankuru benshi, gusobekeranya insanganyamatsiko nyinshi no
kubara inkuru y’ibyabayeho cyangwa ibitarabayeho ariko bishushanya ubuzima
rusange tubamo. Inkuru ndende itandukanye n’inkuru ngufi ku burebure
bwayo, ku kuba ifite abakinankuru benshi, ku kuba igira insanganyamatsiko
nyinshi n’abakinankuru benshi. Inkuru ndende itandukanye n’umugani kuko
mu mugani havugwamo ibitarabayeho kandi bidashobora no kubaho mu
buzima rusange.
Ururimi ni igikoresho gituma abavugana aho bava bakagera bashobora
kuganira bakumvikana. Ubwo bwumvane bushingiye ku bintu bitandatu
by’ingenzi ari byo : uvuga, ubwirwa, ingambo, inzira, interamvugo/ inkurikizo,
ikivugwaho. Amasano y’ayo mafatizo ashobora kwerekanishwa igishushanyo
kitwa indanguruzi y’ubwumvane.
Umumaro nyanteramvugo cyangwa nkurikizo ushingiye ku byerekana
igihe, ahantu n’imiterere bwite by’ubutumwa butangwa. Ibyo bishobora

171

–

guhita bisobanuka mu magambo yakoreshejwe (umumaro nyanteramvugo
nyansano). Bishobora kugaragara mu myubakire y’interuro ariko inkurikizo
yo mu magambo ntibe mu bisobanuro asanganwe. Ibyo tubisanga cyane mu
mizimizo, aho ijambo rita igisobanuro risanganywe ahubwo rigasobanurwa
n’ayo biri kumwe (umumaro nyantera mvugo nyambere). Umumaro nyabusizi
uba mu miryohereze y’injyana n’isubirajwi n’isubirajambo binogeye amatwi,
bikamera nk’umutako w’ikivugwa nyiri izina.
Kugira ngo habeho ubwumvane, uvuga n’ubwirwa bagomba guhuza neza
inkurikizo, guhurira ku kivugwa no guhuza inzira y’ubwumvane.

Isuzuma risoza umutwe wa munani
Kora ku giti cyawe, usome uyu mwandiko maze usubize ibibazo bikurikira
wubahiriza amabwiriza yatanzwe.

Umwandiko: Kwihesha agaciro
Kera ba Kazungu bataragera i Rwanda, twari dutuje, twunze ubumwe, dusabana amazi,
turahurana umuriro; abenshi banywana ntihagire uhirahira ngo atatire igihango.
Aho abo bera ba Kazungu baziye baratwinjiriye, bazana amatwara ya mpatsibihugu,
amatwara yabo atwambura ireme, agaciro kacu karatakara, kwiyandayanda ngo
tukisubize bitubera urujijo cyane ko bazanye ry’ifaranga rikatugira ingwate, kandi
ngo urumiya rwamize inshuti.

Inyigisho za ba gashakabuhake zibanze ahanini ku gucamo abenegihugu ibice ngo
bibe intandaro yo gutegeka. Iyo poritiku nako poritiki yo guca ibice mu benegihugu,
yatugizeho ingaruka, ndetse tutarebye neza zazakomeza kurandagatana zikazasingira
ubuvivi n’ubuvivure bwacu n’abandi b’igihe kizaza bashobora kuzakomeza
kubihomberamo. Hari ingamba zigenda zifatwa uko bwije n’uko bukeye. Mu rwego
rwo kwigira, u Rwanda ubu rwifatiye ingamba zo gushyiraho ikigega kizajya kigoboka
ingengo y’imari ya Leta. Kuko guhora dutegereje ak’imuhana byazatuviramo
kwisuzugura no gusuzugurwa bitagira akagero nk’uko igihe cy’ubukoroni twagiye
tubyerekwa. Mureke natwe twishyirireho akacu.
Ubu abaturage bishyiriraho ubuyobozi, bamwe mu bo tudahuje imyumvire
n’ibitekerezo turababwira bakazinga umunya kubera ko tuzi ibipfa n’ibikira, ko
tuzi kwihitiramo. Ya mvugo ngo: “Ijwi rya rubanda ni ijwi ry’Imana”, yabaye iyacu.
Ubukoroni bwaradushegeshe, bwatumye havaho kandi hasenyuka imiterere
n’imyubakire ya poritiki nyarwanda, biradupyinagaza karahava. Yaciye umucuba ibyo
twari tugezeho, umuco wacu arawuceba: imihango, imigenzo n’imiziririzo birahuna
kandi hari ibyari bicumbikiye ubupfura n’izindi ndangagaciro z’umuco nyarwanda.
Ubukoroni bwashingiye ku moko mpimbano buteza umwiryane, amacakubiri
n’amakimbirane hagati y’Abanyafurika.

172

Ntawakwemeza yivuye inyuma ko ibyo Abanyarwanda bakoraga byose byari
byiza. Imana ya Kazungu imaze gutaha amarembo y’i Rwanda, ukwizera kwacu
kwafashe injyana y’i Burayi; kubandwa, guterekera, kuraguza no gutanga isororo
ry’abavubyi, ibyo byaracitse kandi twemera natwe ko bimwe bitari bikwiye mu kuri
kw’imfura z’i Rwanda. Abantu benshi barapfuye mu gihe habagaho intambara zo
kwigarurira ibihugu by’Afurika, ntawakwiyibagiza Abanyafurika batakaje ubutaka
bwiza ku nyungu z’abakoroni ndetse kugeza uyu munsi nko muri Zimbabwe ibikingi
by’abazungu biracyateye impagarara. Nubwo abakoroni hari aho bafashije kurangiza
intambara hagati y’amoko n’imiryango, hari ibindi bintu bitagenze neza. Bahinduye
imyemerere y’Abanyarwanda n’Abanyafurika muri rusange. None se Kiriziya yari izwi
na ba nde? Ikwirakwira ry’imyemerere ya gikirisitu yahinduye byinshi mu mibereho
ya benshi; abantu bakajya gusenga bakeye, baberewe. Kugeza ubu se tuba tucyambara
impuzu, ishabure n’inkanda?
Ubucuruzi bushingiye ku igurana ry’ibintu, imyaka n’amatungo hari aho byari
kutugeza? Ikibabaje ni ikiboko cya buri munsi na shiku byari byarayogoje
Abanyarwanda hamwe n’abandi Banyafurika bakubitanye na ba Kazungu.
Twarakubititse bihagije, aho ibihe bigeze nimuze dusubize amaso inyuma dushake
ingamba zose zadufasha kwigira, hato wa wundi wadukanze, atongera kutwiba
umugono tugasubira ku kacu, tukongera kuba aka ya nsina ngufi icibwaho urukoma.
Uko kwaba ari ukurangara bitavugwa.

Nta kintu na kimwe kitagira akarango k’ibyiza. Reka tuvuge ko hari ibyiza ubukoroni
bwadusigiye. Abanyarwanda bamenye imahanga uko bamerewe bajyanywe
n’abakoroni, imbuto ziribwa kimwe n’ibindi biribwa bitaha i Rwanda no hirya no
hino muri Afurika. Ariko se tuvuge ko ibi ari byo byabaye ingurane y’amabuye
y’agaciro yagiye akurwa ikuzimu mu bihugu byacu agataha i Burayi? Hari umuhanga
wabyanditse neza avuga ko Afurika yasahuwe cyane umutungo wayo. Ikindi kandi
uwo mugabo yasanze mu bushakashatsi bwe Abanyafurika twari dusanzwe dufite
inzego z’ubuyobozi zubakitse neza, kuzimeneramo kwari ukubanza kuzisenya. Kugira
ngo gashakabuhake atuyobore yabanje rero gusenya inkingi z’umuco wacu n’inzego
z’ubutegetsi.
Ni ukuri, nimuze dufatanye dufate ingamba zo gukomeza gushaka uburyo twisubiza
agaciro abakoroni batunyagishije igitugu gihishemo amayeri menshi. Twarivumbuye,
havuka imitwe ibarwanya imyinshi iraneshwa; abanditsi babigaragaza mu nyandiko
zitandukanye, kuko burembe mu myaka ya za mirongo itandatu batangira kuva ku
izima. Hari Abanyafurika b’abanyeporitiki cyangwa b’abanditsi bagize ubutwari
bwo kurwanya abakoroni. Nko mu banditsi twavuga Nkwame Nkuruma (Nkwame
Nkrumah), Lewopori Sedari Sengoro(Léopold Sédar Senghor) n’abandi. Hari kandi
n’imitwe inyuranye yagiye ibarwanya muri yo twatanga urugero rw’Abamawumawu
muri Kenya na Abamayimayi muri Tanzaniya... Iyo mitwe yumvaga igomba kurwanya
Abakoroni b’Abongereza bivuye inyuma.

173

Duhereye iwacu mu miryango; niba uri umunyeshuri wiyubaha, umenye icyo
gukora, umusore na we ntatege amaboko ngo bibyarwa n’inzu! Icyuho abakoroni
baciyemo muze tukizitire ibyo badufashijemo byiza tubikomeze ariko iby’akarengane
badutegekesha igitugu n’ibindi bipfobya Umunyarwanda n’Umunyafurika wiyubaha
tubyamaganire kure. Twirinde gusuma ngo dusumire inzaduka z’amahanga ngo ni
iterambere. Oya, sigaho! Nitwikosore duce ukubiri no guhora duteze barampa nka
cya gikobwa k’icyomanzi gihwanye na cya gicu cya Nyantango cyabonye ibicu birenga
ngo “Iwacu barantuye”, naho byahe biragatabwa!
Bifatiye ku byanditse kwa Gabiro, R., Ntunyibutse ikiboko, 2015, Kigali, Rwanda.

I. Ibibazo byo kumva no gusesengura umwandiko
		
1. Ingingo nkuru n’izungirije zavuzweho cyane mu mwandiko ni izihe?
		
2. Kuba tugikenera inkunga y’amahanga ntitwabifata nk’ubukoroni?
Sobanura.
		
3. Abanyafurika mu mpande zitandukanye bigaragaje bate berekana
akarengane kabo?
		
4. Hari ingingo zihamya ko Abanyarwanda bahoze bumvikana, bafashanya.
Zirondore.
		
5. Poritiki n’inyigisho n’imikorere y’abakoroni byari byiza byose. Sobanura
utarondogoye.
		
6. Poritiki abakoroni bakoresheje kugira ngo batwigarurire ni iyihe?
Yatugizeho izihe ngaruka?
		
7. Ijwi ry’abaturage ni ijwi ry’Imana. Iyi mvugo urayemera? Sobanura.

9

Ibidukikije

Umwandiko: Amazi n’amashyamba

Hari igihe kimwe amapfa yibasiye ibihugu byo mu majyaruguru y’Afurika, abimukira
bakajya bava mu bihugu binyuranye bakambuka ubutayu bunini berekeza mu bihugu
by’i Burayi. Mu rugendo bakoraga bambuka ubutayu bagendaga bahura n’ibizazane
bikomeye. Urugero twafata ni urw’umuryango w’Abanyarwanda wari warimukiye
mu majyaruguru y’Afurika. Wari ugizwe n’umugabo Gahigi, umugore we Mutesi
n’abana babo babiri Nyiramana w’umukobwa na Kamana w’umuhungu. Mbere yo
kwambuka ubutayu bari biteguye bihagije urwo rugendo kubera ko rumara igihe

177

kirekire. Bamaze kurucagasa, ibyo bari bitwaje birarangira. Mu nzira bahahuriye
n’abandi bimukira bavaga hirya no hino bafatanya urugendo bakagenda basaranganya
ibyo bafite. Bacagashije urugendo, Gahigi araraba yitura hasi bashaka kumuramira
babura ubushobozi arabacika asigara aho. Barakomeza, urugendo rubabana rurerure
dore ko hari n’igihe basubiraga aho bavuye kubera ko kubona amerekezo mu butayu
bigoranye.

Igihe kiragera wa mwana w’umuhungu Kamana na we agira umwuma nka se aba
araguye. Muri wa muryango haba hasigaye wa mukobwa na nyina, bakomeza
kurimbanya. Hashize icyumweru kimwe, kubera agahinda no gucika intege, Mutesi
na we azira urw’umugabo we yazize arapfa. Hasigara ka gakobwa Nyiramana kisunga
abandi bakomeza urugendo. Bambuka ubutayu bagera ku nyanja barahembuka.
Nyiramana anywa amazi arakira amererwa neza yisunga umuryango bari kumwe
barakomeza baragenda. Amaze gutora agatege atangira kuganira n’umugabo wo
muri uwo muryango yisunze.
Yashakaga kumenya impamvu aho banyuze hose nta mazi bigeze babona. Uwo
mugabo yabazaga yari umuntu ugwa neza kandi ukunda kuganira, maze aramusubiza
amubwira ko abantu bagiye bafata nabi amashyamba bakayatsemba imvura ikabura,
amasoko n’imigezi bigakama, ibyo akaba ari byo byateye amapfa. Arakomeza
aramusobanurira amubwira ko kubera ayo mapfa, abari bahatuye basuhutse
bagahunga inzara berekeza ishyanga. Abashatse kugundira ngo bigumire mu bikingi
byabo bazira umwuma no kubura umwuka mwiza wo guhumeka. Aho bari batuye
hahinduka ubutayu hatyo.
Wa mugabo akomeza gusobanurira Mutesi n’abo bari kumwe ababwira akamaro
k’amazi n’amashyamba n’izindi ngaruka z’ibura ryabyo ku buzima bwa muntu.
Abasobanurira ko iyo imvura iguye abantu bahinga imyaka ikera. Arakomeza ati:
“Abubaka amazu na bo babifashwamo n’ayo mazi. Ingufu z’amashanyarazi zikoreshwa
ku isi inyinshi zikomoka ku mazi kandi ni zo zivugwa ko zihendutse kurusha izindi.
Ibiyaga, imigezi n’inzuzi bifatiye runini abaturiye isi; ni byo bibitse ibinyabuzima
bifitiye abantu akamaro nk’amafi, inyamaswa zitandukanye ndetse n’ibimera. Hamwe
na hamwe amazi ni yo mipaka y’ibihugu n’iy’imigabane.”

Wa mugabo ntiyibagiwe no kubasobanurira uruhare amashyamba afitiye abatuye isi.
Amashyamba ni isoko y’umwuka mwiza duhumeka, ni na yo akurura imvura. Tudafite
amashyamba ntitwabona ibikoresho dukenera mu buzima bwa buri munsi nk’intebe,
ibitanda, ameza… Kimwe n’amazi, amashyamba na yo adufasha mu kubaka amazu
tubamo, akanaba n’intaho y’inyamaswa zimwe na zimwe. Amashyamba afata ubutaka
agatuma budatwarwa n’isuri. Amashyamba kandi ni isoko y’ubukerarugendo
buzanira ibihugu amadovize. Ibiti bimwe na bimwe biboneka mu mashyamba cyane
cyane aya kimeza bivamo imiti inyuranye ikoreshwa mu buvuzi gakondo ndetse n’indi
bakoresha kwa muganga ibanje guca mu nganda. Hari ibiti byinshi biduha imbuto
zigirira umubiri wacu akamaro.

178

Akimara kubasobanurira akamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba, abari aho baratangara
maze buri wese muri bo afata ingamba z’icyo yakora kugira ngo abungabunge amazi
n’amashyamba. Wa mukobwa na we ahita abwira abandi ko amenye ikishe abo mu
muryango we igihe bari mu butayu.
Bamaze kugera mu bihugu by’i Burayi bahahurira n’indi mbogamizi y’ururimi. Nubwo
bamwe na bamwe bari baravuye mu gihugu kimwe ntibashoboraga kumvikana
kubera indimi z’uturere twabo zabaga zitandukanye. Abashoboraga kumvikana ni
ababaga bavuga ururimi rumwe ariko nabwo bakaruvuga mu buryo bunyuranye
kubera uturere bakomokagamo twabaga dutandukanye. Bakigera i Burayi bari
barize Igifaransa n’ Icyongereza bumva ko bazumvikana neza n’abaturage b’i Burayi
nyamara si ko byagenze, basanze Igifaransa n’Icyongereza na byo bifite izindi ndimi
zibishamikiyeho ari na zo abaturage b’aho bivugiraga. Uko imyaka yahitaga ni ko
barushagaho kugenda bumvikana ubwabo ndetse bakanumvikana na ba kavukire
bo mu bihugu basuhukiyemo.

I. Inyunguramagambo

A. Sobanura amagambo akurikira:
1. Ibizazane
2. Basaranganya
3. Kuraba
4. Arabacika

5. Kurimbanya		9. Barasuhuka
6. Gutora agatege 		10. Kugundira
7. Kwisunga abandi
11. Ishyanga
8. Amapfa
12. Igikingi cy’umuntu

B. Kora iyi myitozo ku nyunguramagambo usimbuza ijambo ritsindagiye
irindi bivuga kimwe usanga mu mwandiko, wite ku mategeko
ngengasano mu nteruro nshya wubatse.
1. Ukuva kw’izuba ryinshi kandi rikabije byateye ikama ry’ibiyaga n’ibishanga.
2. Amage arisha umugabo ikivuza, inzara se ntiyatumye duhunga tukerekeza i
Bushi.
3. Amazi yarabuze mu mudugudu wacu icyaka gikabije gituma benshi batakaza
ubuzima.
4. Gahigi na Mpwerazikamwa bamaze iminsi bijajaye basubiye mu kibuga.
5. Amazi n’amashyamba bidufitiye akamaro kanini, ntawarota yangiza ibyo
bidukikije.
6. Amashyama n’amazi ni indiri y’inyamaswa n’udukoko dutandukanye.
7. Amafaranga y’amahanga dusigirwa na ba mukerarugendo afasha Igihugu cyacu
muri byinshi.
8. Ku nkiko z’Igihugu cyacu mu burengerazuba hari ikiyaga cya Kivu.

179

II. Ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko

Subiza ibi bibazo byo kumva umwandiko:
1. Ni ubuhe butayu abimukira bambukaga buri mu majyaruguru ya Afurika?
2. Mu mwandiko batubwira ko abo bimukira bahungaga amapfa. Ese amapfa aterwa
n’iki?
3. Ni gute ibyo bari bitwaje byarangiriye mu nzira kandi barahagurutse biteguye
bihagije kandi bazi ko urwo rugendo ruzamara igihe kinini?
4. Abo mu muryango wa Gahigi babuze ubushobozi bwo kumuramira arapfa. Ukeka
ko ari ubuhe bushobozi buvugwa aha?
5. Ni akahe kamaro k’amazi n’amashyamba kagaragara mu mwandiko?
6. Ni gute imigezi n’inzuzi bifatiye runini abatuye isi?
7. Vuga nibura inyamaswa eshatu zitaha mu mashyamba?
8. Ni ibihe biti waba uzi bivamo imiti gakondo?
9. Ni ibihe biti biduha imbuto turya?
10. Imbuto duhabwa n’ibyo biti zigirira akahe kamaro umubiri wacu?
11. Umwandiko ujya kurangira batubwira ko wa mukobwa yamenye ikishe abo mu
muryango we? Utekereza ko yavumbuye ko bishwe n’iki?

III. Gusesengura umwandiko

Tekereza ku buryo bwimbitse maze usubize ibibazo bikurikira:
1. Ni iyihe nsanganyamatsiko ivugwa mu mwandiko?
2. Vuga ingingo z’ingenzi ziri mu mwandiko.
3. Abantu benshi muri Afurika basuhukira i Burayi. Urakeka ko bose baba
bajyanywe no gushaka amaramuko?
4. Nutema kimwe uge utera bibiri. Iyi mvugo ifite ishingiro? Sobanura ku buryo
bwumvikana.
5. Hina umwandiko wasomye mu mirongo itarenze icumi.

IV. Kungurana ibitekerezo

Ungurana ibitekerezo na bagenzi bawe kuri iyi nsanganyamatsiko:
Ganira na bagenzi bawe muvuga ku buryo bwo kubungabunga ibidukikije buri mu
mwandiko n’ubutagaragara mu mwandiko.

180

Umukoro wo guhanga
Hanga umwandiko ntekerezo kuri imwe muri izi nsanganyamatsiko zikurikira:
– Amazi ni isoko y’ubuzima.
– Nutema kimwe uge utera bibiri.

Imiterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda

Soma iki gika kivuye mu mwandiko wizwe maze ukore ubushakashatsi
kuri ibi bibazo: Ururimi rw’igihugu ni iki? Ururimi kavukire ni iki?
Ururimi rw’ubutegetsi ni iki? Ururimi rw’akarere ni iki? Kora ubundi
bushakashatsi bwimbitse ku miterere y’iby’indimi mu Rwanda ugaragaze
n’aho zivugirwa ku ikarita.
[…]

Bamaze kugera mu bihugu by’i Burayi bahahurira n’indi mbogamizi y’ururimi. Nubwo
bamwe na bamwe bari baravuye mu gihugu kimwe ntibashoboraga kumvikana
kubera indimi z’uturere twabo zabaga zitandukanye. Abashoboraga kumvikana ni
ababaga bavuga ururimi rumwe ariko nabwo bakaruvuga mu buryo bunyuranye
kubera uturere bakomokagamo twabaga dutandukanye. Bakigera i Burayi bari
barize Igifaransa n’Icyongereza kuko ari zo zari indimi z’ubutegetsi mu bihugu
bakomotsemo, bumva ko bazumvikana neza n’abaturage b’i Burayi nyamara si ko
byagenze, basanze Igifaransa n’Icyongereza na byo bifite izindi ndimi zibishamikiyeho
ari na zo abaturage b’aho bivugiraga. Uko imyaka yahitaga ni ko barushagaho
kugenda bumvikana ubwabo ndetse bakanumvikana na ba kavukire bo mu bihugu
basuhukiyemo.

1. Inshoza y’ururimi nyagihugu
Ururimi nyagihugu ni ururimi igihugu kiba kihariye ubwacyo kandi rukaba
rwemewe n’amategeko. Zishobora no kuba nyinshi nko muri Repubulika
iharanira Demokarasi ya Kongo. Inshoza y’ururimi nyagihugu mu bihugu bimwe
na bimwe yitiranywa n’iy’ururimi rw’ubutegetsi kubera ko akenshi ururimi
nyagihugu rukoreshwa mu mishyikirano n’ubutegetsi n’umuco.
Mu Rwanda ururimi nyagihugu ni Ikinyarwanda cyo hagati.
Ingero:
Abanyarwanda bavuga Ikinyarwanda, Abarundi bavuga Ikirundi…

2. Inshoza y’ururimi kavukire
Ubusanzwe tuvuga ko kanaka avuga ururimi kavukire iyo tumugereranya n’uvuga
ururimi nyagihugu rufatwa nk’imvugo mbonera cyangwa ururimi mbonera kandi

181

rwo rukaba rufite ingufu. Icyo gihe imvugo kavukire ifatwa nk’imvugo itanoze,
akenshi ikaba isuzugurwa nk’imvugo y’abantu bake cyangwa se imvugo izingiye
ahantu hamwe kandi hato cyane. Akenshi imvugo kavukire ikoreshwa iyo bavuga.
Ni ururimi rw’aho ukomoka, ni ururimi wavukiyemo ruvugwa n’ababyeyi, ba
sogokuru, ba nyogokuru, ba nyogokuruza, ingoma ibihumbi. Mu yandi magambo
ni ururimi gakondo.

Urugero:
Mu Rwanda Ikinyarwanda ni ururimi kavukire rw’Abanyarwanda benshi.

3. Inshoza y’ururimi rw’ubutegetsi
Ururimi rw’ubutegetsi ni ururimi ruteganywa mu itegeko nshinga ry’igihugu,
rugakoreshwa mu nyandiko z’ubuyobozi cyangwa z’imiryango inyuranye ikorera
mu gihugu. Ni ururimi rukoreshwa mu nyandiko zose z’ubuyobozi, mu nkiko,
mu mabanki...
Urugero:
Ubu Rwanda indimi z’ubutegetsi ni enye: Ikinyarwanda, Igifaransa, Icyongereza
n’Igiswayiri.

4. Inshoza y’ururimi rw’akarere
Ururimi rw’akarere ni ururimi ruturuka ku mihindagurikire y’ururimi nyagihugu
bitewe n’imiryango y’abantu cyangwa akarere abaruvuga baherereyemo.
Urugero:
Ikigoyi n’Ikirera ni indimi z’akarere zikoreshwa mu majyaruguru.

5. Ikinyarwanda n’imvugo z’uturere
Mu Rwanda abantu benshi bahuzwa n’ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda. Ikinyarwanda ni
rumwe mu ndimi nyafurika bita Indimi Bantu cyangwa Nyantu. Izo ndimi zivugwa
munsi y’ubutayu bwa Sahara. Izo ndimi nyantu iyo uzisesenguye neza usanga
zifite byinshi zihuriyeho. Duhereye nko ku Giporotobantu cyari gifite inteko
z’amazina makumyabiri n’enye, zaje kugabanywamo cumi n’esheshatu, bityo
ugasanga n’Ikinyarwanda cyarafashe izo nteko cumi n’esheshatu cyongeraho
izindi eshatu ziranga ahantu, zose hamwe ziba cumi n’ikenda.

Imvugo z’uturere n’aho zivugwa
Abasesenguye indimi basanze ururimi rutavugwa kimwe ku butaka ruvugwaho.
Akarere kamwe kagenda kagira umwihariko wako w’imivugire haba mu majwi,
mu masaku, mu magambo no mu buryo bwo kubaka interuro. Uko ni ko n’ururimi
rw’Ikinyarwanda kitavugwa kimwe mu Rwanda hose. Iyo uzengurutse u Rwanda
usanga hari imvugo z’uturere zigera kuri eshanu. Reka turebere hamwe izo mvugo
z’uturere uko zihagaze mu Rwanda.

182

a) Ikigoyi: Kivugwa mu gice cy’u Rwanda kiri hagati y’ishyamba rya Gishwati
n’Igihugu cya Repubulika Iharanira Demokarasi ya Kongo: Kanama, Nyamyumba
na Rubavu. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rubavu.

b) Ikirera: Kivugwa mu cyahoze ari Ruhengeri hose ukuyemo Ndusu n’akandi gace
gato kavugwamo Igikiga. Ikirera kandi kivugwa mu duce twa Mutura, Giciye,
Karago n’agace gato ka Cyungo. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Musanze.

c) Igikiga: Ni imvugo ivanga Ikirera n’imvugo y’ahahoze ari Gitarama (Akarere
ka Kamonyi na Ruhango). Igikiga kivugwa kandi ahahoze ari Gaseke, Satinsyi,
Kibirira, Ramba, Rutsiro, Kayove na Nyakabanda. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Ngororero.
Uretse Nyakabanda iri mu Ntara y’Amajyepfo, utwo duce twose turi mu Ntara
y’Iburengerazuba. Igikiga kandi kivugwa mu gace ka Mwendo na Gisovu
(Kibuye) no ku ishyamba rya Nyungwe mu duce twa Nshiri, Kivu, Mudasomwa
na Musebeya. (Ubu ni mu Karere Karongi)
d) Igisozo: Kivugwa mu gice giherereye hagati y’ishyamba rya Nyungwe n’u Burundi
n’umuhanda uhuza Nyakabuye Bugarama (Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rusizi). Igisozo
n’igikiga bitangiye gucika kubera Ikinyarwanda rusange. Ntibyoroshye kandi
gutandukanya igikiga n’igisozo.

e) Ikinyarwanda cyo hagati: Nk’uko kitwa, kiganje mu gice cyose cyo hagati mu
Gihugu. Ikinyarwanda cyo hagati ni cyo Kinyarwanda rusange kuko ari cyo
cyagizwe ururimi rwigwa mu mashuri, rugakoreshwa mu itangazamakuru no
mu butegetsi kugira ngo Abanyarwanda bose barusheho kumvikana bakoresheje
imvugo imwe.
f) Ikigoti: Ni ururimi rukoreshwa ku nkiko z’amagepfo mu Karere ka Gisagara mu
Murenge wa Mugombwa.
g) Igishobyo n’Ikiyaka: Izi ndimi zikoreshwa mu karere kamwe ahahoze ari
Kanama na Nyamyumba. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rubavu.

h) Urunyagisaka n’Urunyambo: Izi ndimi zikoreshwa ahahoze ari Perefegitura
ya Kibungo. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Ngoma.

Umwitozo
Ni izihe ndimi z’ubutegetsi zikoreshwa mu Rwanda?

183

Inshami z’Ikinyarwanda

Soma aka kandiko maze ukore ubushakashatsi ku bibazo bigakurikira:
Uzi ko Kankera ari nge nyoko ukubyara. Kera twari dutuye i Bushi, sogokuru mu
burengerazuba bw’u Rwanda, ubu ni muri Repubulika Iharanira Demukarasi ya
Kongo yahoze yitwa Zayire. Twe n’abavandimwe bacu bo ku kirwa cya Nkombo no ku
nkengero za Kivu, twavugaga amashi, amahavu; aho tugereye hano mu majyaruguru
byaratugoye kumvikana n’abaturage bo muri iyi midugudu, ndetse iyo tugannye mu
isoko hari ubwo bidutesha umutwe!
Tukiba i Bushi wasangaga tuvuga tuti: “Hari omusale hari ehisale”. Ugira ngo se
ntitwashakaga kuvuga ko hari umusare n’ingirwamusare! Umusare w’umwuga
yakwambutsaga akujyana ku Ijwi ukishima ariko ba bandi b’abiga hari n’ubwo
atamenyaga ikerekezo cy’umuyaga, umuhengeri ukaba wabateza akaga gakomeye.
Hano ku Mulindi w’intwari twahimukiye bavuga ngo: “Ebirungyi ni ho byeija,
rwombeke rugume, ogumire nk’eibare...” nkayoberwa icyo bashatse kuvuga, ariko ubu
ntacyo bambeshya! None se Ceri, wari uzi izina ryawe icyo risobanura? Twakubyariye
hano ku Mulindi tukwita dutyo!
Cerimpa bahimba Macibiri yasubije nyina atishisha. Nyina ntiyari azi ko Macibiri
yamenye kera igisobanuro k’izina rye. Yasubije mu magambo akurikira: icyo
kibazo ni cyo mwarimu yambajije nkitangira mu mashuri y’inshuke. Nzi ko nitwa
Ikimanimpaye, ureke urwo Luciga mwantwereye! Masenge Nyiramatwi yanambwiye
ko aho batuye ku Rusumo, ku mbibi z’u Rwanda na Tanzaniya mu burasirazuba,
bavuga Ikinyambo; twe tuvuga Oluciga atwita Abakiga bavangiye kuko tutahavuka.
Muzamusabe kwirinda kuvangura abantu, ibyo bizanshimisha cyane!

Ibibazo
Izi ndimi ko atari Ikinyarwanda nk’uko dusanzwe tukizi, zaba zifitanye iyihe sano
na cyo? Sobanura igisubizo utanze.
Kora ubushakatsatsi bwimbitse ku ndimi zifitanye isano n’Ikinyarwanda ugaragaza
aho zivugwa.

Inshoza y’ururimi shami
Uretse Ikinyarwanda, hari izindi ndimi nyafurika zishamikiye kuri uru rurimi
rw’Ikinyarwanda. Izi ndimi zishamikiye ku Kinyarwanda usanga ahanini ziganje ku
nkiko z’Igihugu cyacu. Bene izi ndimi usanga zifite isano ya bugufi n’Ikinyarwanda.
Zimwe muri izo ndimi shami z’Ikinyarwanda ni izi zikurikira:
a) Oluciga: Mu Rwanda Oluciga ruvugwa n’abantu benshi ariko by’umwihariko
ruvugwa cyanecyane mu duce duherereye mu majyaruguru y’u Rwanda ahahoze
ari Komini Kiyombe, Muvumba, Cyumba, Kivuye, Mukarange na Butaro. Ubu ni mu

184

Karere ka Gicumbi na Burera. Uru rurimi kandi ruvugwa mu gihugu cy’abaturanyi,
cy’u Bugande.
b) Amashi n’Igihavu cyangwa Amahavu: Abazi izi ndimi bavuga ko zijya gusa
cyane. Izi ndimi zivugwa mu burengerazuba bw’u Rwanda ku mupaka wa
Repubulika Iharanira Demukarasi ya Kongo n’u Rwanda. Igihavu kivugwa ku
kirwa cya Nkombo no ku nkombe z’ikiyaga cya Kivu ahahoze ari muri komini
Gisuma, Gafunzo na Kagano. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rusizi na Nyamasheke. Amashi
yo avugwa mu gice cyo hepfo y’ikiyaga cya Kivu. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Rusizi.
c) Ururashi: Ururashi bamwe bita Urunyambo ruvugwa mu burasirazuba bw’u
Rwanda mu bice bimwe na bimwe by’ahahoze ari Komini Rusumo na Rukira ku
mupaka w’u Rwanda na Tanzaniya. Ubu ni mu Karere ka Ngoma.

Ikitonderwa
N’ubwo abatuye aho hantu bavuga izo ndimi batandukaniraho n’abandi, bafite
ururimi rusange rubahuza n’abandi ari rwo rw’Ikinyarwanda uzasanga rukoreshwa
mu masoko bahaha cyangwa se mu nama zitandukanye no mu biganiro byahuje
abantu.
Aho zimwe mu nshami z’Ikinyarwanda zivugwa mu Rwanda

Amashi

Igihavu
Ururashi
Oluciga
Ikinyarwanda

185

Umwitozo
1. Umaze kwiga no gucengera imiterere y’indimi mu Rwanda, andika inshamake
irebana n’iyo miterere y’indimi.
2. Ushingiye ku miterere y’indimi mu Rwanda, vuga ibice by’u Rwanda bivugwamo
indimi cyangwa imvugo zikurikira: Oluciga, Ururashi, Ikirera n’Amashi.

Inshamake y’ibyizwe mu mutwe wa kenda
–

–
–
–

Amazi n’amashyamba bidufatiye runini. Amashyamba akurura imvura
akayungurura umwuka duhumeka, avamo ibikoresho bitandukanye, hari
ibiti bivamo imiti ituvura. Ikindi ni uko amashyamba acumbikiye inyamaswa
n’utundi dukoko dutandukanye. Amazi atanga ingufu, acumbikira inyamaswa
ziba mu mazi, turayoga, aratekeshwa, aranyobwa, muri make amazi ni
ubuzima.
Abanyarwanda batavuga kimwe ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda. Buri karere
kagenda kagira umwihariko wako w’imivugire, haba mu ijwi, mu masaku, mu
magambo mu buryo bwo kurema interuro n’ibindi. Kubera iyo mpamvu, mu
Rwanda hari indimi zishamikiye ku Kinyarwanda zikoreshwa n’Abanyarwanda.
Mu Rwanda indimi zikoreshwa mu butegetsi ni Ikinyarwanda, Icyongereza,
Igifaransa, n’igiswayire. Indimi z’uturere mu Rwanda ni Ikigoyi, Ikirera, Igikiga,
Igisozo, Ikigoti, Igishobyo, Ikiyaka, Urunyagisaka n’Urunyambo.
Inshami z’Ikinyarwanda ni Oluciga, Amashi, n’Ururashi.

Isuzuma risoza umutwe wa kenda

Kora ku giti cyawe, usome uyu mwandiko maze usubize ibibazo bikurikira
wubahiriza amabwiriza yatanzwe.

Umwandiko: Akamaro k’ibidukikije
Iyo bavuze ibidukikije, abantu benshi ntibasobanukirwa neza n’icyo ibidukikije
bivuze. Ikirere n’isi bifite inkiko zizwi n’abahanga cyangwa abandi bajijutse,
bemeza ko urusobe rw’ ibinyabuzima n’ibitabufite byose biri muri izo mbibi
byitwa ibidukikije. Ikirere kiri hejuru yacu uwakivogera uko yishakiye yaba anyuze
iruhande rw’amategeko agenga ubusugire bw’igihugu gitunze icyo kirere. Mbere
y’uko hakurikizwa amategeko mashya, mu bushorishori bw’ibiti hitwaga mu gihugu
k’inyoni naho ijuru cyangwa ikirere gitambagiramo inyoni zitandukanye, hakaba mu
bwami bw’inkuba. Isi bagereranyaga gusa n’ahaba ubutaka buhingwa, ahashinze imizi
y’ibimera, ahadudubiza udusoko tw’amazi, ahatemba imigezi n’inzuzi, ahari ibiyaga
binini n’ibito, ahatuye abantu, inyamaswa n’udukoko hakitwa Igihugu cy’umwami
Nyirurwanda.

Ibidukikije bidufitiye akamaro kanini kandi kanyuranye. Iyo duhereye ku bimera byo
mu gasozi, abashakashatsi bemeza ko imiti myinshi ivomwa mu bimera. Amaradiyo
anyuranye ajya ahitisha ibiganiro ku buvuzi bwo gukoresha ibyatsi bitandukanye.

186

Nuca ku muravumba, ku ndabo z’umuko, ku gihondohondo, ku gikakarubamba,
akanyamapfundo... uzibuke gusobanuza akamaro kabyo kuko burya ngo utazi
nyakatsi arayinera. Ibiribwa byinshi tubikomora mu butaka. Ibijumba, amateke,
ibikoro tubisanga mu bidukikije. Imboga zimwe zerera mu butaka harimo karoti,
ibitunguru... mwari muzi ko ari ibidukikije? Nyaruka uterere akajisho kuri dodo, isogi,
imbwija, amashu, ibihaza na kayote, wari uzi ko turwanya indwara tubikomoye kuri
izi mboga? Abatazi indarama cyangwa nyiragasogereza, nibasobanuza bazabwirwa
ko ari ubwoko bw’imboga zimera mu bishanga zikaribwa ku bw’amage nubwo zirura
kurusha isogo, ariko abazibonye ntizibagwa nabi. Ibiti bitanga imbuto ziribwa harimo
avoka, amapera, amacunga, indimu n’ubundi bwoko bw’imbuto, biduha umutobe
ugwa neza ubuzima bwacu.
Bantu mutita ku bidukikije, muzatuma isi yacu ikomeza guhura n’akaga k’amapfa.
Dufashe urugero rw’amashyamba n’amazi; usanga abantu benshi bibera ba
tereriyo mu kubungabunga ibi bidukikije by’amazi n’amashyamba. Bantu bangiza
amashyamba, mwari muzi ko ayungurura umwuka duhumeka, ko akurura imvura
imeza kandi igakuza ibihingwa twahoze tuvuga?

Uretse uwo mumaro, amashyamba acumbikiye inyamaswa nyinshi. Umunsi umwe
uzanyarukire mu ishyamba rya Pariki y’Akagera wihere ijisho aho impara n’imparage,
amashyo y’inzovu, imbogo Rwarikamavubi n’intare Rwabwiga, ingwe ari yo Rwara
isha n’ifumberi... uzavayo unezerewe. Ingagi zo mu birunga zitwinjiriza amadovize,
ukeka ko zidatwikiriwe n’ishyamba ugira ngo zahaba? Ishyamba rya Nyungwe
rigaragaramo inyoni zitandukanye zituma ba mukerarugendo badusura bakadusigira
akayabo k’amadovize. Amashyamba kimeza nka Mukura na Cyamudongo afite byinshi
afashamo abayaturiye hamwe n’Igihugu muri rusange. Amashyamba atanga imbaho
zikorwamo ibikoresho bitandukanye. Ababaza imizonobari, za ribuyu, imisave, sipure
n’ibindi ni bo bakubwira ibanga ry’ishyamba. Ishyamba rifite akamaro cyane; gutinda
ibiraro aho bishoboka, gucibwamo amakara, mu bwubatsi bukenera ibikwa n’imbaho.
Ishyamba ritariho twagobokwa n’iki? Akamaro k’amashyamba ni intarondoreka;
twibuke ariko ko niba utemye kimwe uge utera bibiri.
Ibintu ni magirirane. Isi yacu ibuze amashyamba yahinduka ubutayu nka Karahari,
ubutayu bwo muri Namibiya cyangwa Sahara. Ishyamba ritariho imvura yabura,
ikitwa ikinyabuzima kikazimira ku butaka bwabaye ubutayu. Inyamaswa zabura icyo
zirya, zakwicwa n’umwuma, ikitwa ikimera kikaba akamizwe n’ingwe muri iyi si ya
Rurema. Amafi, indagara n’utundi dusimba dusanzwe dutorera mu mazi dutunga
abantu, twazimirana n’amapfa yaba yateye igihugu n’isi. Mutekereze amazi akamye!
Ingomero zitanga ingufu z’amashanyarazi zubatse ku migezi, ku nzuzi n’ibiyaga
zahagarara umwijima ugacura. Mu yandi magambo “nta mazi nta buzima”.

Mucyo twumve ko ibidukikije tugomba kubibungabunga uko dushoboye kuko
nitutabyitaho isi yacu tuzayisenya vuba, tuzayigeza ku mperuka imburagihe bitewe
no kutumva inama tugirwa n’inzobere mu by’ibidukikije. Ba rutwitsi bagikongeza
amashyamba agashya ururimbi agakongoka, bibuke ko bameze nka wa muntu utema

187

ishami ry’igiti aryicayeho. Ararimbura imbaga y’abantu atisize. Ntaho ataniye na ba
bahigi na barushimusi batumaze ku nyamaswa. Ntimugahweme kubamagana, abo
bantu baraducukurira urwobo! Imyotsi ubwayo ihumanya ikirere, kandi ikirere
ni indiri y’umwuka duhumeka. Inkongi y’umuriro ubwayo yica kandi ikirukana
inyamaswa z’ishyamba. Iyo ikirere cyahumanye, amazi yose y’ibiyaga, ay’imigezi,
ay’inzuzi, n’ayo tuvoma arahumana ubuzima bwacu bugakendera. Ibinyabuzima byo
mu mazi na byo bihura n’akaga, kororoka bikanga ndetse bikaba byacika burundu.
Ibidukikije tubibungabunge, twite ku mashyamba, ahari tuyasazure, ahari
amanegeka haterwe ibiti. Ku migezi, ku nzuzi n’ibiyaga igihe duhinga bugufi, twirinde
gutenguriramo itaka, umwanya usabwa wo gusiga ku nkengero zabyo byubahirizwe.
Ikindi kandi imigezi, inzuzi n’ibiyaga bigaragaramo icyatsi bita amarebe avanwemo
kuko byangiza cyangwa se byonona cyane cyane amafi. Ababyiruka mu bana, abasore,
inkumi, abakecuru n’abasaza muze twite ku bidukikije bizatuma turamba ubugira
kenshi.

I. Ibibazo byo kumva no gusesengura umwandiko

		
1. Kera abantu bafataga isi dutuye bakayigabanyagamo inturo cyangwa ibice,
abantu, inyamaswa n’inyoni bibarizwaho. Bivuge usobanura.
		2. Vuga akamaro k’ibimera muri rusange.
		3. Mu bidukikije harimo ibintu bibiri birusha ibindi ingufu. Bivuge.
		
4. Amashyamba akomeye avugwa mu mwandiko ni ayahe?
		
5. Ibimera bibuze twaba mu butayu. Vuga ubutayu bwatanzweho ingero mu
mwandiko.
		6. Rondora ingaruka zaterwa no kubura amazi.
		7. Abantu tugomba kwamagana bangiza amashyamba n’inyamaswa ni ba
nde?
		8. Ingingo nyamukuru n’izungirije zavuzweho mu mwandiko zigaragaze.

10. Ni izihe ngamba zafatwa kugira ngo hakomeze kubungabungwa ibidukikije?

III. Iyigandimi

Tandukanya ururimi rw’Ikinyarwanda, indimi shami n’indimi z’uturere utanga
n’urugero.
