10

Ibiyobyabwenge

Umwandiko: Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge, imungu y’ubuzima
bwacu

Ijambo kuyoba risobanura guta umurongo wagenderagamo cyangwa se guta
umuyoboro ukuganisha aho werekeza. Kuyoba ubwenge rero bigasobanura
guta umurongo w’ibitekerezo bizima ugakora ibitanye n’indangagaciro z’umuco
bigatuma utagaragara nk’uko wagombaga kugaragara mu muryango ubarizwamo.
Ibiyobyabwenge ni ibinyobwa, ibiribwa cyangwa se imiti n’ibihumekwa bishobora
gutuma umuntu atakaza ubushobozi mu mitekerereze no mu myitwarire bikamutesha
ubumuntu ndetse n’icyo yari ari cyo. Mu yandi magambo ikiyobyabwenge ni ikintu

190

cyose iyo kinjiye mu mubiri w’umuntu gihindura ubushobozi bwe bwo gutekereza,
gufata ibyemezo ndetse kikanahindura imikorere isanzwe y’umubiri.

Nubwo ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge rigaragara cyane mu ngeri nyinshi
z’abantu batandukanye, urubyiruko ni rwo rukunze kugaragara cyane mu
ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge. Abantu bakaba bakunze kwishora mu ikoreshwa
ry’ibiyobyabwenge bitwaje impamvu zinyuranye; gushaka akanyamuneza
n’akanyabugabo, gusabana n’urungano, gushakisha ibitotsi, gutuma umuntu atinyuka
akagira ingufu n’ubushyuhe mu mubiri.

Bimwe mu biyobyabwenge bikunze gukoreshwa mu Rwanda twavuga, birimo
inzoga zisindisha nka kanyanga, muriture, bareteta, itabi ry’urumwogi, kole,
kokayine, marijuwana, lisansi n’ibindi. Kubera ububi bwabyo, abakoresha bene
ibyo biyobyabwenge babikora rwihishwa ari na ko babiha utuzina tw’utugenurano
bihariye kugira ngo ababirwanya badapfa kubivumbura. Urugero hari abita kanyanga
mituweri (mituelle) abandi bakayita “wangu” ijambo ry’Igiswayiri risobanura umuntu
wawe.

Ingaruka zo gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge zigaragara mu ngeri zitandukanye uhereye ku
babinywa, ku muryango babamo, ku gihugu ndetse no ku isi muri rusange. Ku banywa
ibiyobyabwenge, ingaruka zihita zigaragaza kuko ubifata ata ubwenge n’umurongo
w’ibitekerezo bitewe n’uko ahanini ibiyobyabwenge byirukankira mu bwonko ako
kanya bityo imitekerereze n’imyitwarire bigahita bihinduka. Ngibyo ibituma abanywi
b’ibiyobyabwenge bakora ibidakorwa birimo urugomo, ubujura, guhohotera abo
bahuye na bo bose, ubwicanyi bwa hato na hato ndetse n’amakimbirane adashira
bikiyongera. Ibyo byose biterwa n’uko uwafashe ibiyobyabwenge nta muntu n’umwe
yubaha cyangwa ngo ahe agaciro. Rimwe na rimwe ukoresha ibiyobyabwenge, kubera
kutagira uwo atinya n’icyo atinya, ashobora kwishora mu ngeso z’ubusambanyi
atiteguye bityo bikaba byamuviramo kwandura indwara z’ibyorezo nka SIDA. Akenshi
akora imibonano mpuzabitsina idakingiye akayikorana n’abo atazi kandi banyuranye.

Ku rundi ruhande, hari bimwe mu biyobyabwenge biterwa mu mubiri hakoreshejwe
inshinge. Benshi mu babikoresha bakunze kubikoresha bakoresheje urushinge
rumwe bityo na bo bakaba bahandurira indwara z’ibyorezo zirimo na SIDA cyangwa
izindi ndwara zandurira mu itembera ry’amaraso. Nubwo abenshi mu bakoresha
ibiyobyabwenge babikora bagamije kwishimisha, ikoreshwa ryabyo ku buryo
bw’indengakamere rishobora gutuma ubuzima bw’umuntu bwangirika. Uko urugero
rw’ibiyobyabwenge rugenda rwiyongera mu maraso ni ko umubiri ugenda uhinduka
umuntu akaba yagwa muri koma cyangwa akahatakariza ubuzima.
Aha kandi ntitwakwirengagiza ko hari abakoresha ibiyobyabwenge bikabagira
imbata zabyo ku buryo kubyikura bitaborohera kuko baba barabigize akamenyero.
Ikiranga uwo ibiyobyabwenge byagize imbata ni uko akenera kongera ingano
y’ikiyobyabwenge kugira ngo yumve amerewe neza kurushaho. Atakaza ishyaka
ryo gukora ibindi bintu bitari ikoresha ry’ikiyobyabwenge, mbese agakoresha igihe

191

kinini cy’umwanya we wa buri munsi ku ikoreshwa ry’ikiyobyabwenge. Akomeza
gukoresha ikiyobyabwenge n’igihe azi neza ingorane cyamuteje n’ingaruka ashobora
guhura na zo. Iyo bene abo bantu batakoresheje ibiyobyabwenge bakunze kurangwa
no kugira ishavu, imihangayiko, gucika intege, kwiheba, kwiheza n’ibindi.

Abakomoka ku bakoresha ibiyobyabwenge bahura n’ingaruka zitandukanye. Rimwe
na rimwe na nyuma y’ivuka iyo ababyeyi bakomeje kwibera imbata y’ibiyobyabwenge
umwana ashobora kugaragaza ihungabana cyangwa guhorana imihangayiko kubera
uburyo umuryango ubayeho n’ibindi. Ikindi kandi abana bakoresha ibiyobyabwenge
byagaragaye ko badakura neza. Ibiyobyabwenge kandi bigira ingaruka ku rwungano
rw’imyakura y’ubwonko. Bishobora no kugira ingaruka ku zindi nzungano, ku
mikurire, ku bwenge, bikaba byaviramo abana kuba bananirwa kwiga gusoma no
kwandika, kuvuga neza n’ibindi.
Hejuru y’ibyo ntawabura kuvuga ku ndwara zinyuranye zikomoka ku ikoreshwa
ry’ibiyobyabwenge. Mu ndwara z’umubiri twavuga nk’uburwayi bw’umwijima kubera
gukora cyane uhindura ibyo biyobyabwenge, ubw’umutima, ubwonko n’imyakura
ndetse n’ubumuga bushobora kubikomokaho, uburwayi bw’urwungano ngogozi,
indwara z’ubuhumekero, iz’ibihaha ku banywi b’itabi, kanseri, gutakaza ubushobozi
bw’umubiri bwo kurwanya indwara, SIDA, n’ibindi.

Henshi ku isi usanga barwanya ibiyobyabwenge kubera ko ahanini bibangamira
iterambere ry’ibihugu. Inyungu zikomoka ku biyobyabwenge usanga zikiza
bamwe bakunze no kwigwizaho imitungo kandi mu by’ukuri ibiyobyabwenge
bibangamira ubuzima bw’abatuye isi. Tuzi neza ko abantu ari umutungo ibihugu
bifite ugomba kubungwabungwa ku ikubitiro. Ntitwakwirengagiza ko abanywi
b’ibiyobyabwenge batakaza amafaranga menshi bayasesagura bikaviramo imiryango
yabo ubukene bw’akarande. Mu rwego rw’ubukungu kandi ibihugu bikoreshwamo
cyane ibiyobyabwenge bisabwa gushora amafaranga menshi mu miti yo kuvura
indwara zikomoka ku ngaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge aho kuyashora mu bindi bikorwa
by’iterambere bitewe no kurwaragurika kw’abanywi babyo n’ababakomokaho.
Ikindi kigaragara ni uko abanywi b’ibiyobyabwenge bagaragaraho kwica akazi bitewe
n’uko bibatesha umutwe bikabangiriza n’ubuzima bibatera gusaza imburagihe. Ibi
bikaba byongera umubare w’imburamukoro ndetse bikanahungabanya iterambere
ry’Igihugu. Bigira kandi ingaruka ku mibanire n’abandi ku murimo, n’ejo hazaza
h’umuntu. Muri izo twavuga: guhorana imyenda, impagarara n’amahane mu
muryango, impushya za hato na hato ndetse no gusiba akazi, kwirukanwa mu mashuri
cyangwa ku kazi, ubukene n’ibindi.

Ku bijyanye n’umuco, kunywa ibiyobyabwenge bihagarika iterambere rishingiye
ku muco. Igihugu kandi ntigishobora gutera imbere iyo kirimo umutekano muke.
Ibiyobyabwenge bishobora kuba intandaro yo gusenyuka kw’ingo kandi ari zo
zakagombye kuba ishingiro ry’iterambere ry’umuco. Ibiyobyabwenge kandi ni

192

intandaro y’ibikorwa bibi birimo ubujura, urugomo, kwigomeka n’ibindi. Ibi byose
bikaba bibangamira iterambere.

Muri make ibiyobyabwenge nta na hamwe biganisha mu murongo uboneye. Umunywi
wabyo atinyuka gukora ibibi atashobora gukora igihe atabikoresheje. Imikoreshereze
yabyo yangiza ubuzima bw’ababikoresha ndetse bikagira ingaruka ku muryango
no ku iterambere ry’Igihugu muri rusange. Ni yo mpamvu gukumira ikoreshwa
ry’ibiyobyabwenge ari inshingano ya buri wese: urubyiruko, ababyeyi, abarezi,
inzego z’umutekano, abajyanama b’ubuzima ndetse n’abaturarwanda bose muri
rusange. By’umwihariko, kubera impungenge baterwa n’ibiyobyabwenge bishobora
no kwadukira amashuri yisumbuye, ababyeyi ntibahwema gushishikariza abana
babo kwamagana ababashora mu biyobyabwenge binyuranye ndetse no kubibutsa
ingaruka zabyo ku buzima bwabo bw’ejo hazaza. Rimwe na rimwe ababyeyi bandikira
abana babo amabaruwa babasaba kwirinda ibiyobyabwenge. Gufasha uwahuye
n’ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge ndetse n’ingaruka zaryo birahenda kandi bisaba
ubumenyi n’imbaraga nyinshi. Birumvikana rero ko kurwanya no gukumira ikoreshwa
ry’ibiyobyabwenge ndetse no guhangana n’ingaruka zabyo biri mu nshingano za buri
wese. Ni ngombwa rero kwigisha abakoresha ibiyobyabwenge kugira ngo bahindure
imyitwarire yabo bagaruke mu murongo uboneye, babone imbaraga zo gukorera
imiryango yabo n’Igihugu kugira ngo twese dusenyere umugozi umwe twubake isi
yacu.

I. Inyunguramagambo

A. Sobanura amagambo akurikira:
1. Indangagaciro		6. Urwungano rw’imyakura
2. Ubumuntu		7. Impagarara
3. Akanyabugabo		8. Intandaro
4. Muriture		9. Kwadukira
5. Bareteta
10. Gusenyera umugozi umwe

B. Kora iyi myitozo ku nyunguramagambo
1. Simbuza ijambo cyangwa itsinda ry’amagambo yanditse atsindagiye irindi/
andi magambo bivuga kimwe usanga mu mwandiko.
a) Ibiyobyabwenge birimo ibice byinshi: ibiterwa umuntu mu rushinge,
ibinyobwa nk’itabi, hari ibisukika n’ibindi.
b) Abantu bari mu kigero kimwe bagomba gukorera hamwe mu bwumvikane
n’amahoro.

193

c) Itsinda ry’abantu badatunganye ntiwakagombye kubishinga kuko
bakuyobya.
d) Uwaheranywe n’ibitotsi ubuticura iyo atitaweho ashobora kugendera ko
agashiramo umwuka.
2. Uzuza interuro zikurikira ukoresheje ijambo rikwiye ukuye mu mwandiko.
a) Umuntu wabaye ................... y’ibiyobyabwenge usanga yarataye umutwe.
b) Umubiri w’umunywi w’ibiyobyabwenge utakaza ................... ryo kunoza
umurimo.
c) Isaho umwana yiberamo igihe nyina amutwise muyita ...................
d) Abasaritswe n’ibiyobyabwenge baba mu ................... butava ku muryango.
e) Abantu b’ ................... bicwa n’inzara kuko baba barimitse ubunebwe
bagashyira inda imbere.

II. Ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko

Subiza ibibazo bikurikira:
1. Vuga impamvu enye abantu bitwaza bakishora mu biyobyabwenge?
2. Tanga ingero z’ibiyobyabwenge bikunze gukoreshwa mu gace ukomokamo.
3. Garagaza ibikorwa binyuranye abanywi b’ibiyobyabwenge bashobora
kwishoramo igihe bamaze kubikoresha.
4. Ni izihe ndwara zishobora gukomoka ku ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge?
5. Ni gute gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge bishobora kuba intandaro yo kwandura
SIDA? Sobanura ukoresheje ingero ebyiri.
6. Ni ryari ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge rishobora kuganisha ku rupfu?
7. Ni ibihe bimenyetso biranga uwagizwe imbata n’ibiyobyabwenge?
8. Usanzwe akoresha ibiyobyabwenge arangwa n’iki igihe yabibuze?
9. Ibiyobyabwenge bibangamira iterambere ry’igihugu kigaragaramo ababikoresha
cyane. Sobanura uburyo bibangamira iterembere wifashishije ingero ebyiri.
10. Ugendeye ku mwandiko, sobanura impamvu tugomba kurwanya ibiyobyabwenge.
11. Uyu mwandiko ni iki ushishikariza ababyeyi bafite abana biga mu mashuri
yisumbuye?
12. Ushingiye ku bivugwa muri uyu mwandiko, ni iyihe nama wagira bagenzi bawe?
Kubera iki?

194

III. Ibibazo byo gusesengura umwandiko

Tekereza ku buryo bwimbitse maze usubize ibibazo bikurikira:
1. Ni iyihe nsanganyamatsiko rusange ivugwa muri uyu mwandiko?
2. Garagaza ingingo z’ingenzi zivugwa muri uyu mwandiko.
3. Ibiyobyabwenge birangiza. Abaporisi n’abandi bashinzwe umutekano bashoboye
bonyine kubirwanya no kubikumira? Gerageza kubisobanura neza.
4. Abantu batari bake usanga bafata ku gahiye ndetse bagasingira ka Wisiki n’izindi
nzoga zikomeye, hari aho bihuriye no kunywa ibiyobyabwenge byarondowe mu
mwandiko?
5. Mu mashuri yisumbuye, mu mashuri makuru na za Kaminuza usanga hari
ubwisanzure bukabije ku buryo kugenzura ko banywa ibiyobyabwenge
bitakorohera uwo ari we wese. Abayobozi b’ayo mashuri wabifuzaho iki?

Umwitozo w’ubumenyi ngiro

Ifatanye na bagenzi bawe mwungurane ibitekerezo mujya impaka zubaka
kuri iyi nsanganyamatsiko:
Uruhare rw’ibiyobyabwenge mu kudindiza iterambere n’ingamba zo kubyirinda.

195

Inyandiko z’ubutegetsi: Ibaruwa n’umwirondoro

Itegereze iyi nteruro maze ukore ubushakashatsi usubize ibibazo biri hasi.
By’umwihariko, kubera impungenge baterwa n’ibiyobyabwenge bishobora no
kwadukira amashuri yisumbuye. Ababyeyi ntibahwema gushishikariza abana
babo kwamagana ababashora mu biyobyabwenge binyuranye ndetse no kubibutsa
ingaruka zabyo ku buzima bwabo bw’ejo hazaza. Rimwe na rimwe ababyeyi
bandikira abana babo amabaruwa babasaba kwirinda ibiyobyabwenge.

Ibibazo:
1. Ibaruwa ivugwa muri iyi nteruro ni bwoko ki?
2. Usibye bene ubwo bwoko bw’ibaruwa nta zindi baruwa waba uzi zandikwa?
Tanga ingero zitandukanye.
3. Mu matangazo amenyesha akazi ni iki bakunze gusaba?
4. Utekereza ko umwirondoro uba ukubiyemo iki?

MUGISHA Arnaud

Umuyobozi w’Akarere ka Buramba
Ibibazo ku mwandiko
1. Uyu mwandiko uri mu buhe bwoko bw’imyandiko? Kubera iki?
2. Ni nde wanditse uyu mwandiko? Yawandikiye nde?
3. Ni ikihe kibazo cyatumye nyiri ukwandika uyu mwandiko awandika?
4. Garagaza itandukaniro riri hagati y’uyu mwandiko n’indi wize.

197

1. Inshoza y’ibaruwa y‘ubutegetsi
Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi ni ibaruwa ngufi kandi ivuga ibya ngombwa birasa ku
ntego, ikirinda uburondogozi no kugaragaza amarangamutima. Igituma iba ngufi
ni uko uwandika agomba gusa kwibanda ku mpamvu yatumye yandika. Bene iyi
baruwa y’ubutegetsi iba igamije gusaba akazi, gusubiza uwasabye akazi, gusaba
ibisobanuro mu kazi, gutanga ibisobanuro, gutanga amabwiriza n’ibindi. Tuvuge
niba uwandika asaba akazi, iyo ni yo mpamvu agaragaza ku rupapuro kandi
ni yo avugaho muri make agaragaza akazi ashaka ako ari ko, ubushobozi afite
bwo kugakora n’uburyo azagakora, kandi byose bikagirwa mu kinyabupfura.
Nta gutandukira ngo agaragaze ko yababaye, ko ubukene bumumereye nabi, ko
arya rimwe mu cyumweru, ko yari afite akazi bakakamwirukanaho ku maherere
n’ibindi. Uwo wandikira usaba akazi ntakeneye ko umurondogoraho cyangwa
ko umutera imbabazi. Ibyo nta mwanya abifitiye, icyo akeneye ni icyo ugamije
kumukorera, ubushobozi ugifitemo, ibyangombwa bibigaragaza. Bene iyi baruwa
yandikwa ku rupapuro rw’umweru bita A4, rukandikwaho ruhagaze, uwandika
agahera ibumoso agana iburyo.
2. Imiterere y’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi
Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi igizwe n’ibice binyuranye:

Aderesi: Ni igice k’ingenzi kigaragaza uwanditse ibaruwa. Hagaragaramo
amazina ye, aho atuye ndetse n’andi makuru yose yafasha uwo yandikiye
kumenya aho yamubariza aramutse amushatse: ashobora kongeramo nimero za
terefoni n’aderesi ye ya interineti. Iki gice gifata umwanya wo hejuru ibumoso
ku rupapuro.
Itariki: Ni ngombwa ko hagaragaramo ahantu ibaruwa yandikiwe n’umunsi iyo
baruwa yandikiweho. Iki gice cyo kijya hejuru iburyo ahateganye n’izina.

Uwandikiwe: Iki gice kigaragara munsi y’itariki ibaruwa yandikiweho. Kiba
kigaragaza uwo ibaruwa igenewe. Si izina rye bwite rigaragaramo ahubwo ni
izina rigaragaza umwanya afite mu kazi. Cyakora hashobora no kugaragazwa
izina iyo ibaruwa igenewe umukozi runaka.
Impamvu: Mu ibaruwa nk’iyo hagomba kugaragaramo impamvu yayo: gusaba
akazi, gusaba ibisobanuro... Ijambo impamvu buri gihe ricibwaho akarongo. Iki
gice kiba kiri munsi ya aderesi kikabangikana n’umurongo wa nyuma wo mu
gice kigaragaza uwo ibaruwa yandikiwe.
Igihimba: Igihimba ni ibaruwa nyirizina. Igihimba k’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi
kigirwa n’ibika bitatu:

a) Intangiriro: Uwandika avuga muri make impamvu imuteye kwandika igirwa
n’igika kimwe kandi ikagaragaza icyo uwandika agamije. Iyo ari nk’ibaruwa isaba
akazi agaragazamo ko azi neza ko uwo mwanya uhari yongeramo aho yabikuye;
mu kinyamakuru cyangwa mu itangazo ryaciye kuri radiyo.

198

b) Igihimba: Uwandika asobanura atarondogoye ingingo z’ingenzi zigize impamvu
yo kwandika. Ni igice kigaragara nk’aho ari kirekire kurusha ibindi, kuko
gishobora no kugira ibika birenze kimwe bitewe n’ingingo zigize ubutumwa. Ni
cyo gice cyonyine gisobanura mu mugambo arambuye ibyavuzwe mu ntangiriro,
kikabisesengura, kikanakurikiranya ibitekerezo. Icyo gihe buri gika kiharira
ingingo yacyo, na none ukirinda gusubiramo ibyo wavuze.
c) Umusozo: Uwandika ibaruwa asoza ashimira uwo yandikiye. Ni cyo gice kirangiza
ibaruwa kandi kigirwa n’igika kimwe. Uwandika arangiza yerekana ikizere afitiye
uwo yandikiye cyangwa se icyubahiro amugomba.
d) Amazina n’umukono: Ni igice gisoza ibaruwa kigizwe n’amazina ndetse
n’umukono wa nyiri ukuyandika.
Ikitonderwa: Bitewe n’imiterere yayo, ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi ishobora kugira ibindi
bice bikurikira:

Binyujijwe: Ni igice kigaragara mu ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi munsi y’aderesi
y’uwandikiwe. Gishyirwaho iyo hari abo iyo baruwa igomba gucaho mbere yo
kohererezwa uwayandikiwe.
Bimenyeshejwe: ni igice kijya mu mpera z’ibaruwa ku ruhande w’ibumoso. Kijya
mu ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi iba igomba kugira abandi bamenyeshwa ibyanditswe.

199

3. Imbata y’ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi

Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi ntawusuhuza cyangwa ngo asezere. Hari amagambo
yabugenewe asoza ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi (Mu gihe ngitegereje igisubizo
cyanyu kiza, nyakubahwa, mbaye mbashimiye/ Mu gihe ntegerezanyije
ikizere, mbaye mbashimiye/ Mbaye mbashimiye Nyakubahwa/ Bwana /
Madamu/ Madamazera…)
Buri gika gitangirira mu cya kabiri cy’urupapuro mu mpagarike yarwo.
Ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi igomba kugira impamvu yayo yihariye bitewe
n’igitumye uyandika yandika.

Umukoro

Andikira umuyobozi w’umurenge utuyemo umusaba ikemezo cy’amavuko kuko
ugikeneye mu kuzuza ifishi izaguhesha uburenganzira bwo gukora ikizamini cya
Leta gisoza amashuri yisumbuye. Wubahirize imbata y’ibaruwa nk’iyo.

B. Umwirondoro
Hari benshi basabwa gutanga umwirondoro igihe basaba akazi cyangwa se igihe
umukoresha ari we shobuja abigusabye ugatangira kwibaza icyo ugomba gushyira
mu mwirondoro. Ni ngombwa kumenya kwandika umwirondoro, ukamenya ko
ibintu bibi byagaragaye mu mateka yawe utabishyira mu mwirondoro. Ushyiramo
ibyo ubona byakugirira akamaro ukurikije aho uwo mwirondoro ukenewe.
Soma umwandiko ukurikira utahure ibishobora kwibandwaho igihe
handikwa umwirondoro.

Ngewe MUTABAZI Gaspard ndemeza neza ko ibyo maze kuvuga ari ukuri kandi ko

Bikorewe i Kagano, ku wa 25 Nyakanga 2017

MUTABAZI Gaspard

Ibibazo
1) Uyu mwandiko ugaragaramo ayahe makuru?
2) Urabona ufite akahe kamaro?
3) Ni nka nde wakenera bene uyu mwandiko?

A. Inshoza y’umwirondoro
Mu buzima busanzwe ari na byo bimenyerewe cyane, umwirondoro ukunze
gusabwa n’umuntu wese ushaka gutanga akazi. Bityo mu byangombwa
yaka ushaka gupiganirwa uwo mwanya haba harimo n’umwirondoro we.
Umwirondoro ni nk’inyandiko yereka umukoresha ishusho y’umukozi akeneye.
Nta wakwirengagiza ko umwirondoro ushobora kuba ngombwa iyo umuntu
asaba ishuri runaka ngo akomerezemo amasomo ye.
B. Ibiranga umwirondoro mwiza
Umwirondoro mwiza ugomba kuba:
– Wanditse ku rupapuro rwiza nta n’amakosa y’ururimi arimo.
– Wuzuye kuko uwusaba akeneye amakuru yuzuye kugira ngo arusheho
kumenya nyiri umwirondoro niba hari ikiburamo ntibizamutere igihe
agishakisha.
– Usomeka neza wanditswe mu nteruro ngu�i.
– Uvuga ukuri. Ukora umwirondoro ntagomba kugira icyo yibagirwa cyangwa
ngo ashyiremo ibidasobanutse cyangwa ibihimbano.
– Ugomba kuba ugenewe koko abo wandikiwe niba ari aho nyirawo asaba
akazi ugomba kuba ujyanye n’aho asaba akazi.
Umwirondoro ntukorwa uko nyirawo yiboneye ugomba kuba ufite uburyo buboneye
ukorwamo, uko ibice biwugize bikurikirana kuko umwanya wabyo uba ufite icyo
usobanura kuri uwo mwirondoro. Ibyo bice rero ni:
– Umutwe
– Ibiranga umuntu
– Amashuri

203

–
–
–
–
–
–

Uburambe
Ubundi bumenyi
Indimi avuga
Ibyo akunda
Abantu bamuzi
Kwemeza ko ari ukuri no gushyiraho umukono we.

1. Umutwe
Umutwe w’umwirondoro wandikwa hejuru ukitwa umwirondoro

2. Ibiranga umuntu
Irangamimerere ni igice gitangira umwirondoro, kikaba kigamije kugaragaza
muri make uwo ari we. Kigomba kuba cyumvikana kandi kirasa ku ntego. Si
ngombwa gushyiramo ibintu byinshi nubwo bwose waba ubona umwirondoro
ari muto.

Mu irangamimerere umuntu avugamo amazina ye. Ni byiza kwandika izina
ry’umuryango mu nyuguti nkuru z’icyapa maze iry’idini rikajya mu nyuguti nto.
Nyuma y’amazina hagaragazwa aho umuntu aherereye, ni ukuvuga aho atuye
(aha iyo afite agasanduku k’iposita ni byiza kugashyiraho). Aho umuntu atuye
hiyongeraho n’uburyo uwamushaka yamubonamo; umurongo wa terefoni na
aderesi ya interineti ku buryo uwabishaka yahita amwandikira.
Ikindi kigomba kujya mu irangamimerere ni imyaka umuntu afite. Aha ariko
ntawandika umubare ibyiza ni ugushyiraho umwaka yavukiye. Iyo yanditse
amatariki, ukwezi akwandika mu izina ryako.

3. Amashuri
Iki gice kigaragaza aho nyiri umwirondoro ahagaze mu rwego rw’ubumenyi ni yo
mpamvu uwandika agomba guhera ku mpamyabumenyi nini afite. Mu kwandika
umwirondoro, amashuri ntatandukana n’impamyabumenyi, umuntu yandika
impamyabumenyi afite bikaba bihwanye no kwandika amashuri yize. Ugaragaza
amashuri yize avuga umwaka, aho yigaga, ibyo yigaga n’impamyabumenyi
yahakuye.
Hari igihe amashuri ajyana n’ibitabo umuntu aba yaranditse. Icyo gihe si
ngombwa kubishyiraho keretse iyo bigira icyo byongera ku kizere umuntu
ashobora kugirirwa n’abo ashyikiriza umwirondoro.

4. Uburambe
Uburambe mu kazi ni igice cyo kwitonderwa. Aha ni ho uwandika umwirondoro
aba agomba kwereka uwo yandikiye icyo azi gukora n’igihe amaze agikora.
Iyo yakoze mu myanya myinshi, ayishyiraho ahereye ku wa nyuma aherukaho
agenda agaragaza igihe yagiye ayimaraho. Hari igihe umuntu aba yarakoze
iyimenyerezamwuga ni ngombwa ko abishyiraho cyanecyane iyo ataramara

204

igihe kinini akora cyangwa se ari bwo bwa mbere yatse akazi. Ibyo bishobora
kumwongerera amahirwe imbere y’uwo aha umwirondoro.

5. Ubundi bumenyi
Kumenya ibintu byinshi nta cyo bitwaye kuko ibyo umuntu azi byose bishobora
kumugirira akamaro mu buzima bwe bwa buri munsi. Iyo rero umuntu azi
ubundi bumenyi ntashidikanya kubigaragaza ku mwirondoro we cyanecyane
iyo bifitanye isano n’akazi asaba. Urugero: Kuba azi mudasobwa, kuba afite
uruhushya rwo gutwara ikinyabiziga...

6. Indimi
Hari igihe umwanya umuntu ashaka uba usaba kumenya indimi z’amahanga. Ni
ngombwa rero ko uwandika umwirondoro ashyiramo indimi zose azi. Mu kazi
ako ari ko kose kumenya indimi z’amahanga byongerera amahirwe ugasaba.
Ukora umwirondoro agaragaza urwego aziho urwo rurimi atabeshya (nduzi
neza cyane, nduzi neza, nduzi bihagije, biciriritse) kuko kubeshya byamugiraho
ingaruka mu gihe k’ikizamini k’ibiganiro.

7. Ibyo akunda
Umuntu ntabaho akora akazi ashinzwe gusa. Na nyuma y’akazi ubuzima
burakomeza. Ibyo umuntu akunda rero biza nyuma y’akazi. Bigizwe n’ibyo
umuntu akora kandi bimushimisha. Ariko na none ukora umwirondoro
ntiyiyibagize ko ibimushimisha bishobora kumubera imbogamizi yo kutabona
umwanya yifuza. Nk’urugero niba ari umuntu ukunda kumva indirimbo kuri
radiyo, bikaba byerekana ko ari umuntu ukunze kuba ari wenyine ko kubana
n’abandi byamugora, mu gihe umuntu ukunda gukina umupira aba agaragaza
ko abana n’abandi neza ko no mu kazi byagenda bityo.
8. Abantu bamuzi cyangwa abahamya
Iyi ngingo y’abantu bazi nyiri umwirondoro si ngombwa buri gihe. Ariko
hari ababisaba mu mwirondoro bikaba ngombwa ko ijyamo. Abantu bakunze
gukenerwa si abaturanyi bawe bakomeye cyangwa se bene wanyu bakomeye.
Abazi umuntu baba bakenewe ni abarimu bamwigishije cyangwa abakoresha
bamukoresheje kuko ukeneye umwirondoro wawe aba ashobora kubabaza ku
bijyanye n’ubumenyi ufite cyangwa se ubushobozi n’imyitwarire byawe mu kazi.
9. Kwemeza ko ibyo uvuze ari ukuri no gushyiraho umukono
Iki ni cyo gice gisoza umwirondoro. Nyiri ukuwandika agomba gusoza yemeza ko
amakuru yatanze ari ukuri ko anashobora kugenzurwa. Hanyuma agashyiraho
itariki n’umukono we.
Ibi ni byo by’ingenzi biba bikubiye mu mwirondoro. Cyakora ntibibujijwe ko hari
andi makuru yakongeramo igihe abona ko hari amahirwe yamwongerera kugira
ngo abone akazi yasabaga.

205

Umukoro
Ubu urangije mu mwaka wa gatandatu w’amashuri yisumbuye. Andika
umwirondoro wawe ukurikije ingingo ziri hejuru aha ufitiye amakuru.

Amatangazo n’ubutumire

Itegereze ishusho ikurikira maze usome umwandiko ukurikiyeho ukore
ubushakashatsi bwimbitse ku matangazo n’ubutumire.

Itangazo
Mu rwego rwo kurwanya ikibazo k’ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge kigaragara mu
Murenge wa Gitaka, ubuyobozi bw’Umurenge wa Gitaka bunejejwe no kumenyesha
abayobozi b’imidugudu bose bo mu Murenge wa Gitaka ko batumiwe mu nama
nyunguranabitekerezo ku buryo bwo kurwanya ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge ku
Cyumweru tariki ya 25/01/2015, saa tatu za mu gitondo (09h00). Kuza kwanyu
ni inkunga ikomeye mu kurwanya ikoreshwa ry’ibiyobyabwenge.
Bikorewe i Gitaka ku wa 20/01/2015
Ubuyobozi bw’Umurenge wa Gitaka.

206

A. Itangazo
1. Inshoza y’itangazo
Igihe cyose ufite icyo ushaka kugeza ku bandi ukimenyesha ugicishije mu itangazo.
Iyo banki zambuwe cyangwa se ibindi bigo biciriritse by’imari bihemukiwe na ba
bihemu bakambura inguzanyo, aba ba bihemu bashyikirizwa inkiko batsindwa
ibyabo bigatezwa cyamunara. Iyo hari imitungo izagurishwa abahesha b’inkiko
b’umwuga batanga amatangazo bahamagarira abaturage kuzaza kwigurira.
Bibaho kandi ko iyo umuryango watakaje umuntu uhitisha itangazo mu
bitangazamakuru na radiyo bahamagarira abantu gutabara umuryango wagize
ibyago. Itangazo ni inyandiko irimo ubutumwa bamanika ahantu cyangwa bugaca
mu kinyamakuru kugira ngo bumenyekane hagamijwe kwamamaza, kurangisha
no kumenyesha.
2. Uturango tw’itangazo
Mu itangazo hagomba kubonekamo ibi bikurikira:
– Umutwe w’itangazo.
– Utanze itangazo.
– Uwo rigenewe.
– Ahantu igikorwa rimenyesha kiri, cyabereye cyangwa kizabera.
– Itariki igikorwa rimenyesha cyabereyeho cyangwa kizabera.

3. Ubwoko bw’amatangazo
Amatangazo yo kubika: Ni amatangazo atabaza agamije kumenyesha abantu
ko hari umuntu witabye Imana akanavuga igihe azashyingurirwa.

Amatangazo yo kumenyesha: Ni amatangazo amenyesha abayumva amakuru
atandukanye nk’inama, akazi, isoko ry’ibintu, cyamunara...

Amatangazo yo kwamamaza: Ni amatangazo atangwa agamije kwamamaza
ibikorwa by’umuntu ku giti ke, by’ishyirahamwe, by’inganda, amashuri, kugira
ngo bimenyekane bibone ababigana mu buryo bwo kubiteza imbere.
Amatangazo yo kurangisha: Ni amatangazo atangwa igihe umuntu yatakaje
ikintu, yabuze umuntu kugira ngo ababimuboneye babimuhe cyangwa yatoye
ibintu kugira ngo nyirabyo abashe kubibona.
Umukoro

Andika amatangazo akurikira wubahiriza imiterere yayo:
- Itangazo ryo kubika.
- Itangazo ryo kumenyesha.

1) Inshoza y’ubutumire
Ubutumire ni inyandiko ngufi itumira umuntu cyangwa abantu kwitabira umunsi
mukuru runaka. Bene izi nyandiko twazigereranya n’amabaruwa y’ubucuti
nubwo zo zidakurikiza imiterere y’ayo mabaruwa. Ubutumire bukoreshwa mu
minsi mikuru inyuranye nko gushyingirwa, kubatirisha, kwizihiza isabukuru
runaka, gutaha igikorwa runaka, gusangira ku meza, kwishimira kugera ku
gikorwa runaka nko gufata impamyabumenyi…

208

2) Uturango tw’ubutumire
Ubutumire burangwa n’imiterere yabwo yo kuba hagaragaramo ibintu by’ingenzi
bikurikira:
– Umutwe w’ubutumire,
– Amazina y’utumira,
– Utumirwa,
– Igikorwa umutumiramo,
– Aho igikorwa kizabera.
– Umunsi n’isaha kizaberaho,
– Aho utumira abarizwa,
Umukoro

Ishyire mu kigwi cy’umuyobozi w’ishuri ryisumbuye wigamo, wandike ubutumire
bw’umuhango wo gutanga impamyabumenyi ku banyeshuri barangiza no kwizihiza
isabukuru y’imyaka 25 ikigo kimaze gishinzwe.

Inshamake y’ibyizwe mu mutwe wa cumi
–
–

–
–
–

Ibiyobyabwenge bigira ingaruka zinyuranye ku muntu ubikoresha, ku
muryango we ndetse no ku iterambere muri rusange. Kurwanya ikoreshwa
ryabyo no gushishikariza abantu kutabikoresha ndetse no kubirwanya ni
inshingano ya buri wese.
Uwandika ibaruwa y’ubutegetsi akoresha amagambo yabugenewe akirinda:
kurondogora, gusuhuza cyangwa gusezera n’andi marangamutima. Ibaruwa
y’ubutegetsi iba ari ngufi kandi ikagira amagambo yabugenewe agomba
kuyitangira no kuyisoza. Urugero rwayitangira: (Bwana Muyobozi/
Nyakubahwa Minisitiri… Urugero rwayisoza: Mu gihe ngitegereje igisubizo
cyanyu kiza, Nyakubahwa, mbaye mbashimiye/ Mu gihe ntegerezanyije
ikizere, mbaye mbashimiye/ Mbaye mbashimiye Nyakubahwa/ Bwana /
Madamu/ Madamazera…)

Umwirondoro ni ibiranga umuntu: amazina y’ababyeyi be, aho atuye, ibyo
yize, ibyo yakoze, ibyo akora, ibyo akunda ndetse n’ibyo azi.

Itangazo ni ubutumwa bugenerwa abantu bufite icyo bubamenyesha cyangwa
bubahamagarira. Muri ubwo butumwa hakaba hagaragaramo ubutanze, uwo
abugeneye, ahantu n’igihe.

Ubutumire ni inyandiko ngufi itumira umuntu cyangwa abantu kwitabira
umunsi mukuru runaka, bukaba burangwa no kugaragaramo amazina
y’utumira, utumirwa, igikorwa cyabaye amutumiramo, aho kizabera, umunsi
n’isaha kizaberaho n’aho utumira abarizwa.

209

Isuzuma risoza umutwe wa cumi
Kora ku giti cyawe, usome uyu mwandiko maze usubize ibibazo bikurikira
wubahiriza amabwiriza yatanzwe.

Umwandiko: Ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge mu rubyiruko
Muvara ni umwana w’umuhungu uri mu kigero k’imyaka cumi n’itandatu. Amaso
yaratukuye, iyo agenda mu nzira agenda yivugisha ahekenya amenyo. Umunsi umwe
twahuriye mu gatsibanzira kitaruye ikigo k’ishuri cya Mabimba atumagura itabi
rizinze mu ikoma ryumye. Yari yambaye impuzankano bigaragara ko ari umunyeshuri.
Ndamwegera, ndamusuhuza maze turatangira turaganira.
-Uraho yewe mwa?
-Ndi aha nyine ntundeba se! Ee! Bite meri wange? Ubu nge mba ndi mu maswingi
wana ntabwo nshaka amagambo menshi! Akimara kunsubiza atyo mpita menya
ko ari umwana wokamwe n’ibiyobyabwenge ndamwegera ntangira kumuganiriza
ntuje. Ambwira ko ari umunyeshuri wiga mu mwaka wa mbere. Ikigero ke
nticyatumaga wakeka ko yiga mu mwaka wa mbere ahubwo wamukekeraga kuba
yararangije amashuri yisumbuye. Ndakomeza ndamuganiriza nihanganira imvugo
nyandagazi yakoreshaga kuko nabonaga na we atari we ahubwo abiterwa no
kubatwa n’ibiyobyabwenge. Ageze aho atangira gucururuka maze aranyemerera
duhuza urugwiro. Icya mbere nifuzaga kumenya ni ibiyobyabwenge urubyiruko
rw’abanyeshuri bakoresha ibyo ari byo, igihe babifatira, aho babikura n’igituma
babifata.
Nyuma y’ikiganiro kirekire nagiranye na we nsanga ibiyobyabwenge bikunze
gukoreshwa n’urubyiruko rw’abanyeshuri ari urumogi, kanyanga n’indi nzoga
ntamenye neza yitaga siriduwire. Ansobanurira ko babifata mu kiruhuko cya
saa sita. Ibyo bituma badasubira ku ishuri nyuma ya saa sita kubera ko ngo baba
babirindutse badashaka kugira amabwiriza ayo ari yo yose bahabwa n’abayobozi
b’ishuri. Ikindi kandi yambwiye ni uko ngo akenshi babifata iyo bari mu biruhuko
bisoza igihembwe cyangwa ibisoza umwaka; basiga babeshye ababyeyi babo ko
bagiye gusobanurirana amasomo ubundi bakigira mu biyobyabwenge. Akimara
kumbwira ibyo byanteye amatsiko yo kumenya aho babikura n’uko babibona kandi
nta mafaranga baba bakorera dore ko bigurwa n’amafaranga menshi. Ansobanurira
ko bayiba ab’iwabo cyangwa bagakoresha amafaranga y’ishuri baba bahawe nyuma
yo kubeshya ababyeyi ko batumwe ibikoresho runaka. Ansobanurira kandi ko aho
babikura ari henshi ko hari bagenzi babo biga bataha baba babicuruza babizana
mu dukapu twabo. Hari n’abaturanyi baba babicuruza bakabigura na bo mu gihe
k’ikiruhuko cya saa yine cyangwa saa sita bakabibagurishiriza ku ruzitiro rw’ishuri
inyuma y’amashuri ahategereye ubuyobozi bw’ishuri.
Ku giti ke, mubaza icyamuteye kunywa ibiyobyabwenge ansobanurira ko bagenzi
be bamubwiraga ko bituma atinyuka, agasubiza mu ishuri ashize amanga kandi ko
ngo binatera akanyabugabo. Nkimara kumva ibisobanuro yampaga nsanga ngomba

210

kumuba hafi nkamufasha kureka ibiyobyabwenge. Muganiriza ntuje mwumvisha
uburyo kuba akiri mu wa mbere kandi abandi bangana bararangije amashuri
yisumbuye ari ukubera kunywa ibiyobyabwenge bigatuma asiba kenshi ishuri, yakora
ibizamini agatsindwa agahora asibira. Musobanurira ko bigira ingaruka ku buzima
bw’ubikoresha nko gutukura amaso, kudatekereza neza, kutagira ikinyabupfura,
kudasinzira iyo atabikoresheje… Ikindi kandi musobanurira uburyo ibiyobyabwenge
bigira ingaruka ku iterambere ry’Igihugu mugaragariza uburyo ubikoresha adakora
kubera kubura imbaraga kandi ko n’amafaranga abigura aba akwiye guteza imbere
igihugu mu bundi buryo. Ariyumvira hashize umwanya aransubiza ati: “None se
nakora iki?” Mubwira ko yabireka kandi akagaragaza n’abandi bagenzi be babifata
ku ishuri ndetse akanavuga uburyo babibona n’aho babikura. Hashira umwanya
munini yiyumvira ageze aho arambwira ati: “Nge ngiye kubireka kandi n’amaniga
yange ndayagira inama abireke. Ni byo bituma ntatsinda mu ishuri kandi bigatuma
mpora mbeshya ababyeyi, mbiba amafaranga! Ahubwo urakoze kuba ungiriye iyi
nama. None se ko nabitangiye bambwira ko nzashira ubwoba nkajya nsubiza neza
mu ishuri none nkaba maze imyaka ine mu wa kabiri bimariye iki? Ndabiretse!
Ahubwo n’utu tubure nari nsigaranye reka ntujugunye ndetse n’aka ka siriduwire
reka nkajugunye. Ubu nange mfashe umugambi wo kugira inama nk’iyi ungiriye
urubyiruko rw’abanyeshuri bangenzi bange ndetse n’urundi rubyiruko duturanye
rutiga, na bo bareke ibiyobyabwenge twiyubakire Igihugu.”
Akimbwira atyo mukora mu ntoki ndamushimira mubwira ko nzajya nza kumusura
kenshi nkamuganiriza. Aranyemerera ansezeraho arataha.

Nyuma y’icyo kiganiro na we, binyereka ko abaturanyi n’ababyeyi ari ngombwa
cyane ko bakurikirana imyigire y’abana babo ku ishuri. Bakamenya igituma batiga
neza kandi bakagenzura niba amafaranga yose abana babo babasaba bavuga ko
bayatumwe ku ishuri biba ari byo koko. Buri mubyeyi ahuze urugwiro n’umwana uwo
ari we wese abona ko afite ikibazo, amuganirize ndetse anamugire inama. Abayobozi
b’ishuri bakurikirane imyigire y’abanyeshuri biga bataha iwabo, bagenzure ibyo
bashobora kuzana ku ishuri, babaze ababyeyi impamvu abana babo bataboneka
buri munsi ku ishuri iyo hari abo babonaho iyo ngeso yo gusiba kenshi. Abayobozi
b’ishuri bagomba gushishikariza buri munyeshuri kugaragaza bagenzi be bakoresha
ibiyobyabwenge cyangwa se abakekwaho kubikoresha kugira ngo bagirwe inama. Ni
ngombwa gufatanya n’ubuyobozi bwa leta bakagenzura abacuruza ibiyobyabwenge
babiha abanyeshuri bakabashyikiriza inzego zibishinzwe.

I. Ibibazo byo kumva umwandiko

		
1. Ni nde uvugwa muri uyu mwandiko? Aravugwaho iki? Iyo umurebye ubona
arangwa n’iki? Kubera iki?
		
2. Vuga ububi bwo kunywa ibiyobyabwenge ushingiye kuri uyu mwandiko
wizwe.
		
3. Vuga ibyiza byo kwirinda gukoresha ibiyobyabwenge uhereye ku byo
wasomye mu mwandiko.

211

		
4. Ni izihe ngamba zivugwa mu mwandiko zo kurwanya ibiyobyabwenge mu
rubyiruko no mu mashuri by’umwihariko?
		
5. Vuga ingingo z’ingenzi n’iz’ingereka ziri mu mwandiko umaze gusoma.
		
6. Ni iyihe nyigisho ukuye muri uyu mwandiko?
		
7. Gereranya ubuzima ubamo n’ibivugwa mu mwandiko, ugeza ku bandi
inyigisho wakuye mu mwandiko.
		
8. Muvuge ingaruka z’ibiyobyabwenge mu muryango nyarwanda.
		
9. Ibyinshi mu biyobyabwenge ni ibinyobwa biba bitujuje ubuziranenge.
Hakorwa iki kugira ngo ubinywa asobanukirwe ibijyanye n’ubuziranenge?

III. Umwitozo wo guhanga

Akayira gato imodoka zicamo.

Ni imyenda isa iba iranga abantu
runaka; abanyeshuri, abasirikare...

		
1. Ishyire mu kigwi cy’umunyeshuri urangije amashuri yisumbuye mu ishami
wiga maze wandike ibaruwa isaba akazi kajyanye n’ibyo wize. Ku mugereka
w’ibaruwa wanditse ushyireho umwirondoro wawe.
		2. Ishyire mu kigwi cy’umubyeyi maze wandikire inshuti yawe uyitumira mu
bukwe bw’umukobwa wawe ugiye gushyingirwa.

212

Imyandiko y’inyongera
Inkuru ndende “Iyo mbimenya”
Umunsi w’ubukwe wakeye nk’indi yose ifite urubanza. Iwabo wa Utamuriza,
abakobwa babyuka bakubura, abagore n’abagabo baza koza ibicuma, kubaka
igisharagati gutegura ibyicaro no gusuka inzoga. Abandi bavoma amazi barateka.
Utamuriza nta kindi yakoze usibye kwitegura gutaha mu rwe. Iwabo wa Mariko na
ho byari uko. Itandukanyirizo ni uko hamwe hari huzuye igisirimu kinshi, ahandi
higanje igiturage. Ibidatunganye abiharira abandi, maze Mariko azinduka yitegura
kwakira umugeni gusa. Byose byari kuri gahunda, isaha igeze imodoka iraza,
abakobwa barayitaka, birangiye Mariko ayijyamo bamujyana iwabo wa Utamuriza.
Yari agiye kumwizanira ngo binjirane mu ngoro y’Imana aho bari busezeranire
kuzabana akaramata. Mariko yasohotse Kabanyana amwitegereza mu maso. Umutima
waramubwiraga uti: “Birarangiye nta kundi. Ntari bugaruke atazanye na Utamuriza
yakunze.” Abasigaye na bo bajya mu modoka bahitira ku kiriziya bategereza abageni.
Baje batinze, baza ari beza, ari abageni bakeye kandi bakwiranye.
Ubukwe bwagenze uko bisanzwe, burangira neza. Iwabo wa Utamuriza basibira
kabiri, ku wa gatatu baza kumutwikurura. Bamuzaniye ibintu bitagira ingano.
Abikorezi bari ishyano ryose. Baranywa, barabyina barizihiza maze uwahageze wese
ataha yishimye. Nijoro abageni bamaze kugaburirwa bajya kuryama.
Bageze ku buriri Mariko abwira Utamuriza:
– “Ndananiwe Nyagasani we! Icyakora biramaze ubwo byose birangiye.”
Utamuriza amusubiza gahoro:
–“Nange ni uko.”
Mariko yumva iyo mvugo atayimenyereye kuri Utamuriza. Yari yahinduye ijwi kandi
avuga asa n’usama umwuka. Amubazanya igihunga:
–“Se ko numva uvuga wahagira ni amahoro?”
Utamuriza yongera kuvuga buhoro kurushaho:
–“Yewe ndumva ntazi uko meze. Nafashwe kare, umutwe uratimbya cyane ngira
n’iseseme ariko ndihangana ngo ntagukura umutima.” Mariko byari bimubereye
urujijo.
Yari azi neza ko umugore we atatashye iwe atwite n’ubwo byari biharawe, bityo
rero ntiyari kubyita kugubwa nabi n’inda. Byongeye kandi yaramukoraga agasanga
yahindutse ubutita. Nta muriro yari afite. Apfa kumubwira byo kumurema agatima:
–“Ni ukunanirwa wari umaze iminsi urara amajoro mu myiteguro. Ryama usinzire.”
Utamuriza yongera kuvuga rutava mu kanwa noneho.
–“Biranga bigakomera. Umutima urenda kunsha mu kanwa.” Mu kanya gato arabyuka
amera nk’usaze. Avuga cyane aranguruye.

213

–“Ye baba weee! Mfata Mariko we, mfata umutwe wange urasandaye we. Ariko se
Imana impoye iki?”

Kuva ubwo Utamuriza araruca ararumira. Mariko abura icyo afata n’icyo areka,
akubita hirya akubita hino, aratabaza ariko abenshi bari babyumvise bakabyita ko ari
abasinzi zaraje mu kigunda. Arakingura maze abari baryamye aho buzura mu cyumba.
Utamuriza yari yacecetse ahumekera hejuru gusa. Ubwo bari bohereje umuntu kujya
kubyutsa se wa Mariko. Barahurura we n’umugore we, azana icyumvirizo yagiraga
mu rugo iwe, apimye umukazana we azinga umunya. Ntibirirwa bajya kubyutsa
ababikira ngo baratira imodoka, abasore b’ibigango batwara Utamuriza mu maboko
bamujyana ku ivuriro. Ntiryari kure. Utamuriza baramupima ariko bayoberwa
indwara imwanuye. Bamutera udushinge bamushyiramo na serumu. Bamwe barara
aho ku bitaro bamanjiriwe, abandi barara mu rugo bicaye. Utamuriza yaraye atazi
iyo ari, wari umutima ugihirita gusa, Mariko amurara iruhande yumva ubwenge
buzunguruka. Bwarakeye abantu barahurura ariko abaganga ntibabemerera
kumureba. Utamuriza yamaze umunsi yirenza undi nta kirahinduka, icyumweru
kirashira, ikindi kirataha. Kwari ukureba ku jisho gusa naho ubundi yari yitungiwe
n’ubuhanga bw’abagerageza kumuvura.
Mariko yabanje kwanga kumuva iruhande, n’ubwo ababyeyi n’inshuti bamwingingaga
kunyuzamo akajya kuruhuka. Muganga yageze aho amwumvisha ko umugore we
atazakanguka vuba noneho abona kwemera rimwe na rimwe kujya ataha ku manywa
akirambika. Muri ako kanya kanzinya abantu ntibamuhaga amahoro. Baboneragaho
kumusura maze uhageze wese akamubwira.
–“Shahu nabaye rwigere urumpe. Nabonye kibonumwe. Nabaye nk’urobye zahabu
y’igiciro kinini maze iranshika yisubirira mu nyanja. Nategereje Utamuriza imyaka
itabarika. Naba ntamureba ibitekerezo bikaguruka ari we bisanga, twaba turi kumwe
nkamureba ubutamuhaga. Namwakiranye ibyishimo n’ubwuzu, mwakirana ikizere
ko ngiye kubana na we mu rukundo no mu mahoro. Ariko se sha hari aho yafashwe
kuriya nkimuririmbira uduhozo?”
Nyina wari ufite ukwemera kurenze urugero yamuhozaga amubwira:

–“Kuri iyi si yacu ibibi n’ibyiza ntibisimburana ahubwo birasobekeranye mu ruvange
rw’urujijo. Uyu munsi uraseka ejo ukarira iyo bitabaye mu kanya. Umwana avuka
hapfa uwundi. Wishima ubuzima none, ejo izuba rikarenga uhindurije nk’ubwire
bwiriye ubusa. Ugakonja ukikunja ugasuherwa ugasigara ufite isura nk’iya rusake
irwaye umuraramo. Ubuzima butwereka ibyiza butuzigamiye n’agahinda, budusekera
kandi buduhema. Sinakubwira ngo ibagirwa ibyatubayeho kuko twese abawe
bikidushegesha n’ubwo umusonga w’undi utakubuza gusinzira. Ndashaka kukubwira
ko ibyago ubifatanyije n’imbaga itabarika, nyamara mudashobora guhura ngo muyage.
Ihangane mwana wange utware umusaraba kigabo.” [...]
Hashize amezi agera kuri atatu bageze imuhira Mariko yari amaze kugarura agatege
gatogato. Afashijwe na nyina yahoraga asenga Nyagasani amusaba kuzamukorera
igitangaza, akamuguranira umusaraba, akamuhereza wa wundi we yabwiye abantu
ko woroshye kuwutwara. Umunsi umwe hari ku cyumweru, arabyuka ajya mu misa.

214

Ihumuje ahita asohoka arataha. Yageze mu nsi ya Kiliziya ngo akebuke inyuma
abona Mukarutabana murumuna wa Utamuriza wagendaga amwiruka inyuma ngo
amusuhuze.
Arahagarara bararamukanya barangije Mukarutabana yahise amubaza:
–“Mu rugo se baraho?”
–“Baraho se baraho iki? Ni ukubyumva ndizera ko namwe muraho.”
–“Twari turaho. Umusaza mukuru wa sogokuru ni we warembye cyane. N’ubu
nasanga yashizemo umwuka.”
–“Iyaba ari bo barwaraga gusa ndetse byarimba akaba ari bo bapfa. Biti ihi se
bakamugara maze abasore ntibavutswe ubuto bwabo by’amanzaganya.”

–“Iyo Imana itaza kuduhisha ibanga ry’ibyago, ubuzima bwari kutubihira cyane. Isi
yari guhindura isura. Abashaje bakiheba bose, abato bakiterura ngo n’inkuba yo
mu bicu ntiyamanuka ngo izababashe. Kurobanya mbona ari ngombwa kandi ari na
byo byiza n’ubwo ibyago wabyumva ahandi.” Mariko arijima azinga umunya avuga
arakaye.

–“Iyo uza kuba utavuka mu nda imwe na Utamuriza mba nkwise umurozi w’ibisebe.
Koko ugatinyuka ngo ubona ari byiza ko n’abato bagwirirwa n’amakuba? Kandi uzi
neza ukuntu mukuru wawe asigaye ashinyagurirwa n’iminsi n’ukuntu yari ageze mu
rwunyunyu rw’ubuzima?”
Mukarutabana yubika umutwe maze asububiza Mariko ababaye:
–“Njya kuvuga ntyo sinatekereje by’umwihariko Utamuriza mwene mama.
Ge navugaga ibyo mbona mpereye ku batuye iyi si bose. Naho mukuru wange
yarambabaje ndarira ngeze aho ndayihanagura. Nsigaye ndi ikinege kandi mufite.
Ibyishimo n’agahinda byange nabisangiraga na we, ubu narabyikubiye mbibika mu
nda.”
–“Nta gihe ibyago twagize bitazadutera agahinda. Ni uko nta cyo twabikoraho. Ariko
kandi birushaho kutubabaza iyo dutekereje ko yazize amaherere n’inda yuzuye
umujinya n’urwango.”

Mukarutabana ntiyari umwana. Yumvaga neza icyo muramu we ashaka kuvuga.
Arerura aramubwira.
–“Ubugome ntibuteze kuzashira mu bantu igihe cyose isi izaba ituwe na bo. Ariko
se ko numva ubinyura hirya ubicamo imigani, mwaba mukeka namwe ko Utamuriza
yazize uburozi?”
–“Nkurikije uburyo umbajije ndumva ariko twese twabifashe. Ariko se umunsi
dusezerana ko yari akeye udashobora gutekereza ko yarangije kwandura?”
Mukarutabana avugira hejuru.
–“Ugiye kuvuga ko yahagurutse iwacu yararozwe? Sigaho sigaho. Utamuriza yazize
iwanyu, arogerwa mu rugo rwa sebukwe. Ahubwo nimutitonda abo mwizeye
mukabashinga ikambere bazabamara.” [...]
Bifatiye ku bya Niyitegeka Mukarugira Yuliyana, Iyo mbimenya, (imp. 88-101)

220

Igitekerezo k’ingabo: Igitero k’imigogo
Iki gitero cyasakiranije by’umwihariko Abanyarwanda n’Abanyankore. Icyakoze
kivugwamo n’ayandi mahanga.

Inkuru y’iki gitero yabarwa mu bice bibiri
Inzira y’Abanyankore
Inzira y’Abanyarwanda
Iki gitero kandi cyabayeho mu gihe umwami Kigeli IV Rwabugili, yari mu Bunyabungo
n’ingabo ze hafi ya zose. Nyuma ariko yaje kubimenya agaruka mu Rwanda kurwanya
Abanyankore.
“Ehururu ya Rwanda“: Abanyankore batera u Rwanda
Dore uko Ntare V Rugingiza rwa Migereka, umwami mu Nkore yateguye gutera u
Rwanda.
Yatumije ingabo n’abatware agira ati:“Ejo muzohereze abantu bakwiriye ingerero,
banzanire abatware b’ingabo, baze mbabwire, bazahigire gutera u Rwanda, bahigire
gutera Rwabugili.” Ati: “Rwabugili ni we njya numva bavuga; Rwabugili bajya
bamunshimira cyane; ni we njya numva bavuga ngo agira igihugu kiza kandi kinini,
ngo agira inka nziza nyinshi, ngo na we aratora nka nge, ngo na we afite intore.” Ati:
“ Maze muzahigire gutera Rwabugili. Nimumara kumunesha nzizera ko mfite ingabo.
Ikindi cya Rwabugili kimbabaza ni uko bangereranya na we ngo ni we duhwanye.”
Arahaguruka Igumira rya Bacwa, Ruharabwoba, aravuga ati: “Gahorane Imana!
Mpigiye gutera Rwabugili! Nzamutera bikumare agahinda. Iki gitero ni icyange.
Nzatura mu gihugu cya Rwabugili, nzatunga inka za Rwabugili. Rwabugili nankundira
tukarwana, ntampunge, nzamufata mpiri mukuzanire!”
Maze arahaguraka Matsiko mu Nyana ati: “Mpigiye gutera Rwabugili. Niyumva
natungutse n’umutwe w’Inyana, akankundira tukarwana, ntampunge, nange
nzamufata mukuzanire aha!“
Arahaguruka Itiri rya Gicobwa, Rugambwishayija, umutware w’Ubwuma n’Abarwanyi.
Ati: “Nange mpigiye gutera Rwabugili niwumva yatungutse mu mutwe w’Ubwuma
n’Abarwanyi, akankundira tukarwana, nzamufata muzane hano!”
Arahaguruka Kijoma cya Kayisinga, Rugatwankurayijo, umutware w’Ingangura
ati: “Mpigiye gutera Rwabugili. Niwumva atungutse mu mutwe w’ingangurarugo,
akankundira tukarwana, nzamufata muzane aha!”
Arahaguruka Rugumayo rwa Kanagayiga Rusheshangabo Rutacwekera, umutware
w’Abanganshuro. Ati: “Mpigiye gutera Rwabugili natunguka mu Banganshuro
nzamufata mukuzanire.”
Arahaguruka Rwirangira Rutakirwa, umutware w’Ibirehe, arahiga, ararangiza.
Arahaguruka Rwishumba rwa Mwendo, arahiga mu Batenganduru, ararangiza.
Arahiga Bwijire mu Badahunga. Na we ararangiza.
Abatware bamaze guhiga, abahungu na bo barakenyera barahiga.
Arahaguruka Nkoko ya Gahunga, Rutakomwa.
Arahaguruka Irabiro rya Gahuta, Rutarindimuka.

221

Arahaguruka Bayija ba Kambiri, Rugomwa.
Arahaguruka Bangonera ba Ndondoza Ihigiro.
Arahaguruka Kamurase ka Bwisheke, Rutakangarana.
Arahaguruka Kakuba ka Kangonya, Rutagengwa Ruhuzabiri.
Arahaguruka Cyanyangutura cya Manunga, Ruteranyangabo.

Bamaze guhiga umutware w’igitero, Igumiro, asaba iminsi, ati: “Iminsi yacu ni
itandatu, uwa karindwi tugatabara i Rwanda.” Baragenda bamara gatandatu, ku munsi
wa karindwi bataha ibwami, barara mu mihigo; buracya birirwa bahabwa intwaro:
abahabwa imbunda barazihabwa, abahabwa amacumu n’imiheto barabihabwa. Uwo
munsi bigaba Rugando, baza Kazinga, baza Mwizi na Kankaranka, banyura Rujebe
rwa Kabuganda, i Gorora rya bene Rukari, bananyura i Rukoni rwa Cyabukemwa,
bagera Rwampara. Amashyo ya Nshenyi arikanga, arahunga, amwe yambukira mu
byambu bya Rina n’Ibanda, andi yambukira mu byambu bya Butsinda na Bugomora,
andi ahunga aza i Rwanda.

Abanyenkore bakomereza i Ngugu, banyura Rubingo, baraza n’i Kabuhunge,
barakomeza i Kiru, Ibare rya Rutsizi, bagera i Rwimukoma, barakomeza i Mirama,
baza Ruhana, baza Kayenzi, Murambi, mu Rukanja rwa Kigina, barambuka batera i
Ndorwa y’u Bugahe, batera i Nkungu, batera i Rwamuniga, batera i Tobo, i Kigando, i
Ruhirima, i Kazaza, i Kamutinga i Kangoma, mu Byashamira bya Ngoma, baragumya
baranyaga, bafata abagore n’abana. Abandi bambukira mu byambu by’Iyimuriro,
barazamuka no mu Mbuga, bagera i Ngungu za Karubereye, barakomeza n’i Ntoma
za Nyarugeyo, bagera i Kijojo, i Masha, i Bare ry’Inzuki, rya Gasinga, i Bunyinya, i
Nyagatare, mu Nsheke za Gahija na Rugarama, batera Gasheshe na Rutaraka kwa
Rwabugili, barahanyaga, batwara abagore n’abana.
Abandi Banyankore banyura mu Gitengure cya Masha, na Gishwamba na Rwantera,
bambukira mu cyambu k’imikinga, barazamuka Bukegete, i Rurenge n’i Gashura.
Batera i Rubambantare, i Nyamikamba, i Rwijo n’i Bukire, barahanyaga, batwara
abagore n’abana.
Abandi baciye iy’Iheru, batera inkiko y’Uruyange. Banateye i Rwigishugi, batera
i Bushara, i Nyagasharara, batera i Rwubuzizi, i Ndengo, i Kanyami, i Gikagata, i
Karama, i Butema, i Mutumba, bambukira mu cyambu cya Rukukumbo, batera inkiko
y’Abarota, batera i Shabana, i Buguma, i Mukama mu Cyabaharure, mu Cyagaju, i
Cyanyambi, i Kagonga, i Kigina, i Gakoma, batera mu Cyabwana, baranyaga, bajyana
abagore n’abana.

Abandi Banyankore bambukiye mu cyambu cy’amasangano cya Rwinturege, abatera
i Bushoga, i Butwaro, i Gakirage, i Twamugabo, i Kibogo, i Cyabayaga, i Kagara, i
Muhingo, i Gituki, mu Nyabwunyu, banatera inkiko y’Abatanguha, baranyaga batwara
abagore n’abana.

Abateye i Rutaraka batera inkiko y’Abashakamba, batera i Kabirizi, mu Kajumu, i
Kagabo, mu Rutoma, i Rubungo, mu Kigarama, i Buyigi, bazamuka rwa Rwakayumba,
batera Mikirage, mu Rurama, i Kimbugu, baza ku ibara rya Murera, batera mu

222

Kabahoza, mu Kamuri, i Kamucemete, batera i Karambi, mu Burambira, mu Bugamba,
mu Mpashane, bazamuka ku i Bare rya Ngarama, ibitero byose bisanganira mu Bitoma
bya Nyambungo.

Igumira ahamagara abatasi ati: “Ko mureba narangije igihugu cya Rwabugili, nkaba
ntaramubona yagiyehe?” Bati: “Rwabugili yatabaye i Bunyabungo.” Ati: “Ese avuye
aha ngaha agera i Bunyabungo amaze kangahe?” Bati: “Ntitubizi, twumva ko ari
kure.” Ati: “Ese avuye aha ngaha agera kuri Nyabarongo amaze kangahe?” Bati:
“Amaze gatatu.” Ati: “Ngiye kwicara kuri Nyabarongo. Rwabugili azahansange.”
Abatasi baramurahira bati: “Uvanyeho kugera kuri Nyabarongo, nta n’ubwo wagera
kuri Muhazi, ari rwo ruzi ruri hafi.” Bati: “Kandi ntugire ngo warangije igihugu cya
Rwabugili, ubu uracyari ku nkiko y’i Ndorwa, nturagera mu Rwanda.” Ati: “Ngiye
kwicara aha ngaha Rwabugili azahansange.”

Abatware bati: “Cyura iminyago!” Na we ati: “Ese ngende nta Rwabugili? Ntare
nazamubwira ngo iki?” Bati: “Cyura iminyago, uzaba ugaruka!” Nuko agenda
agononwa, ataha hafi. Ataha Gakoma, buracya, ataha Gashenyi, buracya ataha za
Ruhirima na Kigando, buracya ataha hakurya ya Ruhamo. Ingabo z’Abanyankore
zigera iwabo ibwami ziriyereka.

Ntare abaza Igumira ati: “Ko numva warangije igihugu cya Rwabugili mwarabonanye?”
Ati: “Oya, nasanze yaratabaye ahantu hitwa i Bunyabungo, nshaka no kuhamukurikira
mbuzwa n’abatware n’abatasi, ngo mfite iminyago da!” Ntare ati: “Ntabwo bakubeshye,
uwagize ishya ni uwabonye iminyago. Niba Rwabugili adakurikiye inka ze n’abantu
be, uzamare iminsi mikeya uzasubireyo.”

Abanyarwanda bo ku nkiko
Twamaze kuvuga ko igihe Abanyankore bateye ku nkiko y’i Ndorwa n’Umutara,
Kigeli IV Rwabugili yari mu Bunyabungo, ingabo zose z’u Rwanda zajyanye na we.
Bisobanura rero ko n’ingabo nyinshi zo mu Ndorwa no mu Mutara zari zaratabaye,
hasigara abarwanyi bake. Ingabo zagerageje gushwashwanya ngo zirwanye
Abanyankore ni izo mu Badahemuka ba Ruvuzacyuma n’Abaruta ba Rwatangabo.
Bagerageje kurwanya ariko baraneshwa, bica gusa Abanyankore barindwi. Mu
miryango y’Abanyankore harimo ubushyo bumwe bw’inyambo za Rwatangabo zari
zaragishiye hafi y’inkiko.

Abahima (Abanyankore) bageze i Rutaraka, ni umusozi wari wubatseho urugo
rwa Rwabugili rwagengaga impugu za ruguru, umutware w’urwo rugo ari we
Kinyamakara cya Ndezi yari mu ruzinduko. Yaragarutse asanga urugo rw’umwami
rwaratwitswe kandi n’umugore wari ushinzwe urwo rugo ari we Nyiranziza
muka Mpore ya Semandira yarajyanywe mu minyango. Kinyamakara abonye ko
atazabihonoka acikira mu Nkore ajya guhakwa kwa Ntare V Rugingiza rwa Migereka,
agezeyo aramubwira ati: “Maze kubona neza ingabo zawe n’imirasanire yazo, kuko
natabaranye na zo! None rero dore naragucikiye kugira ngo ndokore amagara yange,
sinakubeshya! Utatishe neza, uzamenye niba Rwabugili azagutera, maze nubyumva

223

uzahunge hakiri kare! Akurusha ingabo nyinshi, akurusha intwari, ntiwashobora
kumurwanya.” Koko rero n’abandi batasi ni uko babivugaga, Ntare yohereza umutasi
wo kumunekera Rwabugili by’imvaho, atuma uwitwa Muhanika. Uwo mutasi yaje
yiyita ko acikiye mu Rwanda, maze akihagera akeza Rwatangabo rwa Nzigiye,
umutware w’Abaruta bari bagwiriye mu Mutara. Aguma kuri Rwatangabo agitegereje
ko Rwabugili agaruka.

Ihaguruka ry’igitero k’imigogo nyirizina.
Amakuru y’igitero k’Ehururu ya Rwanda, Kigeri IV Rwabugili yabimenyashejwe
n’umuhima witwaga Bukomangando. Uwo yari atumwe na Gatarayiha watwaraga
Abagina bo muri Mpororo kuko uwo Gatarayiha (na we yari umuhima) yayobokaga
umwami w’u Rwanda. Bukomangando asanga Rwabugili ku Miraho mu Bunyabungo,
Rwabugili amaze kubyumva bamuturira mu bwato uwo munsi akomokera i
Rubengera mu Bwishaza. Ategeka ko ingabo zose zari mu Bunyabungo ziturirwa,
kandi ko abatware bose bahuruza ingabo nyinshi akazahurira na bo i Gatsibo.
Rwabugili ntiyasibira i Rubengera ahita mu kibaya cya Rusiga, hagati y’Ubwishaza n’u
Budaha. Bukeye ataha i Kageyo kwa Kingogo, bukeye ataha mu Buhanga bw’i Murera
ahavuye ataha mu Rusumo rwa Kabona ka Mugara, hareba i Bukimbiri. Bwarakeye
ataha i Mpinga kwa Ruharamanzi, mu Buberuka, bukeye ataha i Karehe mu Buyaga
kwa Gahunde ka Nyakaja. Bukeye ataha iwe i Gatsibo.
Akigera i Gatsibo, wa Munyankore w’umutasi Muhanika yimura Rwatangabo aza
kwisohoza kuri Rwabugili kugira ngo amutate ubutamutirimukaho. Rwabugili na
we akigera i Gatsibo yohereza mu Nkore umuntu wo kumutatira ibyo kwa Ntare,
uwo mutasi akaba Kambwa ka Rwakarengwa wo mu ngabo z’Abadahemuka za
Ruvuzacyuma rwa Semuzigura. Igihe ingabo zigikorana Rwabugili ava i Gatsibo ajya
iwe i Sakara ho mu Gisaka agezeyo ahamara iminsi ine, hanyuma agaruka i Gatsibo
kureba ingabo zari zimaze kuhagera. Ubwo ava mu Gisaka ashagawe n’ingabo
z’Abarasa ba Mugugu wa Shumbusho, zarimo amatorero abiri: intwari z’inkotanyi
n’itorero ry’abo mu Ndushabandi za Rwatangabo rwa Nzigiye ryari riturutse mu
Migongo ndetse n’Abadahigwa ba Runyange bo mu Mirenge. Imitwe yari yakoraniye
i Gatsibo yari iyi:
I Nyaruguru za Muhigirwa wa Rwabugili: zarimo amatorero atandatu: Intwari, Ijuru,
Abaganwa, Interarubango, Interacumu n’Ishabi.
Uruyange za Kabare ka Rwakagara, zarimo amatorero ane: Uruyange, Ingorabahizi,
Indinda n’Abagina.
Indushabandi za Rwatangabo rwa Nzigiye: Abarota n’Abahigi.
Abashakamba ba Rutishereka rwa Sentama, barimo amatorero atatu:
Inyambo II, Imbungiramihigo II n’Abashakamba (bari barasubiye ku mazina
y’amatorero ya kera).
Inyange za Cyoya cya Ngwije (umutware w’i Burundi wari waracikiye Rwabugili)
zarimo amatorero atanu: Injyamubiri, Abasoni, Itanganika, Abarara n’Amatara.
Hari kandi n’imitwe itari ifite amatorero, ahubwo abarwanyi bayo bakibumbamo
nk’itorero rimwe.

224

Ni yo iyi:
Imbanzamihigo za Rwidegembya rwa Kigenza.
Intaganzwa za Nturo wa Nyirimigabo.
Imvejuru za Mushikazi na Runigamugabo.
Inyakare za Kanyangemwe ka Rwogera.
Abarinda ba Rwabirinde rwa Rwogera.
Abashumba ba Rugagaza rwa Mabano.
Abanyansanga ba Ruhararamanzi rwa Shumbusho
Abamaragishyika ba Rwinyonga rwa Kigeli IV Rwabugili.
Urugo rw’umwami, Ingangurarugo, rwari rugabwe na Bisangwa bya Rugombituri. Ni
rwo ngabo z’umwihariko za Rwabugili. Zarimo amatorero arindwi: Ingangurarugo,
Inshozamihigo, Ibisumizi, Irityaye, Intarindwa, Urwiririza n’Abarashi (bo mu Gahunga
k’i Murera).
Rwabugili amaze kwerekeza ingabo i Gatsibo azamuka ajya kwa Rutaraka.
Muhanika, wa mutasi wa Ntare asanga ingabo ari nyinshi cyane, kandi atumva neza
iby’amatorero, yibwira ko ibyo bintu byose ari ikivangitirane. Bageze rero kwa
Rutaraka, ashaka gusuzuma niba byahurura byose maze ajya mu mpiga abwira
umugaragu we kuvuza induru avuga ngo: “Abahina banyaze ingishywa.” Iyo nduru ngo
ivuge, mu kanya kangana urwara, za ngabo zihurura nk’umuntu umwe, zivanguyemo
imitwe, umwumwe n’amatorero yawo maze abibonye bimutera ubwoba ingabo na
none zimaze guhurura zisanga iyo nduru yari impuha, ariko ntizamenya uwayivugije.
Muhanika ashaka gutera Rwabugili ubwoba aramubwira ati: “Umva naragucikiye
sinagushuka ibyo urabizi. Wenda wihorere kuko utazashobora Ntare kuko akurusha
ingabo nyinshi kandi z’intwari zikubitiyeho no gutwara imbunda.”

Rwabugili amaze kubyumva, aho kuguranura, nk’uko Muhanika yabikekaga, ahubwo
noneho ategeka abatware kongera ingabo. Igihe bagitegereje ko ingabo zindi
zihuruzwa ajya kwihigira ahantu hitwa Bitete ho mu cyanya cya za Gabiro. Abatware
bamaze kongera ingabo noneho imbaga iradubika.
Hagati aho, ingabo zigitegereje ko umwami ahiguka n’uko izindi ngabo ziza banga
kwicara ubusa, bahimba imikino. Bicamo ibice bibiri, kimwe kitwa Abanyankore
abandi biyita Abanyarwanda. Abanyankore bimika umwami wabo, umugabo Busizori
wo mu Bagina, naho Abanyarwanda bimika Musinga wa Rwabugili, nuko bararwana,
ariko by’imikino. Amaherezo Abanyenkore baratsindwa n’umwami wabo afatwa
mpiri, Abanyarwanda baza kumwiyereka babanje kumusiga amase umubiri wose,
mu mutwe no mu maso.
Rwabugili abonye ko ingabo zivuye inyuma arahaguruka. Ingabo zihagurukira ahantu
hitwa Karwera, zirara ku cyuzi mu gishanga kitwa Rufiha. Iryo joro Muhanika aracika
ajya kubwira Ntare icyo yamutatiye. Babonye ko acitse baba ari bwo bamenya ko
yari umutasi. Ariko ataranagera kwa shebuja, Abagina bo ku Nkiko bari baraburiye
Ntare ko igitero cyahagurutse.

225

Bamaze kubimenyesha Ntare, Kinyamakara cya Ndenzi arongera aramubwira ati:
“Niba uzi ubwenge hambira uhunge! Mwarwaniye mu Bukanga murasana n’Abaganda,
mbarasanamo, ndeba imirasire yanyu, murongera murwana n’Abanyoro mpari,
byose mbireba. Rwabugili akurusha ingabo nyinshi, akurusha n’intwari, uhunge
hakiri kare. ”

Ntare akoranya ingabo ze, kugira ngo ziyereke, maze Kinyamakara arebe. Zizimagiza
Mbarara zigeza mu Biharwana, Ruyonza na Kamukuza no mu Ruharo n’i Syanyu.
Ntare ati: “Kinyamakara ziriya ngabo ntizatsinda Abanyarwanda?” Kinyamakara
ati: “Imitwe yawe itatu izajya itsindwa n’umwe wo mu Rwanda.” Ni bwo Muhanika
ahingutse ati: “Ntare rero nimuhambire duhunge!” Ingabo ziduteye, ahari si abantu
babyarwa ubanza Rwabugili abahinga, akabasarura nk’imyaka! Uko nababonye ni
abagabo bateye ubwoba! N’urugo rw’umwami rutiriwe ruza cyangwa ngo hatere
urugo rumwe rw’umwana w’umwami, umutwe waho umwe ntabwo wawuherana.”
Abatware n’Abanyankore bandi bagaya Muhanika ngo yatase Kinyamusozi, azana
ubwoba aho guhindukira atera ingabo zabo agatima ngo azikaze, zizarwane zidafite
igihunga.

Mu ihaguruka rya Rwabugili Mugugu wa Shumbusho, wari umutoni ukomeye agira
umwami inama ati: “Wari ukwiye gutuma Kabare akajya kuvana Nyiramibambwe i
Giseke, akamuzana i Gatsibo akaba ari ho aza gukorera imihango y’inteko kuko atari
kure cyane y’Igihugu duteye. (Uwo Nyiramibambwe yari umwamikazi wa Rwabugili,
umugabekazi).
Impamvu Mugugu yagiriye umwami iyo nama ni uko yari umwanzi wa Kabare n’uwa
Kanjogera mbere atarimikanwa na Rutarindwa. Yifuzaga ko Kabare yazagwa mu bico
by’Abahima bakamwica maze akabura ishya ryo muri icyo gitero. Kabare yasubiye
guhagurutsa mushiki we, maze ingabo ze z’Uruyange zitabazwa n’abafasha be.

Umugaba w’icyo gitereo yari Nyamuhenda wa Kajeje. Ni we wari wararenzwe,
izina rye ry’ubugaba rikaba Kigeli Ndabarasa Nyamuhenda. Bageze mu Ndorwa
bakora imihango yo gucungura amaboko, bamugerekaho n’undi mugabo Mibambwe
Mutabazi Ruvuzacyuma rwa Semuzigura wari ugabye ingabo ze z’Abadahemuka.
Dore impamvu yatumye batoranya ayo mazina y’ubugaba. Kigeli III Ndabarasa yari
yarigeze gutsinda i Nkore naho Mibambwe I Mutabazi yaratsinze Abanyoro. Uwo
mugaba mushya bamwungukira ingabo za Mugugu wa Shumbusho. Nyamara izo
ngabo za Ruvuzacyuma ntizahabwa inzira yazo ngo zitandukane n’indi mitwe, byose
byagendeye hamwe n’ubwo abagaba bari babiri, ari Nyamuhenda uzibereye inteko.

Igitero gihagurutse ku Rufuha, bataha Nshenyi, bukeye bataha i Gorora, bukeye
bataha i Ngwayera ya Mayenja, bukeye bataha mu mateme ya Gihone, bafata
Rwampara ho mu Nkore. Bukeye ingabo z’inkwakuzi zirasuka i Mbarara kwa Ntare,
zisanga barogoreye kera, umwami n’ingabo ze zose barahunze berekeza mu nkiko
y’amajyaruguru igana i Bunyoro. Rwabugili ashinga ihema rye mu rugo rwa Ntare i
Kambere, bahamara iminsi itandatu.

226

Muri uwo murwa wa Mbarara hari hubatswe amaduka, ahari y’Abahinde, bene
yo na bo bari bahunze banasahuye ibintu byose. Abanyarwanda batinya kwinjira
muri ayo mazu ariko Rwabugili aratinyuka n’abandi baboneraho (aho ni ho havuye
wa mugani w’urutare yinjiyemo rukamwifungiraho). Bahamaze iminsi itandatu,
Mugugu arahaguruka n’umugaba we Ruvuzacyuma, barara bambutse uruzi rwa
Gashara banyuze ku iteme ritinzwe kizungu. Ku munsi wa karindwi, Rwabugili na
we arahaguruka, urugo rwa Ntare bararutwika. Bamaze kwambukira kuri rya teme
bararisenya. Ingabo zigandika mu Masha na Nsanga. Izindi ngabo zica i Ngando ku
kanyanja ka Rwisirabo.

Muri icyo gihe umunyankore witwaga Bwafamba aza gukorera ishyano Ntare
wahungaga. Yaka abagaragu be imyambaro yabo ishaje ayizanira Ntare ati: “Dore
imicuzo y’Abanyarwanda nazaniwe n’abatasi bange! Wowe ntiwatangazwa n’ukuntu
bavuye iwabo iyo gihera bakagera ino bataricwa n’inzara? Reka tuge kubahuhura!”
Ntare ati: “Umva rero, numvise ko Abanyarwanda bakora impamba cyane, nimukorane
mubanze muhige!”
Bwafamba amaze gushuka Ntare, barara mu mihigo ngo bagiye guhurura
Abanyarwanda. Ntare agabanyamo ingabo ze imitwe. Abanza kohereza Abanga,
Abatenganduru n’Inyana. Barara baza ijoro ryose kugira ngo bazasakirane
n’Abanyarwanda hakiri kare ubwo kandi ni bwo Ntare yatabaje Mwanga umwami
w’Ubunganda amutumyeho Rutarurwa. Mwanga aramuhakanira kuko igihugu cyari
cyarabaye icy’abazungu atagifite ububasha bwo kohereza ingabo aho yishakiye.
Intore zimwe za Ntare zari zifite imbunda za Cyarabu
(Bitanga makoba).

Urugamba rwa mbere i Kanyamisisa
Abanyarwanda barakomeza berekeza ku nkiko y’i Bunyoro aho Ntare yari yarahungiye.
uretse n’abatasi inkora y’inka na yo yari ihagije kubayobora inzira.

Batangira kuzamuka umusozi wa Kanyamisisa izuba rirashe bururukiriza ingobyi ya
Rwabugili munsi y’igiti cy’umunyinya. Itorero ryo mu Ngangurarugo ryitwaga Irityaye
risabwa kujya imbere y’indi mitwe. Asokamo abarwanyi babimenyereye: inkotanyi
zo mu Barasa no mu Bahirika bo mu Ndushabandi n’Ijuru bo muri Nyaruguru igice
cy’Akamaragishyika ba Baryinyonza n’igice k’Indinda zo mu Ruyange.
Bakimanuka inyuma y’umusozi wa Kanyamisisa umwami yari atetseho, bahubirana
n’igitero cy’Abanyankore bahita bapopera imbunda za Makoba nyamara
Abanyarwanda bakaba basanzwe bazizi kuko Rwabugili na we yari afite itorero
ryitwa Ibyinigiri ry’abantu mirongo itandatu bari bazifite.

Abanyarwanda bahise bagwa bubamye, amasasu afata ubusa. Abanyarwanda
barababyukana ntibatuma babasha kuzicucumamo ubuganga maze bituma
Abanyankore barwanisha intwaro zisanzwe za gakondo. Abanyarwandwa bakubitwa
inshuro kuko batinyaga guterwa amacumu y’amahima ku mukondo w’Abanyarwanda
hagaruka Butare wo mu Bamaragishyika na Rwabagabo na Rukarakamba rwa

227

Rusekampunzi bo mu Ndushabandi na Runyundo wo mu Ngangurarugo ku ruhembe
rw’Ijuru hagaruka Buyenge bwa Rwankubito. Bongeye gukubitwa inshuro bwa kabiri
hagaruka abungabo.
Bongeye gukubitwa inshuro ubwa gatatu, Buyenge bwa Rwankubito agaruka
ahamiriza inka yitwa Urukatsa ati: “ Mbese Ijuru ko mwananiranye murongera kandi
guhungana iyi nka y’urukatsa?” Ijuru bamugarukiye umwe muri bo arafora, uwo ni
Muhizi wa Ruhogo, injishi y’umuheto we iracika. Yicara hasi ati: “Ushaka guhunga
niyihungire ariko muzabwire umugore wange ko napfuye nanga guhunga.” Yiyicarira
aho, apfundika injishi y’umuheto we arongera arawubanga, abandi bamurwanaho,
urugamba rurahama ntirwongera gucubangana.

Urugamba rwo ku Mukondo rwari rugicubangana, nibwo Zimurinda rya Semurima
ahindutse, kuko atari yaje mu b’inkwakuzi. Atota Ruyundo rwa Kajeje ati: “ Ruyundo
rwa Kajeje, gira umwami! Aragarukana n’ingabo.” Ruyundo ati: “Ceceka nabaye
ngenyine ubwo ahageze ntibyongera.” Bongera gukubitwa inshuro gatatu Zimurinda
akabatanga kugaruka, ubwa kane ati: “Noneho ndasa na Rwabugili!” Ati: “Mbese
aho si ge mfura iri hano genyine?” Ayo magambo arakaza rubanda, bitugatugira
Abanyankore, bakamya inshuro zabo maze Abahima bibuka guhunga bamaze
gukamuka.

Aho umwami yari ari, yumvishe urwamo yamenye ko ruhinanye maze bihutisha
ingabo zindi ngo zivune abagiye imbere. Muhigirwa wa Rwabugili yumvishe imbunda
zicecetse aravuga ati: “Izo mbunda zivuze rimwe zigahora, ubanza Inkaka yahageze!”
yashakaga kuvuga Munanira wa Migabo wo mu Ijuru. Ingabo zagiye kubavuna zasanze
birangiye mu mwanya mutoya cyane. Basanze haguye Abahima mirongo kenda
n’umunani. Abo mu Nyana ni bo bari bahashiriye cyane. Matsiko umugaba wazo na
we arahagwa hamwe na Itiri rya Gikobwa. Abo ni bo abatatsi bashoboye kumenya.
Biratangaje ariko ngo ntamunyarwanda uzwi wahaguye.
Kaningu akomeretswa nabi n’icumu mu ntantu, ariko ntibashobora kumusonga kuko
Rutarikandagira rwa Rutishereka wo mu Rityaye yamwimanye. Aho barambitse
Kaningu bamworoshe ikirago. Rwabugili abibonye ahahingutse ati: “Kiriya kirago
kinteye ubwoba!” Nta ngobyi bari bafite yo kumuhekamo. Ingobyi za ba muka
Rwabugili n’iz’abana be zirahita, hanyuma iya Musinga ihingutse ategeka ko
bamwururutsa. Agendesha amaguru, ingobyi bayihekemo Kaningu. Ibyo binyura
rubanda cyane. Uwo munsi ingabo zigandika ahitwa i Shongi na Rutaka. Muri iryo
joro, bene Shamurari batera ingando z’Abanyarwanda bitwaje imbunda za Makoba.
Amasasu bayaboneza ihema rya Rwabugili bararitobagura cyane bagira ngo ni ryo
arimo. Iyo yabaga ari mu mahanga mu bitero, bashingaga ihema rye, akajya kurara
mu ngando. Hirya ahataryegereye, aho batamukekera. Amasasu yica abantu batatu:
umugaragu wa Nyagatoma wa Gashonga n’uwa Rumagishwa n’uwa Musinga wo
mu bahetsi be. Hapfuye kandi n’inka ebyiri: iya Nyagatoma n’iya Rwanyapfumu.
Hakomerekeye n’abagaragu babiri. Abanyarwanda bapfa gushwashwanya imyambi,
kuko hari mu mwijima, bakarasa aho umuriro w’isasu uturutse. Bica Abanyankore

228

barindwi. Ntibamenye umubare w’abakomeretse. Bukeye bw’aho bakambura bajya
kugandika ahitwa Ruyonza na Rwibitetero, bakomeje inzira Ntare yanyuzemo agana
ku Nkiko y’i Bunyoro.

Ku munsi wakurikiyeho, umugaba w’igitero Nyamuhenda wa Kajeje, akajyana
n’ingabo z’inkwakuzi imbere bashaka kujya kugaruza inka Ntare yahunganye.
Basanga ku nzira intumbi ebyiri z’Abanyankore bari barakomeretse muri ya mirwano
ya nijoro. Ingabo zigeze ku Ibare rya Ngarama zisanga Abanyankore baratwitse
uruhira rukagatiye agahugu kose, kugira ngo umuyonga uvaneho inkora ya za nka
kuko ari yo yagendaga iyobora ingabo z’Abanyarwanda. Ingabo zibuze inkora y’inka
yaziyoboraga ziragaruka zisanga Rwabugili mu ngando ya Ruyonza, batabaruka ubwo,
bazaga barara buhorobuhoro.

Bukeye bahura na Kabare azanye n’ingabo nyinshi cyane zenda kungana umubare
n’izari kumwe n’umwami. Abanyarwanda babonye umutware ukomeye bamwihomaho
ari benshi n’abatari ingabo ze kuko bakekaga ko icyo gitero kigiye mu Nkore kizazana
iminyago myinshi bakanga gucikanwa. Bamaze guhura na Kabare bataha ahitwa
Rwanjura na Kayigoshora. Bamaze gukambika imvura irara igwa ijoro ryose. Mu
gitondo mu ihema rya Rwabugili bahicira inzoka y’impiri nini cyane yaje kuhugama.
Bahahagurutse bataha ahandi baharuhukira iminsi irindwi. Kanimba ka Ruzima
ahatungukana ingabo zindi zingana n’izari zazanye na Kabare. Zari zahagurukijwe
n’impamvu imwe na ya yindi yahomye benshi kuri Kabare: gushaka iminyago.

Urugamba rwa kabiri kuri Nyagakoni
Igihe Kanimba abagezeho, ku munsi wa karindwi, yasanze umwami arimo ahaguruka.
Bashyira nzira bataha i Nyagakoni, Abanyankore bahita Nkoni. Aho ni ho ibintu
byari bigiye gucikira. Haje umugabo Munanira wa Rutimbo wo mu Mutara wo mu
itorero ry’Indinda ryo mu Ruyange rya Kabare. Abwira Kabare na Ruhinankiko na
Rutishereka na Mushyo ati: “Nimubwire umwami murobanure ingabo, kuko igitero
cy’Abanyankore kiri hafi.” Abo batware bohereza Sebaganji ba Bukwege ngo ajye
kureba ingando impande zose mu gihe bo bari bagisiganuza Munanira wa Rutimbo
ngo babone kujya kubibwira umwami.
Ubwo bacaga ingando, ingabo za Ntare zari zabatanze kugera kuri Nyagakoni, zihisha
mu gashyamba kari kahegereye.

Ingabo z’ Abanyarwanda zo zigashaka kuza kubagwa gitumo ngo zikore ishyano.
Ingando zimaze gushingwa, ibitsimbanyi bijya gusenya inkwi Muri ka gashyamba.
Bihingutse bikora mu ntozi kuko cyari igihe cy’utujojoba. Abanyankore babyukana
imbunda zivugira rimwe, bya bitsimbanyi bipfamo umubare munini. Abanyankore
baza babihomereye, babiroha mu ngando. Ingabo zose zenda intwaro icyarimwe,
n’imitwe ntiyarobanurwa kuko nta gihe bari bafite, maze bose bahaguruka uruvange;
ndetse n’abatware bose benda intwaro zabo bigira ku rugamba. Amakamba
bari bambariye ingabo zabo bayashyira mu bikondorero, bayakindikirizaho.
Abanyarwanda bakubitwa inshuro, basukwa mu ngando.

229

Usanga rero ari ingabo zari zimeze nk’i Kivu. Nuko abatware barakomera bati:
“Muramenye ibi bintu byo kurwanira mu ngando, zirimo umwami bitaza gukora
ishyano! Nimushyire ku icumu, upfa apfe ukira akire!” maze Abanyarwanda
basubiranayo Abanyankore, babamerera nk’uruzi rwakutse rutakigize ikirukoma
imbere, rukarengera ibyo rusanze mu nzira byose. Imbunda z’Abanyankore zisumye
imbaga y’abantu irazicecekesha. Abanyankore bibutse guhunga birananirana.
Abanyarwanda babarindimurira mu kibande kitwa mu Ruhumba rwa Ruyojo,
babakukana hakurya nta mu nyankore ugishobora no guhindukira ngo arebe inyuma
ye. Uwakubeshya yavuga ko Abanyankore bashoboye kurokokamo abantu bageze
kuri mirongo itatu abagerageje kurwana akanya hafi y’ingando ni umurari umwe
wari waciye indi nzira akarasukira ku ngando y’Abashakamba n’iya Ndushabandi.
Abo ni bo bahunze bwa nyuma, barokoka icyo cyorezo, kuko bari bateye baturutse
inzira yabo ukwabo.

Abanyarwanda bazwi bahaguye ni Sebwayi bwa Nyamushanja wa Rwakagara na
Rujunga na Kabeba ka Rutihunza wo mu itorero ry’Abaganwa rya Nyaruguru na
Bihekenya w’umusyete wo mu Ngangurarugo. Abahakomerekeye ni Sahaha wa
Bukwege na Rurinda rwa Rushema na Nterarubango ya Mbungira na Nkeramiheto
ya Rushenyi na Rwabigwi rwa Bitinganyi. Abanyankore bo ntiwakwirirwa ubaza
abapfuye. Bahagiriye icyorezo kitigeze kiboneka. Ab’ingenzi bashobora kumenywa ni
abatasi. Uwambere ni Rumukwe wa Ntare. yari afite imbunda ya kizungu ihambiriyeho
idarubindi ikarasa kure cyane. Rwabugili yarayenze aba ari yo arashisha kugeza
amasasu yayo arangira. Iyo mbunda yabitswe ibwami iza guhira ku Rucunshu mu ipfa
rya Mibambwe IV Rutarindwa. Undi ni Rwarangira, yishwe na Rwangampuhwe rwa
Mutazibiza wo mu Barasa. Abandi Banyankore bahaguye bagashobora kumenywa
n’abatasi ni Rwishunda rwa Mwendo wishwe na Biganda bya Rwamuhunga, Bikangu
Ruregeya Rutasharara(Ruutagwikinya), Ngurube ya Mukabasinga, Ruberera Irabiro
rya Gahuta Rutarindimuka, ka Murase ka Bwishike, Rudakangarana, ka Mugundo ka
Bishike ihebuza makungu, Gaturu ka Kigaraga Rupfubya, Gatuka ka Rujuga Rujumera,
(mwibuke ko n’Abahima bitirirwa banyina n’andi benshi).

Indunduro y’igitero mu ruhande rw’Abanyarwanda
Bageze ku ruzi rwa Gashara basanga rwuzuye. Rwabugili yanga kurindira ko
rwuzuruka. Ategeka ko bamuheka bakamwambukiriza mu ngobyi. Abantu bose
bagira ubwoba, baranga, bati: “Kwiyambutsa ubwacu biraruhije none uragira ngo
twambutse ingobyi?” Ariko aranga baramuheka, abarebare bogana ingobyi ye iri mu
mazi, bakagera n’aho barengerwa. Si bo babonye bamugeza hakuno y’uruzi, abantu
bose bari bafite impungenge!
Bamaze kugandika, abatasi bamumenyesha ko umuzungu w’umwongereza ari hafi
aje gutabara Ntare. (Rwabugili ntiyari azi ko yavuye mu gihugu ke cyagenzurwaga
n’abadage, agatera mu cy’abongereza bo bari bamaze imyaka mu Buganda). Rwabugili
amaze kubyumva ati: “Mwumvise ko abazungu ari intwari cyane. Ntarwanye na bo
sinaba ndi intwari.” Abatware baramubuza: bibukaga urwo babonye i Kageyo igihe

230

cya bwana Voni Gotseni (Von Goetzen). Rwabugili ariko yanga kuva mu ngando.
Abatware barategeka barazitwika . Umwotsi umaze gukorana aremera arahaguruka,
barahumeka. Bataha kwa Ruromba bukeye ku Rufuha, hanyuma i Karwera, bukeye
Rukungwa na Nyamikamba, bukeye ku Muvumba, bukeye i Bugamba kwa Rwamwaga
n’umugaragu wa Rwatangabo, bukeye mu Rugarama rwa Gatsibo.

Mu gitondo Rwabugili agera i Gatsibo. Umugaba w’ingabo ariyereka, agororerwa
inka ebyiri. Barara bahigira Nyiramibambwe. Bukeye Rwabugili ajya ku Karubanda
ati: “Rubaanda rwange, narabashimye. Ndabasezeye nimutahe.” Asigara i Gatsibo na
Kabare na Ruhinankiko na Bisangwa, abandi baratabaruka. Ngibyo igitero k’igogo.

Indunduro y’igitero mu ruhande rw’Abanyankore
Wa muzungu w’umwongereza, bivugwa ko yaje akagera i Nyagakoni akahasanga
amahanga ya za ntumbi; ngo akaba yaravuze ati: “Abantu bashoboye kwica abangana
gutya bagomba kuba bafite intwaro zikomeye zitari iz’abirabura zisanzwe!” Ni uko
ngo aravunura yisubirirayo.
Ku birebana na cya gitekerezo twabanjije kuvuga Ehururu ya Rwanda, uretse gusoza
kivuga imitwe y’Abanyankore bashiriye i Nyagakoni cyongeraho kiti: “Abatware ba
Ntare barashize, imitwe ya Ntare irashira, abasigaye bakijijwe n’uko bwari bwije,
baragenda bagera aho Ntare yari ari, bati: “Uko utureba uku ni ko tuje ntugire
undi utegereza! Abatware bawe bashize, imitwe yawe yashize!” Ntare ahamagara
Kinyamakara, azana inzoga ati: “Abanyarwanda ndabashimiye cyane muri abagabo.
Watubujije kurwana na Rwabugili, turakunanira n’abatware bange; none uko
Rwabugili namubwiwe ni ko mubonye! Rwabugili yazanywe no kunyikisha irungu!”
Nuko Ntare asigarana inka zitagira abantu.
(Bafatiye ku bya Padiri Kagame Alexis)

Urugero rw’ubucurabwenge
1. Uyu mwami twimitse ni Mutara, izina rye ari umututsi ni Rudahigwa. Nyina ni
Nyiramavugo, izina rye ari umututsi ni Kankazi ka Mbanzabigwi ya Rwakagara,
rwa Gaga rya Mutezintare, wa Sesonga, ya Makara, ya Kiramiro, cya Mucuzi,
wa Nyantabana, wa Bugirande ,bwa Ngoga, ya Gihinira, cya Ndiga, ya Gahutu
,ka Serwega, rwa Mututsi, akaba umukobwa w’Abega. Nyina ni Nyiranteko ya
Nzagura ya Mbonyingabo akaba umukobwa w’Abashambo. Aho ga nyine Abega
bakabyarana abami n’Abanyiginya.
2. Mutara ni uwa Ruganzu, izina rye akiri umututsi akaba Ndori. Nyina ni
Nyiraruganzu, izina rye akiri umututsi akaba Nyabacuzi ba Kibogora: akaba
umukobwa w’ aAbakono. Nyina akaba Nyirarugwe wa Nkuba ya Bwimba
bwa Gitore cya KigeriMukobanyaumwami wa rubanda: akaba umukobwa
w’Abanyiginya.Aho ga nyine Abakono bakabyarana abami n’Abanyiginya.
(Bafatiye ku bya Padiri Kagame Alexis)

Inyunguramagambo nshya

B

Abatasi: abantu boherezwaga mu gihugu kitari icyabo biyoberanyije kugira
ngo bamenye amabanga y’ibijyanye n’ingabo ndetse n’intwaro bafite kugira ngo
nibakenera kubatera bazamenye uko babyitwaramo.
Abavuzi b’amacumu: abantu bajyanaga n’ingabo ku rugamba kugira ngo
nirurangira bazavuge uko igitero cyagenze. Abavuzi b’amacumu birinda kuvuga
abapfuye cyangwa abahunze.
Abo byagize imbata: Abo byigaruriye, abo byagize abagaragu, badashobora
kubyigobotora, kubireka.
Abo byakoronije: abo byahinduye abagaragu, abo byagize imbata.
Agakungu: ihuriro ryanduza ingeso mbi.
Akabande: igice cy’umusozi giteze buke kiri hagati y’imisozi ibiri.
Akanozangendo: inzoga baha abashyitsi babasezeraho, babifuriza kugera
imuhira amahoro.
Akanyabugabo: agashema umuntu yigiramo kakamutera agasumbwe.
Akanyamuneza: umunezero, ibyishimo bihera imbere umuntu yiyumvamo.
Akenura inka: yita ku nka, akorera inka ibyo zikenera kugira ngo zibeho neza
birimo.
Amadeni: imyenda.
Amafiri: ibyishimo bidafite aho bishingiye.
Amagambo azaba ashize ivuga: byose bizaba birangiye.
Amagi y’umuzungu: indundo y’amagi abaturage bagemuriraga ababirigi baraye
ku gasozi runaka.
Amajune: ibyago, ibibazo, akaga.
Amapfa: icana cyane ry’izuba ibimera bikuma, amazi agakama mu bishanga mu
migezi n’inzuzi.
Amaso si aya: nta bwo ari ubwa mbere nkubonye.
Amazi atararenga inkombe: igihe kikiriho, ibintu bitarabakomerana ; bitaracika,
bigifite igaruriro, bigishoka.
Arabacika: arapfa, yitaba Imana, avamo umwuka, aratabaruka.
Babaye imbata: babaye abagaragu.
Babaswe n’urumogi: urumogi rwarabigaruriye ntibashobora kurureka.
Bamwemerera batazuyaje: bamwemerera badashidikanyije, batagononwa.

-

-

Barasuhuka: bimukira, bava aho bari batuye bajya ahandi hantu.
Bareteta: ubwoko bw’inzoga y’inkorano
Basaranganya: basangira uduke abantu bafite, basangira ibyo kurya bike bari
bafite.
Batadukoma mu nkokora: batatubangamira, bataduca intege.
Bihungabanya (ubuzima): bibugirira nabi.
Bimukora ku mutima: yumva arabikunze cyane.
Bimwanga mu nda: bituma adatuza, gutuma umuntu yivuguruza ku mugambi
yari yafashe agafata indi.
Binikiza inka: batangira gukama inka.
Bugarijwe n’ibiyobyabwenge: bamerewe nabi kubera ibiyobyabwenge, kubera
ibyo bafata bigatuma bitwara nk’abataye ubwenge, nk’abatari bazima.

C, D, E, F
- Canamake: imbabura cyangwa iziko bitamara inkwi.

G, H
- Guca inshuro: gukora umurimo uri buhemberwe ibiribwa, gukorera icyo uri
burye.
- Gucocera: kwambara ibirere cyane cyane igihe umuntu agiye gukora ikintu
cyamwanduriza imyambaro, kwambara imyambaro yashaje cyane.
- Gucurwa inkumbi n’indwara: kwicwa n’indwara
- Gufata ingamba: gushaka uburyo bwo kurwanya ikintu runaka
- Guha urwaho: gutuma ikintu bigerwaho, gutanga umwanya.
- Guhabwa akato: kutagira umuntu ukwegera ukaba wenyine ku bw’igihano
cyangwa ari nk’indwara yandura.
- Gucibwa ihazabu: guhabwa igihano cyo gutanga amafaranga ku cyaha wakoze.
- Guca ukubiri n’ikintu: kutagira aho wongera guhurira na cyo.
- Guhagurukira umuntu: kumufatira ibyemezo bikarishye, bikaze.
- Guhana umugambi: kumvikana n’umuntu ku kintu mushaka gukora, ku buryo
cyangwa ku gihe kizakorwa.
- Guhara amagara: kwiyemeza gukora ikintu ukaba wanakizira.
- Guhashya mu maguru mashya: kurwanya kandi ugaca intege ikintu hakiri kare.
- Guhomba: gucuruza ukabura inyungu n’igice cy’ayo washoye cyangwa yose.
- Guhohotera abandi: kubagirira urugomo, kubagirira nabi.
- Guhora muri bombori bombori: guhora mu mahane.
- Gukomeza umutsi: kwihangana.
- Gukumira: kubuza gukora igikorwa runaka.
- Gukura se: gusimbura se.
- Gushingana akavumu: kubakana urugo, gushyingiranwa.
- Gushora imari: Gushora imari ni ugushyira amafaranga menshi ahantu cyangwa
mu bintu ugamije kunguka.
- Gusonga: guhwanya ikenda gupfa, kongerera umuntu ibyago cyangwa ububabare
yari yisanganiwe n’ibindi.

233

-

Guta agaciro: gusubira inyuma mu kamaro cyangwa mu gushima.
Guterura ikiganiro: gutangira kuganiriza abantu, gutangira ikiganiro.
Gutesha agaciro umuntu: kumwambura ubumuntu, gusubiza umuntu inyuma
mu kamaro cyangwa mu gushima.
Gutora agatege: gutangira kugira akabaraga/imbaraga.

I, J
- Ibiribwa bihumanye: ibiribwa byanduye bidafite ubuziranenge
- Ibizazane: ibibazo bitandukanye, ingorane zizitira umuntu kugira icyo ageraho.
- Ibyambu: ahantu abantu baca bambuka umugezi, ikiyaga cyangwa igishanga,
kwaba kugenda ku maguru mu bwato cyangwa banyuze ku kiraro.
- Icyanzu: ubusanzwe icyanzu ni agasesero ko mu rugo kazwi na bene urugo,
katari irembo rizwi na bose, ribonwa na bose.
- Igikatsi: ishinge bengesheje ibitoki maze bakabikamuramo umutobe wose.
Bivugwa kandi ku kintu cyose cyashizemo amazi.
- Igikingi cy’umuntu: umurima munini umuntu ahinga akawubyaza umusaruro.
- Igikingi cya sogokuru: igikingi ni umusozi cyangwa igice cy’umusozi gihabwa
umutunzi ngo akigire urwuri rw’inka ze.
- Igishoro: ibintu cyangwa imari umuntu ashyira mu bucuruzi kugira ngo
bizunguke.
- Impagarara: ibintu by’ibizira byaduka ku muntu cyangwa mu bantu benshi
bigatuma babura amahoro.
- Impagarara: ibitekerezo bituma wumva udatuje mu mutima.
- Impiru: agati kabaje nk’agahiri barashisha nk’umwambi.
- Indangagaciro: umuco umuntu cyangwa abantu baba bafite utuma bashobora
kwitwara neza mu bandi.
- Inda z’indaro: inda abakobwa batwita nta bagabo bazwi bafite cyangwa bari
bagira.
- Indiri: aho ikintu kiba (kirara)
- Ingaruka: impinduka mbi, inkurikizi.
- Ingorane: ibibazo.
- Inka z’inyarurembo: inka zahukaga zigataha mu rugo rw’umwami cyangwa
rw’umutware. Kera ubundi inka ziberaga mu nzuri. Izatahaga mu rugo rutari
urw’umutware zitwaga Inka z’intahira.
- Inka z’umugogoro: ni inka zakamirwaga umuzungu waraye ku gasozi kariho
abatunzi, bashoboraga gukama zahumuza bakazicyura, bitashoboka bakiyemeza
kujya bagemura.
- Inka zihumuje: inka zimaze gukamwa.
- Inkiko: imipaka y’igihugu.
- Inkingi yitwa kanagazi: inkingi yo mu nzu ya Kinyarwanda yashyigikiraga
uruhamo rw’umuryango.
- Inkingi ya mwamba: inkingi y’inzu bateraho ingiga y’igiti bashoreraho
amaburiti. Mu mwandiko ni ukuvuga ikintu k’ingirakamaro cyane, ibintu byinshi
bifite akamaro bishamikiyeho.
